####################################################################################################
package domain

import (
	"errors"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

var (
	ErrInvalidCategoryID       = errors.New("invalid category id")
	ErrInvalidCategoryCode     = errors.New("invalid category code")
	ErrInvalidCategoryName     = errors.New("invalid category name")
	ErrInvalidCategoryKind     = errors.New("invalid category kind")
	ErrInvalidCategoryStatus   = errors.New("invalid category status")
	ErrInvalidParentCategory   = errors.New("invalid parent category")
	ErrCategoryArchived        = errors.New("category is archived")
	ErrInvalidCategoryMutation = errors.New("invalid category mutation")
)

type CategoryID string

type CategoryKind string

type CategoryStatus string

const (
	CategoryKindGeneral     CategoryKind = "general"
	CategoryKindSpecialized CategoryKind = "specialized"
)

const (
	CategoryStatusDraft    CategoryStatus = "draft"
	CategoryStatusActive   CategoryStatus = "active"
	CategoryStatusArchived CategoryStatus = "archived"
)

func (k CategoryKind) IsValid() bool {
	return k == CategoryKindGeneral || k == CategoryKindSpecialized
}

func (s CategoryStatus) IsValid() bool {
	return s == CategoryStatusDraft ||
		s == CategoryStatusActive ||
		s == CategoryStatusArchived
}

type CreateCategoryInput struct {
	ID        CategoryID
	Code      string
	Name      string
	Kind      CategoryKind
	ParentID  CategoryID
	SortOrder int
}

type Category struct {
	id        CategoryID
	code      string
	name      string
	kind      CategoryKind
	parentID  CategoryID
	sortOrder int

	status    CategoryStatus
	version   uint64
	createdAt time.Time
	updatedAt time.Time
}

func NewCategory(input CreateCategoryInput, now time.Time) (*Category, error) {
	if strings.TrimSpace(string(input.ID)) == "" {
		return nil, ErrInvalidCategoryID
	}

	code := normalizeCategoryCode(input.Code)
	if !isValidCategoryCode(code) {
		return nil, ErrInvalidCategoryCode
	}

	name := strings.TrimSpace(input.Name)
	if name == "" || len([]rune(name)) > 160 {
		return nil, ErrInvalidCategoryName
	}

	if !input.Kind.IsValid() {
		return nil, ErrInvalidCategoryKind
	}

	if input.ParentID != "" && input.ParentID == input.ID {
		return nil, ErrInvalidParentCategory
	}

	if now.IsZero() {
		return nil, ErrInvalidTimestamp
	}

	return &Category{
		id:        input.ID,
		code:      code,
		name:      name,
		kind:      input.Kind,
		parentID:  input.ParentID,
		sortOrder: input.SortOrder,
		status:    CategoryStatusDraft,
		version:   1,
		createdAt: now.UTC(),
		updatedAt: now.UTC(),
	}, nil
}

func normalizeCategoryCode(value string) string {
	return strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(value))
}

func isValidCategoryCode(value string) bool {
	if len(value) < 2 || len(value) > 64 {
		return false
	}

	for _, r := range value {
		valid := (r >= 'A' && r <= 'Z') ||
			(r >= '0' && r <= '9') ||
			r == '-' ||
			r == '_' ||
			r == '.'

		if !valid {
			return false
		}
	}

	return true
}

func (c *Category) ID() CategoryID {
	return c.id
}

func (c *Category) Code() string {
	return c.code
}

func (c *Category) Name() string {
	return c.name
}

func (c *Category) Kind() CategoryKind {
	return c.kind
}

func (c *Category) ParentID() CategoryID {
	return c.parentID
}

func (c *Category) SortOrder() int {
	return c.sortOrder
}

func (c *Category) Status() CategoryStatus {
	return c.status
}

func (c *Category) Version() uint64 {
	return c.version
}

func (c *Category) CreatedAt() time.Time {
	return c.createdAt
}

func (c *Category) UpdatedAt() time.Time {
	return c.updatedAt
}

func (c *Category) Rename(name string, now time.Time) error {
	if c.status == CategoryStatusArchived {
		return ErrCategoryArchived
	}

	name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
	if name == "" || len([]rune(name)) > 160 {
		return ErrInvalidCategoryName
	}

	if err := c.validateTimestamp(now); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if name == c.name {
		return nil
	}

	c.name = name
	c.touch(now)

	return nil
}

func (c *Category) ChangeParent(parentID CategoryID, now time.Time) error {
	if c.status == CategoryStatusArchived {
		return ErrCategoryArchived
	}

	if parentID != "" && parentID == c.id {
		return ErrInvalidParentCategory
	}

	if err := c.validateTimestamp(now); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if parentID == c.parentID {
		return nil
	}

	c.parentID = parentID
	c.touch(now)

	return nil
}

func (c *Category) ChangeSortOrder(sortOrder int, now time.Time) error {
	if c.status == CategoryStatusArchived {
		return ErrCategoryArchived
	}

	if err := c.validateTimestamp(now); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if sortOrder == c.sortOrder {
		return nil
	}

	c.sortOrder = sortOrder
	c.touch(now)

	return nil
}

func (c *Category) Activate(now time.Time) error {
	switch c.status {
	case CategoryStatusDraft:
		if err := c.validateTimestamp(now); err != nil {
			return err
		}

		c.status = CategoryStatusActive
		c.touch(now)
		return nil

	case CategoryStatusActive:
		return ErrInvalidCategoryMutation

	case CategoryStatusArchived:
		return ErrCategoryArchived

	default:
		return ErrInvalidCategoryStatus
	}
}

func (c *Category) Archive(now time.Time) error {
	if c.status == CategoryStatusArchived {
		return ErrInvalidCategoryMutation
	}

	if err := c.validateTimestamp(now); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	c.status = CategoryStatusArchived
	c.touch(now)

	return nil
}

func (c *Category) IsSelectable() bool {
	return c.status == CategoryStatusActive
}

func (c *Category) validateTimestamp(now time.Time) error {
	if now.IsZero() || now.Before(c.updatedAt) {
		return ErrInvalidTimestamp
	}

	return nil
}

func (c *Category) touch(now time.Time) {
	c.updatedAt = now.UTC()
	c.version++
}


####################################################################################################
FILE: internal\catalogue\domain\category_mapping.go
SIZE: 4346 bytes
LAST MODIFIED: 11/04/2026 08:05:51
####################################################################################################
package domain

import (
	"errors"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

var (
	ErrInvalidCategoryMappingID = errors.New("invalid category mapping id")
	ErrInvalidCategoryMapping   = errors.New("invalid category mapping")
	ErrCategoryMappingArchived  = errors.New("category mapping is archived")
	ErrCategoryMappingState     = errors.New("invalid category mapping state")
	ErrCategoryKindsMismatch    = errors.New("category kinds do not match mapping direction")
	ErrCategoryNotActive        = errors.New("both categories must be active")
)

type CategoryMappingID string

type CategoryMappingStatus string

const (
	CategoryMappingStatusDraft    CategoryMappingStatus = "draft"
	CategoryMappingStatusActive   CategoryMappingStatus = "active"
	CategoryMappingStatusArchived CategoryMappingStatus = "archived"
)

type CreateCategoryMappingInput struct {
	ID                    CategoryMappingID
	PublicCategoryID      CategoryID
	SpecializedCategoryID CategoryID
	MarketID              string
	Description           string
}

type CategoryMapping struct {
	id                    CategoryMappingID
	publicCategoryID      CategoryID
	specializedCategoryID CategoryID
	marketID              string
	description           string

	status    CategoryMappingStatus
	version   uint64
	createdAt time.Time
	updatedAt time.Time
}

func NewCategoryMapping(
	input CreateCategoryMappingInput,
	now time.Time,
) (*CategoryMapping, error) {
	if strings.TrimSpace(string(input.ID)) == "" {
		return nil, ErrInvalidCategoryMappingID
	}

	if strings.TrimSpace(string(input.PublicCategoryID)) == "" ||
		strings.TrimSpace(string(input.SpecializedCategoryID)) == "" ||
		input.PublicCategoryID == input.SpecializedCategoryID {
		return nil, ErrInvalidCategoryMapping
	}

	if now.IsZero() {
		return nil, ErrInvalidTimestamp
	}

	return &CategoryMapping{
		id:                    input.ID,
		publicCategoryID:      input.PublicCategoryID,
		specializedCategoryID: input.SpecializedCategoryID,
		marketID:              strings.TrimSpace(input.MarketID),
		description:           strings.TrimSpace(input.Description),
		status:                CategoryMappingStatusDraft,
		version:               1,
		createdAt:             now.UTC(),
		updatedAt:             now.UTC(),
	}, nil
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) ID() CategoryMappingID {
	return m.id
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) PublicCategoryID() CategoryID {
	return m.publicCategoryID
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) SpecializedCategoryID() CategoryID {
	return m.specializedCategoryID
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) MarketID() string {
	return m.marketID
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) Description() string {
	return m.description
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) Status() CategoryMappingStatus {
	return m.status
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) Version() uint64 {
	return m.version
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) CreatedAt() time.Time {
	return m.createdAt
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) UpdatedAt() time.Time {
	return m.updatedAt
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) Activate(
	publicCategory *Category,
	specializedCategory *Category,
	now time.Time,
) error {
	if m.status == CategoryMappingStatusArchived {
		return ErrCategoryMappingArchived
	}

	if m.status != CategoryMappingStatusDraft {
		return ErrCategoryMappingState
	}

	if publicCategory == nil || specializedCategory == nil {
		return ErrInvalidCategoryMapping
	}

	if publicCategory.ID() != m.publicCategoryID ||
		specializedCategory.ID() != m.specializedCategoryID {
		return ErrInvalidCategoryMapping
	}

	if publicCategory.Kind() != CategoryKindGeneral ||
		specializedCategory.Kind() != CategoryKindSpecialized {
		return ErrCategoryKindsMismatch
	}

	if !publicCategory.IsSelectable() || !specializedCategory.IsSelectable() {
		return ErrCategoryNotActive
	}

	if now.IsZero() || now.Before(m.updatedAt) {
		return ErrInvalidTimestamp
	}

	m.status = CategoryMappingStatusActive
	m.touch(now)

	return nil
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) Archive(now time.Time) error {
	if m.status == CategoryMappingStatusArchived {
		return ErrCategoryMappingState
	}

	if now.IsZero() || now.Before(m.updatedAt) {
		return ErrInvalidTimestamp
	}

	m.status = CategoryMappingStatusArchived
	m.touch(now)

	return nil
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) IsUsable() bool {
	return m.status == CategoryMappingStatusActive
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) touch(now time.Time) {
	m.updatedAt = now.UTC()
	m.version++
}

package domain

import (
	"errors"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

func createMappingTestCategories(t *testing.T) (*Category, *Category, time.Time) {
	t.Helper()

	now := time.Date(2026, 11, 4, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)

	publicCategory, err := NewCategory(CreateCategoryInput{
		ID:   "public-1",
		Code: "PUBLIC",
		Name: "Public Category",
		Kind: CategoryKindGeneral,
	}, now)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("create public category: %v", err)
	}

	specializedCategory, err := NewCategory(CreateCategoryInput{
		ID:   "special-1",
		Code: "SPECIAL",
		Name: "Specialized Category",
		Kind: CategoryKindSpecialized,
	}, now)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("create specialized category: %v", err)
	}

	if err := publicCategory.Activate(now.Add(time.Minute)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("activate public category: %v", err)
	}

	if err := specializedCategory.Activate(now.Add(time.Minute)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("activate specialized category: %v", err)
	}

	return publicCategory, specializedCategory, now
}

func TestNewCategoryMapping(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Now().UTC()

	mapping, err := NewCategoryMapping(CreateCategoryMappingInput{
		ID:                    "mapping-1",
		PublicCategoryID:      "public-1",
		SpecializedCategoryID: "special-1",
		MarketID:              "market-1",
		Description:           "Public to specialized mapping",
	}, now)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("NewCategoryMapping() error = %v", err)
	}

	if mapping.Status() != CategoryMappingStatusDraft {
		t.Fatalf("expected draft status, got %s", mapping.Status())
	}

	if mapping.Version() != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("expected version 1, got %d", mapping.Version())
	}
}

func TestNewCategoryMappingRejectsSameCategory(t *testing.T) {
	_, err := NewCategoryMapping(CreateCategoryMappingInput{
		ID:                    "mapping-1",
		PublicCategoryID:      "same",
		SpecializedCategoryID: "same",
	}, time.Now().UTC())

	if !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidCategoryMapping) {
		t.Fatalf("expected ErrInvalidCategoryMapping, got %v", err)
	}
}

func TestCategoryMappingActivation(t *testing.T) {
	publicCategory, specializedCategory, now := createMappingTestCategories(t)

	mapping, err := NewCategoryMapping(CreateCategoryMappingInput{
		ID:                    "mapping-1",
		PublicCategoryID:      publicCategory.ID(),
		SpecializedCategoryID: specializedCategory.ID(),
		MarketID:              "market-1",
	}, now.Add(2*time.Minute))

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("NewCategoryMapping() error = %v", err)
	}

	activationTime := now.Add(3 * time.Minute)

	if err := mapping.Activate(publicCategory, specializedCategory, activationTime); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Activate() error = %v", err)
	}

	if !mapping.IsUsable() {
		t.Fatal("mapping should be usable")
	}

	if mapping.Status() != CategoryMappingStatusActive {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected status: %s", mapping.Status())
	}

	if mapping.Version() != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("expected version 2, got %d", mapping.Version())
	}
}

func TestCategoryMappingRejectsWrongKinds(t *testing.T) {
	publicCategory, specializedCategory, now := createMappingTestCategories(t)

	mapping, err := NewCategoryMapping(CreateCategoryMappingInput{
		ID:                    "mapping-1",
		PublicCategoryID:      publicCategory.ID(),
		SpecializedCategoryID: specializedCategory.ID(),
	}, now.Add(2*time.Minute))

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("NewCategoryMapping() error = %v", err)
	}

	err = mapping.Activate(specializedCategory, publicCategory, now.Add(3*time.Minute))

	if !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidCategoryMapping) {
		// The IDs are deliberately reversed, so the mapping identity check
		// must reject the operation before category-kind validation.
		t.Fatalf("expected ErrInvalidCategoryMapping, got %v", err)
	}
}

func TestCategoryMappingRequiresActiveCategories(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Now().UTC()

	publicCategory, err := NewCategory(CreateCategoryInput{
		ID:   "public-1",
		Code: "PUBLIC",
		Name: "Public",
		Kind: CategoryKindGeneral,
	}, now)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	specializedCategory, err := NewCategory(CreateCategoryInput{
		ID:   "special-1",
		Code: "SPECIAL",
		Name: "Specialized",
		Kind: CategoryKindSpecialized,
	}, now)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	mapping, err := NewCategoryMapping(CreateCategoryMappingInput{
		ID:                    "mapping-1",
		PublicCategoryID:      publicCategory.ID(),
		SpecializedCategoryID: specializedCategory.ID(),
	}, now.Add(time.Minute))

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	err = mapping.Activate(publicCategory, specializedCategory, now.Add(2*time.Minute))

	if !errors.Is(err, ErrCategoryNotActive) {
		t.Fatalf("expected ErrCategoryNotActive, got %v", err)
	}
}

func TestCategoryMappingArchive(t *testing.T) {
	publicCategory, specializedCategory, now := createMappingTestCategories(t)

	mapping, err := NewCategoryMapping(CreateCategoryMappingInput{
		ID:                    "mapping-1",
		PublicCategoryID:      publicCategory.ID(),
		SpecializedCategoryID: specializedCategory.ID(),
	}, now.Add(2*time.Minute))

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	if err := mapping.Activate(publicCategory, specializedCategory, now.Add(3*time.Minute)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	if err := mapping.Archive(now.Add(4 * time.Minute)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Archive() error = %v", err)
	}

	if mapping.IsUsable() {
		t.Fatal("archived mapping must not be usable")
	}

	if err := mapping.Activate(publicCategory, specializedCategory, now.Add(5*time.Minute)); !errors.Is(err, ErrCategoryMappingArchived) {
		t.Fatalf("expected ErrCategoryMappingArchived, got %v", err)
	}
}

package domain

import (
	"errors"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

func newTestCategory(
	t *testing.T,
	id CategoryID,
	code string,
	kind CategoryKind,
) *Category {
	t.Helper()

	now := time.Date(2026, 11, 4, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)

	category, err := NewCategory(CreateCategoryInput{
		ID:        id,
		Code:      code,
		Name:      "Test Category",
		Kind:      kind,
		SortOrder: 10,
	}, now)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("NewCategory() error = %v", err)
	}

	return category
}

func TestNewCategory(t *testing.T) {
	category := newTestCategory(
		t,
		"category-001",
		"general.goods",
		CategoryKindGeneral,
	)

	if category.Status() != CategoryStatusDraft {
		t.Fatalf("expected draft, got %s", category.Status())
	}

	if category.Code() != "GENERAL.GOODS" {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected code: %s", category.Code())
	}

	if category.Version() != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("expected version 1, got %d", category.Version())
	}
}

func TestNewCategoryRejectsInvalidInput(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Now().UTC()

	tests := []struct {
		name  string
		input CreateCategoryInput
		want  error
	}{
		{
			name: "missing id",
			input: CreateCategoryInput{
				Code: "CAT",
				Name: "Category",
				Kind: CategoryKindGeneral,
			},
			want: ErrInvalidCategoryID,
		},
		{
			name: "invalid code",
			input: CreateCategoryInput{
				ID:   "cat-1",
				Code: "bad code",
				Name: "Category",
				Kind: CategoryKindGeneral,
			},
			want: ErrInvalidCategoryCode,
		},
		{
			name: "invalid kind",
			input: CreateCategoryInput{
				ID:   "cat-1",
				Code: "CAT",
				Name: "Category",
				Kind: "unknown",
			},
			want: ErrInvalidCategoryKind,
		},
		{
			name: "self parent",
			input: CreateCategoryInput{
				ID:       "cat-1",
				Code:     "CAT",
				Name:     "Category",
				Kind:     CategoryKindGeneral,
				ParentID: "cat-1",
			},
			want: ErrInvalidParentCategory,
		},
	}

	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			_, err := NewCategory(tt.input, now)

			if !errors.Is(err, tt.want) {
				t.Fatalf("expected %v, got %v", tt.want, err)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestCategoryLifecycle(t *testing.T) {
	category := newTestCategory(
		t,
		"cat-1",
		"CAT",
		CategoryKindGeneral,
	)

	base := category.UpdatedAt()

	if err := category.Activate(base.Add(time.Minute)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Activate() error = %v", err)
	}

	if !category.IsSelectable() {
		t.Fatal("active category should be selectable")
	}

	if err := category.Archive(base.Add(2 * time.Minute)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Archive() error = %v", err)
	}

	if category.IsSelectable() {
		t.Fatal("archived category must not be selectable")
	}

	if err := category.Activate(base.Add(3 * time.Minute)); !errors.Is(err, ErrCategoryArchived) {
		t.Fatalf("expected ErrCategoryArchived, got %v", err)
	}
}

func TestCategoryMutation(t *testing.T) {
	category := newTestCategory(
		t,
		"cat-1",
		"CAT",
		CategoryKindGeneral,
	)

	base := category.UpdatedAt()

	if err := category.Rename("New Name", base.Add(time.Minute)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Rename() error = %v", err)
	}

	if category.Name() != "New Name" {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected name: %s", category.Name())
	}

	if err := category.ChangeParent("parent-1", base.Add(2*time.Minute)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("ChangeParent() error = %v", err)
	}

	if category.ParentID() != "parent-1" {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected parent: %s", category.ParentID())
	}

	if err := category.ChangeSortOrder(20, base.Add(3*time.Minute)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("ChangeSortOrder() error = %v", err)
	}

	if category.SortOrder() != 20 {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected sort order: %d", category.SortOrder())
	}

	if category.Version() != 4 {
		t.Fatalf("expected version 4, got %d", category.Version())
	}
}

func TestCategoryRejectsSelfParent(t *testing.T) {
	category := newTestCategory(
		t,
		"cat-1",
		"CAT",
		CategoryKindGeneral,
	)

	err := category.ChangeParent("cat-1", category.UpdatedAt().Add(time.Minute))

	if !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidParentCategory) {
		t.Fatalf("expected ErrInvalidParentCategory, got %v", err)
	}
}

func TestCategoryRejectsMutationAfterArchive(t *testing.T) {
	category := newTestCategory(
		t,
		"cat-1",
		"CAT",
		CategoryKindGeneral,
	)

	base := category.UpdatedAt()

	if err := category.Archive(base.Add(time.Minute)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Archive() error = %v", err)
	}

	if err := category.Rename("Forbidden", base.Add(2*time.Minute)); !errors.Is(err, ErrCategoryArchived) {
		t.Fatalf("expected ErrCategoryArchived, got %v", err)
	}
}

####################################################################################################
FILE: internal\catalogue\handler.go
SIZE: 9233 bytes
LAST MODIFIED: 10/27/2026 11:01:46
####################################################################################################
package catalogue

import (
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"net/http"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

const (
	defaultPageSize = 20
	maxPageSize     = 100
	maxSearchLength = 120
)

type Handler struct {
	DB *sql.DB
}

type OfferSummary struct {
	ID                 string          `json:"id"`
	Code               string          `json:"code"`
	MarketID           string          `json:"market_id"`
	MarketName         string          `json:"market_name"`
	PublicCategoryID   string          `json:"public_category_id,omitempty"`
	PublicCategoryName string          `json:"public_category_name,omitempty"`
	ClassificationID   string          `json:"classification_id"`
	Title              string          `json:"title"`
	Summary            string          `json:"summary,omitempty"`
	OfferType          string          `json:"offer_type"`
	PriceAmount        string          `json:"price_amount,omitempty"`
	CurrencyCode       string          `json:"currency_code,omitempty"`
	QuantityTotal      string          `json:"quantity_total,omitempty"`
	QuantityUnit       string          `json:"quantity_unit,omitempty"`
	Attributes         json.RawMessage `json:"attributes"`
	PublishedAt        time.Time       `json:"published_at"`
	ValidUntil         *time.Time      `json:"valid_until,omitempty"`
}

type ListResponse struct {
	Data       []OfferSummary `json:"data"`
	Page       int            `json:"page"`
	PageSize   int            `json:"page_size"`
	Total      int64          `json:"total"`
	TotalPages int64          `json:"total_pages"`
}

type APIError struct {
	Code    string `json:"code"`
	Message string `json:"message"`
}

func (h Handler) Register(mux *http.ServeMux) {
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /api/v1/catalogue/offers", h.ListOffers)
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /api/v1/catalogue/offers/{id}", h.GetOffer)
}

func (h Handler) ListOffers(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	page, err := parsePositiveInt(r.URL.Query().Get("page"), 1, 1000000)
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid_page", "page must be a positive integer.")
		return
	}

	pageSize, err := parsePositiveInt(r.URL.Query().Get("page_size"), defaultPageSize, maxPageSize)
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid_page_size", "page_size must be between 1 and 100.")
		return
	}

	search := strings.TrimSpace(r.URL.Query().Get("q"))
	if len(search) > maxSearchLength {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "search_too_long", "q must not exceed 120 characters.")
		return
	}

	categoryID := strings.TrimSpace(r.URL.Query().Get("category_id"))
	marketID := strings.TrimSpace(r.URL.Query().Get("market_id"))

	if h.DB == nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, "database_unavailable", "Catalogue storage is unavailable.")
		return
	}

	const filter = `
		FROM offers o
		INNER JOIN markets m ON m.id = o.market_id
		LEFT JOIN public_categories pc ON pc.id = o.public_category_id
		WHERE o.status = 'published'
		  AND m.status = 'active'
		  AND (o.valid_from IS NULL OR o.valid_from <= now())
		  AND (o.valid_until IS NULL OR o.valid_until > now())
		  AND ($1 = '' OR o.title ILIKE '%' || $1 || '%' OR COALESCE(o.summary, '') ILIKE '%' || $1 || '%')
		  AND ($2 = '' OR o.public_category_id::text = $2)
		  AND ($3 = '' OR o.market_id::text = $3)
	`

	var total int64
	countQuery := "SELECT count(*) " + filter
	if err := h.DB.QueryRowContext(r.Context(), countQuery, search, categoryID, marketID).Scan(&total); err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "catalogue_query_failed", "The catalogue could not be loaded.")
		return
	}

	offset := (page - 1) * pageSize
	const listSelect = `
		SELECT
			o.id::text,
			o.public_code,
			o.market_id::text,
			m.name,
			COALESCE(o.public_category_id::text, ''),
			COALESCE(pc.name, ''),
			o.classification_id::text,
			o.title,
			COALESCE(o.summary, ''),
			o.offer_type,
			COALESCE(o.price_amount::text, ''),
			COALESCE(o.currency_code, ''),
			COALESCE(o.quantity_total::text, ''),
			COALESCE(o.quantity_unit, ''),
			o.attributes,
			o.published_at,
			o.valid_until
	`
	rows, err := h.DB.QueryContext(
		r.Context(),
		listSelect+filter+`
			ORDER BY o.published_at DESC, o.id DESC
			LIMIT $4 OFFSET $5
		`,
		search, categoryID, marketID, pageSize, offset,
	)
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "catalogue_query_failed", "The catalogue could not be loaded.")
		return
	}
	defer rows.Close()

	data := make([]OfferSummary, 0, pageSize)
	for rows.Next() {
		var item OfferSummary
		var categoryIDValue string
		var categoryName string
		var validUntil sql.NullTime
		var attributes []byte

		if err := rows.Scan(
			&item.ID,
			&item.Code,
			&item.MarketID,
			&item.MarketName,
			&categoryIDValue,
			&categoryName,
			&item.ClassificationID,
			&item.Title,
			&item.Summary,
			&item.OfferType,
			&item.PriceAmount,
			&item.CurrencyCode,
			&item.QuantityTotal,
			&item.QuantityUnit,
			&attributes,
			&item.PublishedAt,
			&validUntil,
		); err != nil {
			writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "catalogue_scan_failed", "A catalogue record could not be read.")
			return
		}

		item.PublicCategoryID = categoryIDValue
		item.PublicCategoryName = categoryName
		if validUntil.Valid {
			value := validUntil.Time
			item.ValidUntil = &value
		}
		if len(attributes) == 0 {
			item.Attributes = json.RawMessage(`{}`)
		} else if json.Valid(attributes) {
			item.Attributes = json.RawMessage(append([]byte(nil), attributes...))
		} else {
			writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "invalid_catalogue_data", "A catalogue record contains invalid attributes.")
			return
		}
		data = append(data, item)
	}
	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "catalogue_read_failed", "The catalogue could not be read completely.")
		return
	}

	totalPages := int64(0)
	if total > 0 {
		totalPages = (total + int64(pageSize) - 1) / int64(pageSize)
	}

	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, ListResponse{
		Data:       data,
		Page:       page,
		PageSize:   pageSize,
		Total:      total,
		TotalPages: totalPages,
	})
}

func (h Handler) GetOffer(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if h.DB == nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, "database_unavailable", "Catalogue storage is unavailable.")
		return
	}

	id := strings.TrimSpace(r.PathValue("id"))
	if id == "" || len(id) > 64 {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid_offer_id", "A valid offer ID is required.")
		return
	}

	const query = `
		SELECT
			o.id::text,
			o.public_code,
			o.market_id::text,
			m.name,
			COALESCE(o.public_category_id::text, ''),
			COALESCE(pc.name, ''),
			o.classification_id::text,
			o.title,
			COALESCE(o.summary, ''),
			o.offer_type,
			COALESCE(o.price_amount::text, ''),
			COALESCE(o.currency_code, ''),
			COALESCE(o.quantity_total::text, ''),
			COALESCE(o.quantity_unit, ''),
			o.attributes,
			o.published_at,
			o.valid_until
		FROM offers o
		INNER JOIN markets m ON m.id = o.market_id
		LEFT JOIN public_categories pc ON pc.id = o.public_category_id
		WHERE o.id::text = $1
		  AND o.status = 'published'
		  AND m.status = 'active'
		  AND (o.valid_from IS NULL OR o.valid_from <= now())
		  AND (o.valid_until IS NULL OR o.valid_until > now())
	`

	var item OfferSummary
	var categoryIDValue string
	var categoryName string
	var validUntil sql.NullTime
	var attributes []byte

	err := h.DB.QueryRowContext(r.Context(), query, id).Scan(
		&item.ID,
		&item.Code,
		&item.MarketID,
		&item.MarketName,
		&categoryIDValue,
		&categoryName,
		&item.ClassificationID,
		&item.Title,
		&item.Summary,
		&item.OfferType,
		&item.PriceAmount,
		&item.CurrencyCode,
		&item.QuantityTotal,
		&item.QuantityUnit,
		&attributes,
		&item.PublishedAt,
		&validUntil,
	)

	if err != nil {
		if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
			writeError(w, http.StatusNotFound, "offer_not_found", "The published offer was not found.")
			return
		}
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "catalogue_query_failed", "The offer could not be loaded.")
		return
	}

	item.PublicCategoryID = categoryIDValue
	item.PublicCategoryName = categoryName
	if validUntil.Valid {
		value := validUntil.Time
		item.ValidUntil = &value
	}
	if len(attributes) == 0 {
		item.Attributes = json.RawMessage(`{}`)
	} else if json.Valid(attributes) {
		item.Attributes = json.RawMessage(append([]byte(nil), attributes...))
	} else {
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "invalid_catalogue_data", "The offer contains invalid attributes.")
		return
	}

	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, item)
}

func parsePositiveInt(raw string, fallback, maximum int) (int, error) {
	if raw == "" {
		return fallback, nil
	}
	value, err := strconv.Atoi(raw)
	if err != nil || value < 1 || value > maximum {
		return 0, errors.New("out of range")
	}
	return value, nil
}

func writeError(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, code, message string) {
	writeJSON(w, status, APIError{Code: code, Message: message})
}

func writeJSON(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, value any) {
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
	w.Header().Set("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
	w.WriteHeader(status)
	_ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(value)
}

package database

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	_ "github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/stdlib"
)

type Config struct {
	URL             string
	MaxOpenConns    int
	MaxIdleConns    int
	ConnMaxLifetime time.Duration
}

func Open(ctx context.Context, cfg Config) (*sql.DB, error) {
	if cfg.URL == "" {
		return nil, nil
	}

	db, err := sql.Open("pgx", cfg.URL)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("open database: %w", err)
	}

	if cfg.MaxOpenConns > 0 {
		db.SetMaxOpenConns(cfg.MaxOpenConns)
	}
	if cfg.MaxIdleConns >= 0 {
		db.SetMaxIdleConns(cfg.MaxIdleConns)
	}
	if cfg.ConnMaxLifetime > 0 {
		db.SetConnMaxLifetime(cfg.ConnMaxLifetime)
	}

	pingCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	if err := db.PingContext(pingCtx); err != nil {
		_ = db.Close()
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("connect to database: %w", err)
	}

	return db, nil
}


####################################################################################################
FILE: internal\database\migrate.go
SIZE: 3443 bytes
LAST MODIFIED: 11/04/2026 07:23:48
####################################################################################################
package database

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/hex"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/migrations"
)

const migrationLockID int64 = 5270645408566230100

func Migrate(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB) error {
	if db == nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("database is not configured")
	}

	conn, err := db.Conn(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("acquire migration connection: %w", err)
	}
	defer conn.Close()

	if _, err := conn.ExecContext(
		ctx,
		"SELECT pg_advisory_lock($1)",
		migrationLockID,
	); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("acquire migration advisory lock: %w", err)
	}

	defer func() {
		unlockCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 5*time.Second)
		defer cancel()
		_, _ = conn.ExecContext(
			unlockCtx,
			"SELECT pg_advisory_unlock($1)",
			migrationLockID,
		)
	}()

	_, err = conn.ExecContext(ctx, `
		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schema_migrations (
			version TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			checksum TEXT,
			applied_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
		)
	`)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("initialize migration tracking: %w", err)
	}

	_, err = conn.ExecContext(ctx, `
		ALTER TABLE schema_migrations
		ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS checksum TEXT
	`)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("upgrade migration tracking: %w", err)
	}

	files, err := fs.Glob(migrations.Files, "*.sql")
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("list migration files: %w", err)
	}
	sort.Strings(files)

	for _, file := range files {
		if strings.Contains(file, "..") {
			return fmt.Errorf("invalid migration path: %s", file)
		}

		content, err := migrations.Files.ReadFile(file)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("read migration %s: %w", file, err)
		}

		version := strings.TrimSuffix(file, ".sql")
		sum := sha256.Sum256(content)
		checksum := hex.EncodeToString(sum[:])

		var stored sql.NullString
		err = conn.QueryRowContext(
			ctx,
			`SELECT checksum FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = $1`,
			version,
		).Scan(&stored)

		if err == nil {
			if !stored.Valid || stored.String == "" {
				// Existing installations created before checksum support:
				// adopt the currently deployed migration as the baseline.
				_, err = conn.ExecContext(
					ctx,
					`UPDATE schema_migrations
					 SET checksum = $1
					 WHERE version = $2 AND checksum IS NULL`,
					checksum,
					version,
				)
				if err != nil {
					return fmt.Errorf("adopt legacy checksum for %s: %w", version, err)
				}
				continue
			}

			if stored.String != checksum {
				return fmt.Errorf(
					"migration checksum mismatch for %s: applied migration file was modified; restore the original file or create a new migration",
					version,
				)
			}
			continue
		}

		if err != sql.ErrNoRows {
			return fmt.Errorf("check migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		tx, err := conn.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("begin migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, string(content)); err == nil {
			_, err = tx.ExecContext(
				ctx,
				`INSERT INTO schema_migrations (version, checksum)
				 VALUES ($1, $2)`,
				version,
				checksum,
			)
		}

		if err != nil {
			_ = tx.Rollback()
			return fmt.Errorf("apply migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		if err := tx.Commit(); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("commit migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}
	}

	return nil
}

package health

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"time"
)

type Handler struct {
	DB *sql.DB
}

func (h Handler) Liveness(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{
		"status": "ok",
	})
}

func (h Handler) Readiness(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if h.DB == nil {
		writeJSON(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, map[string]string{
			"status": "not_ready",
			"reason": "database_not_configured",
		})
		return
	}

	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(r.Context(), 2*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	if err := h.DB.PingContext(ctx); err != nil {
		writeJSON(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, map[string]string{
			"status": "not_ready",
			"reason": "database_unavailable",
		})
		return
	}

	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{
		"status": "ready",
	})
}

func writeJSON(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, value any) {
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
	w.WriteHeader(status)
	_ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(value)
}

SIZE: 1827 bytes

package httpapi

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/catalogue"
)

func New(db *sql.DB) http.Handler {
	mux := http.NewServeMux()

	mux.HandleFunc("GET /health/live", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		writeHealthJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{
			"status": "alive",
		})
	})

	mux.HandleFunc("GET /health/ready", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		if db == nil {
			writeHealthJSON(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, map[string]string{
				"status": "not_ready",
				"reason": "database_unavailable",
			})
			return
		}

		ctx, cancel := timeouts(r)
		defer cancel()

		if err := db.PingContext(ctx); err != nil {
			writeHealthJSON(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, map[string]string{
				"status": "not_ready",
				"reason": "database_unavailable",
			})
			return
		}

		writeHealthJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{
			"status": "ready",
		})
	})

	catalogue.Handler{DB: db}.Register(mux)

	return securityHeaders(mux)
}

func timeouts(r *http.Request) (context.Context, context.CancelFunc) {
	return context.WithTimeout(r.Context(), 2*time.Second)
}

func writeHealthJSON(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, body any) {
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
	w.Header().Set("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
	w.WriteHeader(status)
	_ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(body)
}

func securityHeaders(next http.Handler) http.Handler {
	return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		w.Header().Set("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
		w.Header().Set("X-Frame-Options", "DENY")
		w.Header().Set("Referrer-Policy", "no-referrer")
		w.Header().Set("Cache-Control", "no-store")
		next.ServeHTTP(w, r)
	})
}

package domain

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

var (
	ErrInvalidMarketID       = errors.New("invalid market id")
	ErrInvalidMarketCode     = errors.New("invalid market code")
	ErrInvalidMarketName     = errors.New("invalid market name")
	ErrInvalidMarketTimezone = errors.New("invalid market timezone")
	ErrInvalidTimestamp      = errors.New("invalid timestamp")
	ErrInvalidMarketState    = errors.New("invalid market state")
	ErrMarketClosed          = errors.New("market is closed")
)

type MarketID string

type MarketStatus string

const (
	MarketStatusDraft     MarketStatus = "draft"
	MarketStatusActive    MarketStatus = "active"
	MarketStatusSuspended MarketStatus = "suspended"
	MarketStatusClosed    MarketStatus = "closed"
)

func (s MarketStatus) IsValid() bool {
	switch s {
	case MarketStatusDraft,
		MarketStatusActive,
		MarketStatusSuspended,
		MarketStatusClosed:
		return true
	default:
		return false
	}
}

type CreateMarketInput struct {
	ID          MarketID
	Code        string
	Name        string
	Description string
	Timezone    string
}

type Market struct {
	id          MarketID
	code        string
	name        string
	description string
	timezone    string

	status    MarketStatus
	version   uint64
	createdAt time.Time
	updatedAt time.Time
}

func NewMarket(input CreateMarketInput, now time.Time) (*Market, error) {
	if strings.TrimSpace(string(input.ID)) == "" {
		return nil, ErrInvalidMarketID
	}

	code := normalizeMarketCode(input.Code)
	if !isValidMarketCode(code) {
		return nil, ErrInvalidMarketCode
	}

	name := strings.TrimSpace(input.Name)
	if name == "" || len([]rune(name)) > 160 {
		return nil, ErrInvalidMarketName
	}

	if now.IsZero() {
		return nil, ErrInvalidTimestamp
	}

	timezone := strings.TrimSpace(input.Timezone)
	if timezone == "" {
		timezone = "UTC"
	}

	if _, err := time.LoadLocation(timezone); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: %s", ErrInvalidMarketTimezone, timezone)
	}

	return &Market{
		id:          input.ID,
		code:        code,
		name:        name,
		description: strings.TrimSpace(input.Description),
		timezone:    timezone,
		status:      MarketStatusDraft,
		version:     1,
		createdAt:   now.UTC(),
		updatedAt:   now.UTC(),
	}, nil
}

func normalizeMarketCode(value string) string {
	return strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(value))
}

func isValidMarketCode(value string) bool {
	if len(value) < 2 || len(value) > 32 {
		return false
	}

	for _, r := range value {
		valid := (r >= 'A' && r <= 'Z') ||
			(r >= '0' && r <= '9') ||
			r == '-' ||
			r == '_'

		if !valid {
			return false
		}
	}

	return true
}

func (m *Market) ID() MarketID {
	return m.id
}

func (m *Market) Code() string {
	return m.code
}

func (m *Market) Name() string {
	return m.name
}

func (m *Market) Description() string {
	return m.description
}

func (m *Market) Timezone() string {
	return m.timezone
}

func (m *Market) Status() MarketStatus {
	return m.status
}

func (m *Market) Version() uint64 {
	return m.version
}

func (m *Market) CreatedAt() time.Time {
	return m.createdAt
}

func (m *Market) UpdatedAt() time.Time {
	return m.updatedAt
}

func (m *Market) UpdateDetails(
	name string,
	description string,
	timezone string,
	now time.Time,
) error {
	if m.status == MarketStatusClosed {
		return ErrMarketClosed
	}

	name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
	if name == "" || len([]rune(name)) > 160 {
		return ErrInvalidMarketName
	}

	timezone = strings.TrimSpace(timezone)
	if timezone == "" {
		timezone = "UTC"
	}

	if _, err := time.LoadLocation(timezone); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("%w: %s", ErrInvalidMarketTimezone, timezone)
	}

	if err := m.validateTimestamp(now); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	m.name = name
	m.description = strings.TrimSpace(description)
	m.timezone = timezone
	m.touch(now)

	return nil
}

func (m *Market) Activate(now time.Time) error {
	switch m.status {
	case MarketStatusDraft, MarketStatusSuspended:
		if err := m.validateTimestamp(now); err != nil {
			return err
		}

		m.status = MarketStatusActive
		m.touch(now)
		return nil

	case MarketStatusActive:
		return ErrInvalidMarketState

	case MarketStatusClosed:
		return ErrMarketClosed

	default:
		return ErrInvalidMarketState
	}
}

func (m *Market) Suspend(now time.Time) error {
	if m.status != MarketStatusActive {
		return ErrInvalidMarketState
	}

	if err := m.validateTimestamp(now); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	m.status = MarketStatusSuspended
	m.touch(now)

	return nil
}

func (m *Market) Close(now time.Time) error {
	if m.status == MarketStatusClosed {
		return ErrInvalidMarketState
	}

	if err := m.validateTimestamp(now); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	m.status = MarketStatusClosed
	m.touch(now)

	return nil
}

func (m *Market) IsOperational() bool {
	return m.status == MarketStatusActive
}

func (m *Market) validateTimestamp(now time.Time) error {
	if now.IsZero() {
		return ErrInvalidTimestamp
	}

	if now.Before(m.updatedAt) {
		return ErrInvalidTimestamp
	}

	return nil
}

func (m *Market) touch(now time.Time) {
	m.updatedAt = now.UTC()
	m.version++
}

package domain

import (
	"errors"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

func testMarket(t *testing.T) *Market {
	t.Helper()

	now := time.Date(2026, 11, 4, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)

	market, err := NewMarket(CreateMarketInput{
		ID:          MarketID("market-001"),
		Code:        "general-market",
		Name:        "General Market",
		Description: "Main general market",
		Timezone:    "UTC",
	}, now)

	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("NewMarket() error = %v", err)
	}

	return market
}

func TestNewMarket(t *testing.T) {
	market := testMarket(t)

	if market.Status() != MarketStatusDraft {
		t.Fatalf("expected draft status, got %s", market.Status())
	}

	if market.Code() != "GENERAL-MARKET" {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected normalized code: %s", market.Code())
	}

	if market.Version() != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("expected version 1, got %d", market.Version())
	}
}

func TestNewMarketRejectsInvalidInput(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Now().UTC()

	tests := []struct {
		name  string
		input CreateMarketInput
		want  error
	}{
		{
			name: "missing id",
			input: CreateMarketInput{
				Code: "MKT",
				Name: "Market",
			},
			want: ErrInvalidMarketID,
		},
		{
			name: "invalid code",
			input: CreateMarketInput{
				ID:   "m-1",
				Code: "invalid code!",
				Name: "Market",
			},
			want: ErrInvalidMarketCode,
		},
		{
			name: "missing name",
			input: CreateMarketInput{
				ID:   "m-1",
				Code: "MKT",
				Name: " ",
			},
			want: ErrInvalidMarketName,
		},
		{
			name: "invalid timezone",
			input: CreateMarketInput{
				ID:       "m-1",
				Code:     "MKT",
				Name:     "Market",
				Timezone: "Not/A/Timezone",
			},
			want: ErrInvalidMarketTimezone,
		},
	}

	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			_, err := NewMarket(tt.input, now)

			if !errors.Is(err, tt.want) {
				t.Fatalf("expected error %v, got %v", tt.want, err)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestMarketLifecycle(t *testing.T) {
	market := testMarket(t)
	base := market.UpdatedAt()

	if err := market.Activate(base.Add(time.Minute)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Activate() error = %v", err)
	}

	if !market.IsOperational() {
		t.Fatal("market should be operational")
	}

	if err := market.Suspend(base.Add(2 * time.Minute)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Suspend() error = %v", err)
	}

	if market.Status() != MarketStatusSuspended {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected status: %s", market.Status())
	}

	if err := market.Activate(base.Add(3 * time.Minute)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("reactivation error = %v", err)
	}

	if err := market.Close(base.Add(4 * time.Minute)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Close() error = %v", err)
	}

	if market.Status() != MarketStatusClosed {
		t.Fatalf("expected closed status, got %s", market.Status())
	}

	if err := market.Activate(base.Add(5 * time.Minute)); !errors.Is(err, ErrMarketClosed) {
		t.Fatalf("expected ErrMarketClosed, got %v", err)
	}
}

func TestMarketRejectsInvalidTransitions(t *testing.T) {
	market := testMarket(t)
	now := market.UpdatedAt()

	if err := market.Suspend(now.Add(time.Minute)); !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidMarketState) {
		t.Fatalf("expected invalid state error, got %v", err)
	}

	if err := market.Activate(now.Add(time.Minute)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Activate() error = %v", err)
	}

	if err := market.Activate(now.Add(2 * time.Minute)); !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidMarketState) {
		t.Fatalf("expected invalid state error, got %v", err)
	}
}

func TestMarketRejectsTimeRegression(t *testing.T) {
	market := testMarket(t)

	err := market.Activate(market.CreatedAt().Add(-time.Minute))

	if !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidTimestamp) {
		t.Fatalf("expected ErrInvalidTimestamp, got %v", err)
	}
}

func TestMarketVersionIncrementsOnMutation(t *testing.T) {
	market := testMarket(t)
	initialVersion := market.Version()
	now := market.UpdatedAt()

	if err := market.UpdateDetails(
		"Updated Market",
		"Updated description",
		"UTC",
		now.Add(time.Minute),
	); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("UpdateDetails() error = %v", err)
	}

	if market.Version() != initialVersion+1 {
		t.Fatalf("expected version %d, got %d", initialVersion+1, market.Version())
	}
}


####################################################################################################
FILE: internal\marketflow\capabilities.go
SIZE: 23395 bytes
LAST MODIFIED: 11/04/2026 09:31:56
####################################################################################################
package marketflow

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

var (
	ErrInvalidInput       = errors.New("invalid input")
	ErrInvalidTransition  = errors.New("invalid state transition")
	ErrNotEligible        = errors.New("operation is not eligible")
	ErrNotFound           = errors.New("entity not found")
	ErrConflict           = errors.New("conflict")
	ErrPolicyViolation    = errors.New("execution policy violation")
	ErrExternalNotAllowed = errors.New("external operation is not authorized")
)

type ID string

func validID(id ID) bool {
	return strings.TrimSpace(string(id)) != ""
}

func validTime(t time.Time) bool {
	return !t.IsZero()
}

// ============================================================
// MARKET AND OFFER CONFIGURATION
// ============================================================

type MarketState string

const (
	MarketDraft     MarketState = "draft"
	MarketActive    MarketState = "active"
	MarketSuspended MarketState = "suspended"
	MarketClosed    MarketState = "closed"
)

type OfferState string

const (
	OfferDraft     OfferState = "draft"
	OfferReview    OfferState = "pending_review"
	OfferApproved  OfferState = "approved"
	OfferPublished OfferState = "published"
	OfferSuspended OfferState = "suspended"
	OfferRejected  OfferState = "rejected"
	OfferExpired   OfferState = "expired"
	OfferClosed    OfferState = "closed"
)

type ParameterType string

const (
	ParamString  ParameterType = "string"
	ParamNumber  ParameterType = "number"
	ParamBoolean ParameterType = "boolean"
)

type ParameterDefinition struct {
	Code     string
	Type     ParameterType
	Required bool
	Min      *float64
	Max      *float64
	Allowed  []string
}

type OfferConfiguration struct {
	ID           ID
	CategoryID   ID
	Version      uint64
	Parameters   []ParameterDefinition
	RequiredDocs []string
	Active       bool
	CreatedAt    time.Time
}

type OfferParameters map[string]any

func (c OfferConfiguration) Validate(values OfferParameters) error {
	if !validID(c.ID) || !validID(c.CategoryID) ||
		c.Version == 0 || !validTime(c.CreatedAt) {
		return ErrInvalidInput
	}

	for _, definition := range c.Parameters {
		value, exists := values[definition.Code]

		if !exists {
			if definition.Required {
				return fmt.Errorf("%w: missing parameter %s",
					ErrInvalidInput, definition.Code)
			}
			continue
		}

		switch definition.Type {
		case ParamString:
			if _, ok := value.(string); !ok {
				return fmt.Errorf("%w: parameter %s must be string",
					ErrInvalidInput, definition.Code)
			}

		case ParamBoolean:
			if _, ok := value.(bool); !ok {
				return fmt.Errorf("%w: parameter %s must be boolean",
					ErrInvalidInput, definition.Code)
			}

		case ParamNumber:
			number, ok := value.(float64)
			if !ok {
				return fmt.Errorf("%w: parameter %s must be number",
					ErrInvalidInput, definition.Code)
			}
			if definition.Min != nil && number < *definition.Min {
				return fmt.Errorf("%w: parameter %s below minimum",
					ErrInvalidInput, definition.Code)
			}
			if definition.Max != nil && number > *definition.Max {
				return fmt.Errorf("%w: parameter %s above maximum",
					ErrInvalidInput, definition.Code)
			}

		default:
			return ErrInvalidInput
		}

		if len(definition.Allowed) > 0 {
			text, ok := value.(string)
			if !ok {
				return ErrInvalidInput
			}

			allowed := false
			for _, candidate := range definition.Allowed {
				if candidate == text {
					allowed = true
					break
				}
			}
			if !allowed {
				return fmt.Errorf("%w: parameter %s is not allowed",
					ErrInvalidInput, definition.Code)
			}
		}
	}

	return nil
}

// ============================================================
// COMPANY ELIGIBILITY
// ============================================================

type QualificationState string

const (
	QualificationPending  QualificationState = "pending"
	QualificationApproved QualificationState = "approved"
	QualificationRejected QualificationState = "rejected"
	QualificationExpired  QualificationState = "expired"
)

type CompanyQualification struct {
	CompanyID    ID
	CategoryID   ID
	ActivityCode string
	State        QualificationState
	Documents    []string
	ValidUntil   time.Time
	Version      uint64
}

func (q CompanyQualification) IsEligible(
	categoryID ID,
	activityCode string,
	requiredDocuments []string,
	now time.Time,
) error {
	if !validID(q.CompanyID) || !validID(categoryID) ||
		!validTime(now) || q.Version == 0 {
		return ErrInvalidInput
	}

	if q.State != QualificationApproved ||
		q.CategoryID != categoryID ||
		q.ActivityCode != activityCode {
		return ErrNotEligible
	}

	if !q.ValidUntil.IsZero() && now.After(q.ValidUntil) {
		return ErrNotEligible
	}

	available := make(map[string]bool, len(q.Documents))
	for _, document := range q.Documents {
		available[document] = true
	}

	for _, document := range requiredDocuments {
		if !available[document] {
			return fmt.Errorf("%w: missing document %s",
				ErrNotEligible, document)
		}
	}

	return nil
}

// ============================================================
// OFFER LIFECYCLE
// ============================================================

type Offer struct {
	ID                    ID
	MarketID              ID
	OwnerID               ID
	PublicCategoryID      ID
	SpecializedCategoryID ID
	ConfigurationID       ID
	ConfigurationVersion  uint64
	Title                 string
	Description           string
	Quantity              int64
	Remaining             int64
	Unit                  string
	Price                 int64
	Currency              string
	State                 OfferState
	ValidUntil            time.Time
	Version               uint64
	CreatedAt             time.Time
	UpdatedAt             time.Time
}

func (o *Offer) Validate() error {
	if o == nil || !validID(o.ID) || !validID(o.MarketID) ||
		!validID(o.OwnerID) || !validID(o.PublicCategoryID) ||
		!validID(o.SpecializedCategoryID) ||
		!validID(o.ConfigurationID) ||
		o.ConfigurationVersion == 0 ||
		strings.TrimSpace(o.Title) == "" ||
		o.Quantity <= 0 || o.Remaining < 0 ||
		o.Remaining > o.Quantity || o.Price < 0 ||
		strings.TrimSpace(o.Unit) == "" ||
		strings.TrimSpace(o.Currency) == "" ||
		o.Version == 0 || !validTime(o.CreatedAt) ||
		!validTime(o.UpdatedAt) {
		return ErrInvalidInput
	}
	return nil
}

func (o *Offer) SubmitForReview(now time.Time) error {
	if err := o.Validate(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if o.State != OfferDraft || !validTime(now) {
		return ErrInvalidTransition
	}
	o.State = OfferReview
	o.touch(now)
	return nil
}

func (o *Offer) Approve(now time.Time) error {
	if o.State != OfferReview || !validTime(now) {
		return ErrInvalidTransition
	}
	o.State = OfferApproved
	o.touch(now)
	return nil
}

func (o *Offer) Publish(now time.Time) error {
	if o.State != OfferApproved || !validTime(now) {
		return ErrInvalidTransition
	}
	if o.Remaining <= 0 {
		return ErrNotEligible
	}
	if !o.ValidUntil.IsZero() && !now.Before(o.ValidUntil) {
		return ErrNotEligible
	}
	o.State = OfferPublished
	o.touch(now)
	return nil
}

func (o *Offer) Suspend(now time.Time) error {
	if o.State != OfferPublished || !validTime(now) {
		return ErrInvalidTransition
	}
	o.State = OfferSuspended
	o.touch(now)
	return nil
}

func (o *Offer) Close(now time.Time) error {
	if o.State == OfferClosed || !validTime(now) {
		return ErrInvalidTransition
	}
	o.State = OfferClosed
	o.touch(now)
	return nil
}

func (o *Offer) touch(now time.Time) {
	o.UpdatedAt = now.UTC()
	o.Version++
}

// ============================================================
// SUPPLY REQUESTS AND PROPOSALS
// ============================================================

type RequestState string

const (
	RequestOpen      RequestState = "open"
	RequestSelected  RequestState = "selected"
	RequestClosed    RequestState = "closed"
	RequestCancelled RequestState = "cancelled"
)

type SupplyRequest struct {
	ID                 ID
	RequesterID        ID
	CategoryID         ID
	Quantity           int64
	Unit               string
	MaxPrice           int64
	Currency           string
	State              RequestState
	SelectedProposalID ID
	CreatedAt          time.Time
}

type ProposalState string

const (
	ProposalSubmitted ProposalState = "submitted"
	ProposalAccepted  ProposalState = "accepted"
	ProposalRejected  ProposalState = "rejected"
	ProposalWithdrawn ProposalState = "withdrawn"
)

type SupplyProposal struct {
	ID            ID
	RequestID     ID
	SupplierID    ID
	OfferID       ID
	Quantity      int64
	UnitPrice     int64
	Currency      string
	DeliveryTerms string
	State         ProposalState
	CreatedAt     time.Time
}

func (r SupplyRequest) Validate() error {
	if !validID(r.ID) || !validID(r.RequesterID) ||
		!validID(r.CategoryID) || r.Quantity <= 0 ||
		r.Unit == "" || r.Currency == "" ||
		!validTime(r.CreatedAt) {
		return ErrInvalidInput
	}
	return nil
}

func (p SupplyProposal) IsCompatible(r SupplyRequest) bool {
	return p.RequestID == r.ID &&
		p.Quantity >= r.Quantity &&
		p.UnitPrice > 0 &&
		p.Currency == r.Currency &&
		p.State == ProposalSubmitted
}

func SelectProposal(
	request *SupplyRequest,
	proposal SupplyProposal,
) error {
	if request == nil || request.State != RequestOpen {
		return ErrInvalidTransition
	}
	if !validID(proposal.ID) || !proposal.IsCompatible(*request) {
		return ErrNotEligible
	}
	if request.MaxPrice > 0 && proposal.UnitPrice > request.MaxPrice {
		return ErrNotEligible
	}

	request.SelectedProposalID = proposal.ID
	request.State = RequestSelected
	return nil
}

// ============================================================
// ORDERS AND ORDER BOOK
// All prices are integer minor units. No float arithmetic.
// ============================================================

type OrderSide string

const (
	Buy  OrderSide = "buy"
	Sell OrderSide = "sell"
)

type OrderState string

const (
	OrderPending   OrderState = "pending"
	OrderOpen      OrderState = "open"
	OrderPartial   OrderState = "partially_filled"
	OrderFilled    OrderState = "filled"
	OrderCancelled OrderState = "cancelled"
	OrderRejected  OrderState = "rejected"
)

type Order struct {
	ID        ID
	OfferID   ID
	MarketID  ID
	UserID    ID
	Side      OrderSide
	Price     int64
	Quantity  int64
	Filled    int64
	State     OrderState
	CreatedAt time.Time
	Version   uint64
}

func (o Order) Validate() error {
	if !validID(o.ID) || !validID(o.OfferID) ||
		!validID(o.MarketID) || !validID(o.UserID) ||
		(o.Side != Buy && o.Side != Sell) ||
		o.Price <= 0 || o.Quantity <= 0 ||
		o.Filled < 0 || o.Filled > o.Quantity ||
		!validTime(o.CreatedAt) || o.Version == 0 {
		return ErrInvalidInput
	}
	return nil
}

func (o *Order) Open() error {
	if err := o.Validate(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if o.State != OrderPending {
		return ErrInvalidTransition
	}
	o.State = OrderOpen
	o.Version++
	return nil
}

func (o *Order) Cancel() error {
	if o.State != OrderOpen && o.State != OrderPartial {
		return ErrInvalidTransition
	}
	o.State = OrderCancelled
	o.Version++
	return nil
}

func (o *Order) Remaining() int64 {
	return o.Quantity - o.Filled
}

type MatchResult struct {
	BuyOrderID  ID
	SellOrderID ID
	Quantity    int64
	Price       int64
}

func MatchOrders(buy, sell *Order) (MatchResult, error) {
	if buy == nil || sell == nil {
		return MatchResult{}, ErrInvalidInput
	}
	if err := buy.Validate(); err != nil {
		return MatchResult{}, err
	}
	if err := sell.Validate(); err != nil {
		return MatchResult{}, err
	}

	if buy.Side != Buy || sell.Side != Sell ||
		buy.State != OrderOpen && buy.State != OrderPartial ||
		sell.State != OrderOpen && sell.State != OrderPartial {
		return MatchResult{}, ErrInvalidTransition
	}

	if buy.MarketID != sell.MarketID ||
		buy.OfferID != sell.OfferID ||
		buy.UserID == sell.UserID ||
		buy.Price < sell.Price {
		return MatchResult{}, ErrNotEligible
	}

	quantity := buy.Remaining()
	if sell.Remaining() < quantity {
		quantity = sell.Remaining()
	}
	if quantity <= 0 {
		return MatchResult{}, ErrNotEligible
	}

	// Price-time rule: older order is the maker.
	executionPrice := buy.Price
	if sell.CreatedAt.Before(buy.CreatedAt) ||
		sell.CreatedAt.Equal(buy.CreatedAt) {
		executionPrice = sell.Price
	}

	buy.Filled += quantity
	sell.Filled += quantity

	if buy.Remaining() == 0 {
		buy.State = OrderFilled
	} else {
		buy.State = OrderPartial
	}

	if sell.Remaining() == 0 {
		sell.State = OrderFilled
	} else {
		sell.State = OrderPartial
	}

	buy.Version++
	sell.Version++

	return MatchResult{
		BuyOrderID:  buy.ID,
		SellOrderID: sell.ID,
		Quantity:    quantity,
		Price:       executionPrice,
	}, nil
}

func SortOrderBook(orders []Order) {
	sort.SliceStable(orders, func(i, j int) bool {
		a, b := orders[i], orders[j]

		if a.Side != b.Side {
			return a.Side == Buy
		}
		if a.Price != b.Price {
			if a.Side == Buy {
				return a.Price > b.Price
			}
			return a.Price < b.Price
		}
		return a.CreatedAt.Before(b.CreatedAt)
	})
}

// ============================================================
// TRADE - independent status dimensions
// ============================================================

type TradeState string
type ContractState string
type ServiceState string
type DeliveryState string
type SettlementReadiness string
type SettlementState string

const (
	TradeCreated   TradeState = "created"
	TradeConfirmed TradeState = "confirmed"
	TradeExecuting TradeState = "executing"
	TradeCompleted TradeState = "completed"
	TradeCancelled TradeState = "cancelled"

	ContractNotRequired ContractState = "not_required"
	ContractPending     ContractState = "pending"
	ContractCreated     ContractState = "created"
	ContractFailed      ContractState = "failed"

	ServiceNotRequired ServiceState = "not_required"
	ServicePending     ServiceState = "pending"
	ServiceInProgress  ServiceState = "in_progress"
	ServiceCompleted   ServiceState = "completed"
	ServiceFailed      ServiceState = "failed"

	DeliveryNotRequired DeliveryState = "not_required"
	DeliveryPending     DeliveryState = "pending"
	DeliveryConfirmed   DeliveryState = "confirmed"
	DeliveryFailed      DeliveryState = "failed"

	SettlementNotReady SettlementReadiness = "not_ready"
	SettlementReady    SettlementReadiness = "ready"

	SettlementPending SettlementState = "pending"
	SettlementDone    SettlementState = "completed"
	SettlementFailed  SettlementState = "failed"
)

type Trade struct {
	ID          ID
	MatchID     ID
	BuyOrderID  ID
	SellOrderID ID
	BuyerID     ID
	SellerID    ID
	OfferID     ID
	Quantity    int64
	Price       int64

	State               TradeState
	Contract            ContractState
	Services            ServiceState
	Delivery            DeliveryState
	SettlementReadiness SettlementReadiness
	Settlement          SettlementState

	CreatedAt time.Time
	UpdatedAt time.Time
	Version   uint64
}

func (t Trade) Validate() error {
	if !validID(t.ID) || !validID(t.BuyOrderID) ||
		!validID(t.SellOrderID) || !validID(t.BuyerID) ||
		!validID(t.SellerID) || !validID(t.OfferID) ||
		t.BuyerID == t.SellerID || t.Quantity <= 0 ||
		t.Price <= 0 || !validTime(t.CreatedAt) ||
		!validTime(t.UpdatedAt) || t.Version == 0 {
		return ErrInvalidInput
	}
	return nil
}

func (t *Trade) RecalculateSettlementReadiness() {
	if t.Contract == ContractFailed ||
		t.Services == ServiceFailed ||
		t.Delivery == DeliveryFailed {
		t.SettlementReadiness = SettlementNotReady
		return
	}

	contractOK := t.Contract == ContractNotRequired ||
		t.Contract == ContractCreated
	servicesOK := t.Services == ServiceNotRequired ||
		t.Services == ServiceCompleted
	deliveryOK := t.Delivery == DeliveryNotRequired ||
		t.Delivery == DeliveryConfirmed

	if contractOK && servicesOK && deliveryOK {
		t.SettlementReadiness = SettlementReady
	} else {
		t.SettlementReadiness = SettlementNotReady
	}
}

// ============================================================
// VERSIONED EXECUTION POLICIES
// ============================================================

type StepRequirement string

const (
	StepRequired    StepRequirement = "required"
	StepOptional    StepRequirement = "optional"
	StepConditional StepRequirement = "conditional"
)

type ExecutionStep struct {
	Code              string
	Order             int
	Requirement       StepRequirement
	RequiredDocuments []string
	DependsOn         []string
}

type ExecutionPolicy struct {
	ID              ID
	Name            string
	Version         uint64
	OfferCategoryID ID
	Steps           []ExecutionStep
	Active          bool
	CreatedAt       time.Time
}

func (p ExecutionPolicy) Validate() error {
	if !validID(p.ID) || !validID(p.OfferCategoryID) ||
		strings.TrimSpace(p.Name) == "" ||
		p.Version == 0 || !validTime(p.CreatedAt) ||
		len(p.Steps) == 0 {
		return ErrInvalidInput
	}

	seen := make(map[string]bool)
	for _, step := range p.Steps {
		if strings.TrimSpace(step.Code) == "" ||
			step.Order < 1 || seen[step.Code] {
			return ErrInvalidInput
		}
		if step.Requirement != StepRequired &&
			step.Requirement != StepOptional &&
			step.Requirement != StepConditional {
			return ErrInvalidInput
		}
		seen[step.Code] = true
	}

	for _, step := range p.Steps {
		for _, dependency := range step.DependsOn {
			if !seen[dependency] || dependency == step.Code {
				return ErrPolicyViolation
			}
		}
	}

	return nil
}

type ExecutionProgress struct {
	TradeID        ID
	PolicyID       ID
	PolicyVersion  uint64
	CompletedSteps map[string]bool
}

func (p ExecutionProgress) CanComplete(
	policy ExecutionPolicy,
	stepCode string,
) error {
	if p.PolicyID != policy.ID ||
		p.PolicyVersion != policy.Version {
		return ErrConflict
	}

	var selected *ExecutionStep
	for i := range policy.Steps {
		if policy.Steps[i].Code == stepCode {
			selected = &policy.Steps[i]
			break
		}
	}
	if selected == nil {
		return ErrNotFound
	}

	for _, dependency := range selected.DependsOn {
		if !p.CompletedSteps[dependency] {
			return fmt.Errorf("%w: dependency %s",
				ErrPolicyViolation, dependency)
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// ============================================================
// PHYSICAL COMMODITY AGGREGATION
// ============================================================

type CommodityLot struct {
	ID                 ID
	OfferID            ID
	CommodityCode      string
	Quantity           int64
	Unit               string
	Verified           bool
	AggregationAllowed bool
}

type CommodityAggregation struct {
	ID            ID
	CommodityCode string
	Unit          string
	LotIDs        []ID
	TotalQuantity int64
	CreatedAt     time.Time
}

func AggregateCommodity(
	id ID,
	lots []CommodityLot,
	minimumQuantity int64,
	now time.Time,
) (CommodityAggregation, error) {
	if !validID(id) || len(lots) == 0 ||
		minimumQuantity <= 0 || !validTime(now) {
		return CommodityAggregation{}, ErrInvalidInput
	}

	first := lots[0]
	if first.CommodityCode == "" || first.Unit == "" {
		return CommodityAggregation{}, ErrInvalidInput
	}

	result := CommodityAggregation{
		ID:            id,
		CommodityCode: first.CommodityCode,
		Unit:          first.Unit,
		CreatedAt:     now.UTC(),
	}

	seen := make(map[ID]bool)

	for _, lot := range lots {
		if !validID(lot.ID) || lot.Quantity <= 0 ||
			lot.CommodityCode != first.CommodityCode ||
			lot.Unit != first.Unit ||
			!lot.Verified || !lot.AggregationAllowed ||
			seen[lot.ID] {
			return CommodityAggregation{}, ErrNotEligible
		}

		seen[lot.ID] = true
		result.LotIDs = append(result.LotIDs, lot.ID)
		result.TotalQuantity += lot.Quantity
	}

	if result.TotalQuantity < minimumQuantity {
		return CommodityAggregation{}, ErrNotEligible
	}

	return result, nil
}

// ============================================================
// REAL ESTATE - platform position != legal ownership
// ============================================================

type PropertyWorkflowState string
type PlatformPositionState string
type LegalRegistrationState string

const (
	PropertyPending      PropertyWorkflowState = "pending"
	PropertyTrading      PropertyWorkflowState = "trading"
	PropertyContracting  PropertyWorkflowState = "contracting"
	PropertyRegistration PropertyWorkflowState = "registration"
	PropertyCompleted    PropertyWorkflowState = "completed"

	PositionNotRecorded PlatformPositionState = "not_recorded"
	PositionRecorded    PlatformPositionState = "recorded"
	PositionRejected    PlatformPositionState = "rejected"

	LegalNotRequired LegalRegistrationState = "not_required"
	LegalPending     LegalRegistrationState = "pending"
	LegalSubmitted   LegalRegistrationState = "submitted"
	LegalConfirmed   LegalRegistrationState = "confirmed"
	LegalRejected    LegalRegistrationState = "rejected"
)

type PropertyWorkflow struct {
	ID                ID
	OfferID           ID
	TradeID           ID
	PropertyReference string
	PlatformPosition  PlatformPositionState
	LegalRegistration LegalRegistrationState
	State             PropertyWorkflowState
	UpdatedAt         time.Time
}

func (p *PropertyWorkflow) RecordPlatformPosition(
	confirmedTradeID ID,
	now time.Time,
) error {
	if p == nil || !validID(confirmedTradeID) ||
		confirmedTradeID != p.TradeID ||
		!validTime(now) {
		return ErrNotEligible
	}

	p.PlatformPosition = PositionRecorded
	p.UpdatedAt = now.UTC()
	return nil
}

func (p PropertyWorkflow) IsLegallyRegistered() bool {
	return p.LegalRegistration == LegalConfirmed
}

// ============================================================
// LEGAL / EXTERNAL INTEGRATION TRACKING
// ============================================================

type ExternalRequestState string

const (
	ExternalPending   ExternalRequestState = "pending"
	ExternalSent      ExternalRequestState = "sent"
	ExternalSucceeded ExternalRequestState = "succeeded"
	ExternalFailed    ExternalRequestState = "failed"
)

type ExternalRequest struct {
	ID                 ID
	CorrelationID      ID
	IdempotencyKey     string
	InstitutionCode    string
	Purpose            string
	Scope              []string
	PayloadReference   string
	State              ExternalRequestState
	ExternalTrackingID string
	ResponseReference  string
	Attempt            int
	CreatedAt          time.Time
	UpdatedAt          time.Time
}

func (r ExternalRequest) ValidateAuthorization(
	grantedScopes []string,
) error {
	if !validID(r.ID) || !validID(r.CorrelationID) ||
		strings.TrimSpace(r.IdempotencyKey) == "" ||
		strings.TrimSpace(r.InstitutionCode) == "" ||
		strings.TrimSpace(r.Purpose) == "" ||
		len(r.Scope) == 0 || !validTime(r.CreatedAt) {
		return ErrInvalidInput
	}

	allowed := make(map[string]bool)
	for _, scope := range grantedScopes {
		allowed[scope] = true
	}

	for _, scope := range r.Scope {
		if !allowed[scope] {
			return ErrExternalNotAllowed
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// ============================================================
// AUDIT
// ============================================================

type AuditEvent struct {
	ID              ID
	AggregateType   string
	AggregateID     ID
	Action          string
	ActorID         ID
	CorrelationID   ID
	PreviousVersion uint64
	NewVersion      uint64
	OccurredAt      time.Time
	Metadata        map[string]string
}

func (e AuditEvent) Validate() error {
	if !validID(e.ID) || !validID(e.AggregateID) ||
		!validID(e.ActorID) || !validID(e.CorrelationID) ||
		strings.TrimSpace(e.AggregateType) == "" ||
		strings.TrimSpace(e.Action) == "" ||
		!validTime(e.OccurredAt) ||
		e.NewVersion < e.PreviousVersion {
		return ErrInvalidInput
	}
	return nil
}

package marketflow

import (
	"errors"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

func TestOfferLifecycle(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Date(2026, 11, 4, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)

	offer := &Offer{
		ID:                    "offer-1",
		MarketID:              "market-1",
		OwnerID:               "owner-1",
		PublicCategoryID:      "public-1",
		SpecializedCategoryID: "special-1",
		ConfigurationID:       "config-1",
		ConfigurationVersion:  1,
		Title:                 "Test offer",
		Quantity:              100,
		Remaining:             100,
		Unit:                  "unit",
		Price:                 1000,
		Currency:              "IRR",
		State:                 OfferDraft,
		Version:               1,
		CreatedAt:             now,
		UpdatedAt:             now,
	}

	if err := offer.SubmitForReview(now.Add(time.Minute)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := offer.Approve(now.Add(2 * time.Minute)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := offer.Publish(now.Add(3 * time.Minute)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if offer.State != OfferPublished {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected state: %s", offer.State)
	}
}

func TestOrderMatchingPartialFill(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Date(2026, 11, 4, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)

	buy := &Order{
		ID: "buy-1", OfferID: "offer-1", MarketID: "market-1",
		UserID: "buyer", Side: Buy, Price: 1200,
		Quantity: 100, State: OrderOpen,
		CreatedAt: now, Version: 1,
	}

	sell := &Order{
		ID: "sell-1", OfferID: "offer-1", MarketID: "market-1",
		UserID: "seller", Side: Sell, Price: 1100,
		Quantity: 40, State: OrderOpen,
		CreatedAt: now.Add(time.Minute), Version: 1,
	}

	result, err := MatchOrders(buy, sell)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	if result.Quantity != 40 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 40, got %d", result.Quantity)
	}
	if buy.State != OrderPartial || sell.State != OrderFilled {
		t.Fatal("incorrect order states after partial fill")
	}
	if buy.Remaining() != 60 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 60 remaining, got %d", buy.Remaining())
	}
}

func TestOrdersCannotMatchTheirOwnUser(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Now().UTC()

	buy := &Order{
		ID: "b", OfferID: "o", MarketID: "m",
		UserID: "same", Side: Buy, Price: 100,
		Quantity: 10, State: OrderOpen,
		CreatedAt: now, Version: 1,
	}

	sell := &Order{
		ID: "s", OfferID: "o", MarketID: "m",
		UserID: "same", Side: Sell, Price: 90,
		Quantity: 10, State: OrderOpen,
		CreatedAt: now, Version: 1,
	}

	_, err := MatchOrders(buy, sell)
	if !errors.Is(err, ErrNotEligible) {
		t.Fatalf("expected ErrNotEligible, got %v", err)
	}
}

func TestProposalSelection(t *testing.T) {
	request := &SupplyRequest{
		ID:          "request-1",
		RequesterID: "buyer",
		CategoryID:  "category-1",
		Quantity:    50,
		Unit:        "ton",
		MaxPrice:    200,
		Currency:    "IRR",
		State:       RequestOpen,
		CreatedAt:   time.Now().UTC(),
	}

	proposal := SupplyProposal{
		ID:         "proposal-1",
		RequestID:  "request-1",
		SupplierID: "supplier",
		OfferID:    "offer-1",
		Quantity:   60,
		UnitPrice:  180,
		Currency:   "IRR",
		State:      ProposalSubmitted,
		CreatedAt:  time.Now().UTC(),
	}

	if err := SelectProposal(request, proposal); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	if request.State != RequestSelected ||
		request.SelectedProposalID != proposal.ID {
		t.Fatal("proposal was not selected")
	}
}

func TestExternalRequestRequiresGrantedScope(t *testing.T) {
	request := ExternalRequest{
		ID:              "external-1",
		CorrelationID:   "correlation-1",
		IdempotencyKey:  "idempotency-1",
		InstitutionCode: "registry",
		Purpose:         "property verification",
		Scope:           []string{"property.read"},
		State:           ExternalPending,
		CreatedAt:       time.Now().UTC(),
	}

	if err := request.ValidateAuthorization([]string{"property.read"}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	if err := request.ValidateAuthorization([]string{"identity.read"}); !errors.Is(err, ErrExternalNotAllowed) {
		t.Fatalf("expected authorization failure, got %v", err)
	}
}

func TestExecutionPolicyDependencies(t *testing.T) {
	policy := ExecutionPolicy{
		ID:              "policy-1",
		Name:            "Property transaction",
		Version:         1,
		OfferCategoryID: "property",
		Active:          true,
		CreatedAt:       time.Now().UTC(),
		Steps: []ExecutionStep{
			{Code: "contract", Order: 1, Requirement: StepRequired},
			{Code: "position", Order: 2, Requirement: StepRequired, DependsOn: []string{"contract"}},
		},
	}

	if err := policy.Validate(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	progress := ExecutionProgress{
		TradeID:        "trade-1",
		PolicyID:       policy.ID,
		PolicyVersion:  1,
		CompletedSteps: map[string]bool{},
	}

	if err := progress.CanComplete(policy, "position"); !errors.Is(err, ErrPolicyViolation) {
		t.Fatalf("expected dependency failure, got %v", err)
	}

	progress.CompletedSteps["contract"] = true
	if err := progress.CanComplete(policy, "position"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func TestCommodityAggregation(t *testing.T) {
	now := time.Now().UTC()

	lots := []CommodityLot{
		{
			ID: "lot-1", CommodityCode: "WHEAT",
			Quantity: 30, Unit: "ton",
			Verified: true, AggregationAllowed: true,
		},
		{
			ID: "lot-2", CommodityCode: "WHEAT",
			Quantity: 40, Unit: "ton",
			Verified: true, AggregationAllowed: true,
		},
	}

	result, err := AggregateCommodity("aggregation-1", lots, 60, now)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if result.TotalQuantity != 70 {
		t.Fatalf("expected 70, got %d", result.TotalQuantity)
	}
}

func TestPlatformPositionDoesNotMeanLegalRegistration(t *testing.T) {
	workflow := PropertyWorkflow{
		ID:                "property-flow-1",
		OfferID:           "offer-1",
		TradeID:           "trade-1",
		PlatformPosition:  PositionNotRecorded,
		LegalRegistration: LegalPending,
		State:             PropertyRegistration,
	}

	if err := workflow.RecordPlatformPosition("trade-1", time.Now()); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	if workflow.IsLegallyRegistered() {
		t.Fatal("platform position must not imply legal registration")
	}
}

package marketflow

import "context"

// These are permanent contracts.
// Implementations can later use PostgreSQL and real service clients.
// No service below is treated as operational until an adapter exists.

type OfferRepository interface {
	GetOffer(context.Context, ID) (Offer, error)
	SaveOffer(context.Context, Offer, uint64) error
	ListPublishedOffers(context.Context, int, int) ([]Offer, error)
}

type OrderRepository interface {
	GetOrder(context.Context, ID) (Order, error)
	SaveOrder(context.Context, Order, uint64) error
	GetOpenBook(context.Context, ID, ID) ([]Order, error)
}

type TradeRepository interface {
	GetTrade(context.Context, ID) (Trade, error)
	SaveTrade(context.Context, Trade, uint64) error
}

type SupplyRequestRepository interface {
	GetSupplyRequest(context.Context, ID) (SupplyRequest, error)
	SaveSupplyRequest(context.Context, SupplyRequest) error
}

type ExecutionPolicyRepository interface {
	GetActivePolicy(context.Context, ID) (ExecutionPolicy, error)
}

type AuditRepository interface {
	Append(context.Context, AuditEvent) error
	ListByAggregate(context.Context, string, ID, int) ([]AuditEvent, error)
}

// Account service is the authority for identity and base permissions.
type IdentityGateway interface {
	VerifyIdentity(context.Context, ID) error
	Authorize(context.Context, ID, string) error
}

// My Assets is the authority for buying power and reservations.
type AssetGateway interface {
	CheckBuyingPower(context.Context, ID, int64, string) error
	Reserve(context.Context, ID, ID, int64, string) (ID, error)
	Release(context.Context, ID, ID) error
	RegisterPlatformPosition(context.Context, ID, ID, int64) error
}

// Independent authoritative services.
type ContractGateway interface {
	CreateContract(context.Context, ID, ID) (ID, error)
	GetContractStatus(context.Context, ID) (string, error)
}

type ServiceGateway interface {
	RequestService(context.Context, ID, string, map[string]string) (ID, error)
	GetServiceStatus(context.Context, ID) (string, error)
}

type SettlementGateway interface {
	RequestSettlement(context.Context, ID, ID) (ID, error)
	GetSettlementStatus(context.Context, ID) (string, error)
}

type LegalGateway interface {
	SubmitAuthorizedRequest(context.Context, ExternalRequest) (string, error)
	GetRequestResult(context.Context, string) (string, error)
}

// atomic writes, and idempotency where applicable.
// The domain package itself does not pretend to provide persistence.

[CmdletBinding()]
param(

# ============================================================

# Market + Catalogue Domain Modeling

#   - Go domain models
#   - Domain invariants
