function Write-Section {
    param([string]$Message)
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host ("=" * 72) -ForegroundColor DarkCyan
    Write-Host $Message -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host ("=" * 72) -ForegroundColor DarkCyan
}

function Write-Info {
    param([string]$Message)
    Write-Host "[INFO] $Message" -ForegroundColor Gray
}

function Write-Success {
    param([string]$Message)
    Write-Host "[OK] $Message" -ForegroundColor Green
}

function Write-WarningMessage {
    param([string]$Message)
    Write-Host "[WARNING] $Message" -ForegroundColor Yellow
}

function Stop-Script {
    param([string]$Message)
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "[STOP] $Message" -ForegroundColor Red
    throw $Message
}

function Find-ProjectRoot {
    param([string]$InitialPath)

    if (-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($InitialPath)) {
        $candidate = (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $InitialPath).Path

        if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $candidate "go.mod"))) {
            Stop-Script "go.mod در مسیر تعیین‌شده پیدا نشد: $candidate"
        }

        return $candidate
    }

    $current = (Get-Location).Path

    while (-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($current)) {
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $current "go.mod")) {
            return $current
        }

        $parent = Split-Path -Parent $current

        if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($parent) -or $parent -eq $current) {
            break
        }

        $current = $parent
    }

    Stop-Script "پروژه Go پیدا نشد. اسکریپت را از ریشه پروژه اجرا کنید یا ProjectRoot بدهید."
}

function Resolve-ModuleDirectory {
    param(
        [string]$Root,
        [string[]]$Candidates,
        [string]$ModuleName
    )

    $found = @()

    foreach ($candidate in $Candidates) {
        $fullPath = Join-Path $Root $candidate

        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $fullPath -PathType Container) {
            $found += $fullPath
        }
    }

    if ($found.Count -eq 0) {
        Stop-Script "ماژول '$ModuleName' پیدا نشد. مسیرهای بررسی‌شده: $($Candidates -join ', ')"
    }

    if ($found.Count -gt 1) {
        Stop-Script "برای ماژول '$ModuleName' بیش از یک مسیر معتبر وجود دارد: $($found -join ', '). مسیر باید دستی تعیین تکلیف شود."
    }

    return $found[0]
}

function Write-Utf8NoBom {
    param(
        [string]$Path,
        [string]$Content
    )

    $encoding = New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)
    [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($Path, $Content, $encoding)
}

function Add-NewFile {
    param(
        [string]$Path,
        [string]$Content
    )

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Path) {
        Stop-Script "فایل مقصد از قبل وجود دارد و برای جلوگیری از بازنویسی، اجرا متوقف شد: $Path"
    }

    if ($WhatIf) {
        Write-Host "[WHATIF] ایجاد فایل: $Path" -ForegroundColor Yellow
        return
    }

    $parent = Split-Path -Parent $Path

    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $parent)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $parent -Force | Out-Null
    }

    Write-Utf8NoBom -Path $Path -Content $Content
    Write-Success "ایجاد شد: $Path"
}

)

$ExistingTargets = @(
    $AllTargets | Where-Object {
        Test-Path -LiteralPath $_
    }
)

if ($ExistingTargets.Count -gt 0) {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-WarningMessage "فایل‌های زیر از قبل وجود دارند:"
    $ExistingTargets | ForEach-Object {
        Write-Host " - $_" -ForegroundColor Yellow
    }

    Stop-Script "هیچ فایلی بازنویسی نشد. ابتدا باید مدل‌های موجود بررسی و با این بسته ادغام شوند."
}

if ($WhatIf) {
    Write-WarningMessage "حالت WhatIf فعال است؛ هیچ فایلی نوشته نمی‌شود."

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Market domain model
# ------------------------------------------------------------

$MarketGo = @'
package domain

import (
    "errors"
    "fmt"
    "strings"
    "time"
)

var (
    ErrInvalidMarketID       = errors.New("invalid market id")
    ErrInvalidMarketCode     = errors.New("invalid market code")
    ErrInvalidMarketName     = errors.New("invalid market name")
    ErrInvalidMarketTimezone = errors.New("invalid market timezone")
    ErrInvalidTimestamp      = errors.New("invalid timestamp")
    ErrInvalidMarketState    = errors.New("invalid market state")
    ErrMarketClosed          = errors.New("market is closed")
)

type MarketID string

type MarketStatus string

const (
    MarketStatusDraft     MarketStatus = "draft"
    MarketStatusActive    MarketStatus = "active"
    MarketStatusSuspended MarketStatus = "suspended"
    MarketStatusClosed    MarketStatus = "closed"
)

func (s MarketStatus) IsValid() bool {
    switch s {
    case MarketStatusDraft,
        MarketStatusActive,
        MarketStatusSuspended,
        MarketStatusClosed:
        return true
    default:
        return false
    }
}

type CreateMarketInput struct {
    ID          MarketID
    Code        string
    Name        string
    Description string
    Timezone    string
}

type Market struct {
    id          MarketID
    code        string
    name        string
    description string
    timezone    string

    status    MarketStatus
    version   uint64
    createdAt time.Time
    updatedAt time.Time
}

func NewMarket(input CreateMarketInput, now time.Time) (*Market, error) {
    if strings.TrimSpace(string(input.ID)) == "" {
        return nil, ErrInvalidMarketID
    }

    code := normalizeMarketCode(input.Code)
    if !isValidMarketCode(code) {
        return nil, ErrInvalidMarketCode
    }

    name := strings.TrimSpace(input.Name)
    if name == "" || len([]rune(name)) > 160 {
        return nil, ErrInvalidMarketName
    }

    if now.IsZero() {
        return nil, ErrInvalidTimestamp
    }

    timezone := strings.TrimSpace(input.Timezone)
    if timezone == "" {
        timezone = "UTC"
    }

    if _, err := time.LoadLocation(timezone); err != nil {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: %s", ErrInvalidMarketTimezone, timezone)
    }

    return &Market{
        id:          input.ID,
        code:        code,
        name:        name,
        description: strings.TrimSpace(input.Description),
        timezone:    timezone,
        status:      MarketStatusDraft,
        version:     1,
        createdAt:   now.UTC(),
        updatedAt:   now.UTC(),
    }, nil
}

func normalizeMarketCode(value string) string {
    return strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(value))
}

func isValidMarketCode(value string) bool {
    if len(value) < 2 || len(value) > 32 {
        return false
    }

    for _, r := range value {
        valid := (r >= 'A' && r <= 'Z') ||
            (r >= '0' && r <= '9') ||
            r == '-' ||
            r == '_'

        if !valid {
            return false
        }
    }

    return true
}

func (m *Market) ID() MarketID {
    return m.id
}

func (m *Market) Code() string {
    return m.code
}

func (m *Market) Name() string {
    return m.name
}

func (m *Market) Description() string {
    return m.description
}

func (m *Market) Timezone() string {
    return m.timezone
}

func (m *Market) Status() MarketStatus {
    return m.status
}

func (m *Market) Version() uint64 {
    return m.version
}

func (m *Market) CreatedAt() time.Time {
    return m.createdAt
}

func (m *Market) UpdatedAt() time.Time {
    return m.updatedAt
}

func (m *Market) UpdateDetails(
    name string,
    description string,
    timezone string,
    now time.Time,
) error {
    if m.status == MarketStatusClosed {
        return ErrMarketClosed
    }

    name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
    if name == "" || len([]rune(name)) > 160 {
        return ErrInvalidMarketName
    }

    timezone = strings.TrimSpace(timezone)
    if timezone == "" {
        timezone = "UTC"
    }

    if _, err := time.LoadLocation(timezone); err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("%w: %s", ErrInvalidMarketTimezone, timezone)
    }

    if err := m.validateTimestamp(now); err != nil {
        return err
    }

    m.name = name
    m.description = strings.TrimSpace(description)
    m.timezone = timezone
    m.touch(now)

    return nil
}

func (m *Market) Activate(now time.Time) error {
    switch m.status {
    case MarketStatusDraft, MarketStatusSuspended:
        if err := m.validateTimestamp(now); err != nil {
            return err
        }

        m.status = MarketStatusActive
        m.touch(now)
        return nil

    case MarketStatusActive:
        return ErrInvalidMarketState

    case MarketStatusClosed:
        return ErrMarketClosed

    default:
        return ErrInvalidMarketState
    }
}

func (m *Market) Suspend(now time.Time) error {
    if m.status != MarketStatusActive {
        return ErrInvalidMarketState
    }

    if err := m.validateTimestamp(now); err != nil {
        return err
    }

    m.status = MarketStatusSuspended
    m.touch(now)

    return nil
}

func (m *Market) Close(now time.Time) error {
    if m.status == MarketStatusClosed {
        return ErrInvalidMarketState
    }

    if err := m.validateTimestamp(now); err != nil {
        return err
    }

    m.status = MarketStatusClosed
    m.touch(now)

    return nil
}

func (m *Market) IsOperational() bool {
    return m.status == MarketStatusActive
}

func (m *Market) validateTimestamp(now time.Time) error {
    if now.IsZero() {
        return ErrInvalidTimestamp
    }

    if now.Before(m.updatedAt) {
        return ErrInvalidTimestamp
    }

    return nil
}

func (m *Market) touch(now time.Time) {
    m.updatedAt = now.UTC()
    m.version++
}
'@

$MarketTest = @'
package domain

import (
    "errors"
    "testing"
    "time"
)

func testMarket(t *testing.T) *Market {
    t.Helper()

    now := time.Date(2026, 11, 4, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)

    market, err := NewMarket(CreateMarketInput{
        ID:          MarketID("market-001"),
        Code:        "general-market",
        Name:        "General Market",
        Description: "Main general market",
        Timezone:    "UTC",
    }, now)

    if err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("NewMarket() error = %v", err)
    }

    return market
}

func TestNewMarket(t *testing.T) {
    market := testMarket(t)

    if market.Status() != MarketStatusDraft {
        t.Fatalf("expected draft status, got %s", market.Status())
    }

    if market.Code() != "GENERAL-MARKET" {
        t.Fatalf("unexpected normalized code: %s", market.Code())
    }

    if market.Version() != 1 {
        t.Fatalf("expected version 1, got %d", market.Version())
    }
}

func TestNewMarketRejectsInvalidInput(t *testing.T) {
    now := time.Now().UTC()

    tests := []struct {
        name  string
        input CreateMarketInput
        want  error
    }{
        {
            name: "missing id",
            input: CreateMarketInput{
                Code: "MKT",
                Name: "Market",
            },
            want: ErrInvalidMarketID,
        },
        {
            name: "invalid code",
            input: CreateMarketInput{
                ID:   "m-1",
                Code: "invalid code!",
                Name: "Market",
            },
            want: ErrInvalidMarketCode,
        },
        {
            name: "missing name",
            input: CreateMarketInput{
                ID:   "m-1",
                Code: "MKT",
                Name: " ",
            },
            want: ErrInvalidMarketName,
        },
        {
            name: "invalid timezone",
            input: CreateMarketInput{
                ID:       "m-1",
                Code:     "MKT",
                Name:     "Market",
                Timezone: "Not/A/Timezone",
            },
            want: ErrInvalidMarketTimezone,
        },
    }

    for _, tt := range tests {
        t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
            _, err := NewMarket(tt.input, now)

            if !errors.Is(err, tt.want) {
                t.Fatalf("expected error %v, got %v", tt.want, err)
            }
        })
    }
}

func TestMarketLifecycle(t *testing.T) {
    market := testMarket(t)
    base := market.UpdatedAt()

    if err := market.Activate(base.Add(time.Minute)); err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("Activate() error = %v", err)
    }

    if !market.IsOperational() {
        t.Fatal("market should be operational")
    }

    if err := market.Suspend(base.Add(2 * time.Minute)); err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("Suspend() error = %v", err)
    }

    if market.Status() != MarketStatusSuspended {
        t.Fatalf("unexpected status: %s", market.Status())
    }

    if err := market.Activate(base.Add(3 * time.Minute)); err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("reactivation error = %v", err)
    }

    if err := market.Close(base.Add(4 * time.Minute)); err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("Close() error = %v", err)
    }

    if market.Status() != MarketStatusClosed {
        t.Fatalf("expected closed status, got %s", market.Status())
    }

    if err := market.Activate(base.Add(5 * time.Minute)); !errors.Is(err, ErrMarketClosed) {
        t.Fatalf("expected ErrMarketClosed, got %v", err)
    }
}

func TestMarketRejectsInvalidTransitions(t *testing.T) {
    market := testMarket(t)
    now := market.UpdatedAt()

    if err := market.Suspend(now.Add(time.Minute)); !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidMarketState) {
        t.Fatalf("expected invalid state error, got %v", err)
    }

    if err := market.Activate(now.Add(time.Minute)); err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("Activate() error = %v", err)
    }

    if err := market.Activate(now.Add(2 * time.Minute)); !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidMarketState) {
        t.Fatalf("expected invalid state error, got %v", err)
    }
}

func TestMarketRejectsTimeRegression(t *testing.T) {
    market := testMarket(t)

    err := market.Activate(market.CreatedAt().Add(-time.Minute))

    if !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidTimestamp) {
        t.Fatalf("expected ErrInvalidTimestamp, got %v", err)
    }
}

func TestMarketVersionIncrementsOnMutation(t *testing.T) {
    market := testMarket(t)
    initialVersion := market.Version()
    now := market.UpdatedAt()

    if err := market.UpdateDetails(
        "Updated Market",
        "Updated description",
        "UTC",
        now.Add(time.Minute),
    ); err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("UpdateDetails() error = %v", err)
    }

    if market.Version() != initialVersion+1 {
        t.Fatalf("expected version %d, got %d", initialVersion+1, market.Version())
    }
}
'@

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Catalogue category domain model
# ------------------------------------------------------------

$CategoryGo = @'
package domain

import (
    "errors"
    "strings"
    "time"
)

var (
    ErrInvalidCategoryID       = errors.New("invalid category id")
    ErrInvalidCategoryCode     = errors.New("invalid category code")
    ErrInvalidCategoryName     = errors.New("invalid category name")
    ErrInvalidCategoryKind     = errors.New("invalid category kind")
    ErrInvalidCategoryStatus   = errors.New("invalid category status")
    ErrInvalidParentCategory   = errors.New("invalid parent category")
    ErrCategoryArchived        = errors.New("category is archived")
    ErrInvalidCategoryMutation = errors.New("invalid category mutation")
)

type CategoryID string

type CategoryKind string

type CategoryStatus string

const (
    CategoryKindGeneral    CategoryKind = "general"
    CategoryKindSpecialized CategoryKind = "specialized"
)

const (
    CategoryStatusDraft    CategoryStatus = "draft"
    CategoryStatusActive   CategoryStatus = "active"
    CategoryStatusArchived CategoryStatus = "archived"
)

func (k CategoryKind) IsValid() bool {
    return k == CategoryKindGeneral || k == CategoryKindSpecialized
}

func (s CategoryStatus) IsValid() bool {
    return s == CategoryStatusDraft ||
        s == CategoryStatusActive ||
        s == CategoryStatusArchived
}

type CreateCategoryInput struct {
    ID       CategoryID
    Code     string
    Name     string
    Kind     CategoryKind
    ParentID CategoryID
    SortOrder int
}

type Category struct {
    id        CategoryID
    code      string
    name      string
    kind      CategoryKind
    parentID  CategoryID
    sortOrder int

    status    CategoryStatus
    version   uint64
    createdAt time.Time
    updatedAt time.Time
}

func NewCategory(input CreateCategoryInput, now time.Time) (*Category, error) {
    if strings.TrimSpace(string(input.ID)) == "" {
        return nil, ErrInvalidCategoryID
    }

    code := normalizeCategoryCode(input.Code)
    if !isValidCategoryCode(code) {
        return nil, ErrInvalidCategoryCode
    }

    name := strings.TrimSpace(input.Name)
    if name == "" || len([]rune(name)) > 160 {
        return nil, ErrInvalidCategoryName
    }

    if !input.Kind.IsValid() {
        return nil, ErrInvalidCategoryKind
    }

    if input.ParentID != "" && input.ParentID == input.ID {
        return nil, ErrInvalidParentCategory
    }

    if now.IsZero() {
        return nil, ErrInvalidTimestamp
    }

    return &Category{
        id:        input.ID,
        code:      code,
        name:      name,
        kind:      input.Kind,
        parentID:  input.ParentID,
        sortOrder: input.SortOrder,
        status:    CategoryStatusDraft,
        version:   1,
        createdAt: now.UTC(),
        updatedAt: now.UTC(),
    }, nil
}

func normalizeCategoryCode(value string) string {
    return strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(value))
}

func isValidCategoryCode(value string) bool {
    if len(value) < 2 || len(value) > 64 {
        return false
    }

    for _, r := range value {
        valid := (r >= 'A' && r <= 'Z') ||
            (r >= '0' && r <= '9') ||
            r == '-' ||
            r == '_' ||
            r == '.'

        if !valid {
            return false
        }
    }

    return true
}

func (c *Category) ID() CategoryID {
    return c.id
}

func (c *Category) Code() string {
    return c.code
}

func (c *Category) Name() string {
    return c.name
}

func (c *Category) Kind() CategoryKind {
    return c.kind
}

func (c *Category) ParentID() CategoryID {
    return c.parentID
}

func (c *Category) SortOrder() int {
    return c.sortOrder
}

func (c *Category) Status() CategoryStatus {
    return c.status
}

func (c *Category) Version() uint64 {
    return c.version
}

func (c *Category) CreatedAt() time.Time {
    return c.createdAt
}

func (c *Category) UpdatedAt() time.Time {
    return c.updatedAt
}

func (c *Category) Rename(name string, now time.Time) error {
    if c.status == CategoryStatusArchived {
        return ErrCategoryArchived
    }

    name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
    if name == "" || len([]rune(name)) > 160 {
        return ErrInvalidCategoryName
    }

    if err := c.validateTimestamp(now); err != nil {
        return err
    }

    if name == c.name {
        return nil
    }

    c.name = name
    c.touch(now)

    return nil
}

func (c *Category) ChangeParent(parentID CategoryID, now time.Time) error {
    if c.status == CategoryStatusArchived {
        return ErrCategoryArchived
    }

    if parentID != "" && parentID == c.id {
        return ErrInvalidParentCategory
    }

    if err := c.validateTimestamp(now); err != nil {
        return err
    }

    if parentID == c.parentID {
        return nil
    }

    c.parentID = parentID
    c.touch(now)

    return nil
}

func (c *Category) ChangeSortOrder(sortOrder int, now time.Time) error {
    if c.status == CategoryStatusArchived {
        return ErrCategoryArchived
    }

    if err := c.validateTimestamp(now); err != nil {
        return err
    }

    if sortOrder == c.sortOrder {
        return nil
    }

    c.sortOrder = sortOrder
    c.touch(now)

    return nil
}

func (c *Category) Activate(now time.Time) error {
    switch c.status {
    case CategoryStatusDraft:
        if err := c.validateTimestamp(now); err != nil {
            return err
        }

        c.status = CategoryStatusActive
        c.touch(now)
        return nil

    case CategoryStatusActive:
        return ErrInvalidCategoryMutation

    case CategoryStatusArchived:
        return ErrCategoryArchived

    default:
        return ErrInvalidCategoryStatus
    }
}

func (c *Category) Archive(now time.Time) error {
    if c.status == CategoryStatusArchived {
        return ErrInvalidCategoryMutation
    }

    if err := c.validateTimestamp(now); err != nil {
        return err
    }

    c.status = CategoryStatusArchived
    c.touch(now)

    return nil
}

func (c *Category) IsSelectable() bool {
    return c.status == CategoryStatusActive
}

func (c *Category) validateTimestamp(now time.Time) error {
    if now.IsZero() || now.Before(c.updatedAt) {
        return ErrInvalidTimestamp
    }

    return nil
}

func (c *Category) touch(now time.Time) {
    c.updatedAt = now.UTC()
    c.version++
}
'@

$CategoryTest = @'
package domain

import (
    "errors"
    "testing"
    "time"
)

func newTestCategory(
    t *testing.T,
    id CategoryID,
    code string,
    kind CategoryKind,
) *Category {
    t.Helper()

    now := time.Date(2026, 11, 4, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)

    category, err := NewCategory(CreateCategoryInput{
        ID:        id,
        Code:      code,
        Name:      "Test Category",
        Kind:      kind,
        SortOrder: 10,
    }, now)

    if err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("NewCategory() error = %v", err)
    }

    return category
}

func TestNewCategory(t *testing.T) {
    category := newTestCategory(
        t,
        "category-001",
        "general.goods",
        CategoryKindGeneral,
    )

    if category.Status() != CategoryStatusDraft {
        t.Fatalf("expected draft, got %s", category.Status())
    }

    if category.Code() != "GENERAL.GOODS" {
        t.Fatalf("unexpected code: %s", category.Code())
    }

    if category.Version() != 1 {
        t.Fatalf("expected version 1, got %d", category.Version())
    }
}

func TestNewCategoryRejectsInvalidInput(t *testing.T) {
    now := time.Now().UTC()

    tests := []struct {
        name  string
        input CreateCategoryInput
        want  error
    }{
        {
            name: "missing id",
            input: CreateCategoryInput{
                Code: "CAT",
                Name: "Category",
                Kind: CategoryKindGeneral,
            },
            want: ErrInvalidCategoryID,
        },
        {
            name: "invalid code",
            input: CreateCategoryInput{
                ID:   "cat-1",
                Code: "bad code",
                Name: "Category",
                Kind: CategoryKindGeneral,
            },
            want: ErrInvalidCategoryCode,
        },
        {
            name: "invalid kind",
            input: CreateCategoryInput{
                ID:   "cat-1",
                Code: "CAT",
                Name: "Category",
                Kind: "unknown",
            },
            want: ErrInvalidCategoryKind,
        },
        {
            name: "self parent",
            input: CreateCategoryInput{
                ID:       "cat-1",
                Code:     "CAT",
                Name:     "Category",
                Kind:     CategoryKindGeneral,
                ParentID: "cat-1",
            },
            want: ErrInvalidParentCategory,
        },
    }

    for _, tt := range tests {
        t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
            _, err := NewCategory(tt.input, now)

            if !errors.Is(err, tt.want) {
                t.Fatalf("expected %v, got %v", tt.want, err)
            }
        })
    }
}

func TestCategoryLifecycle(t *testing.T) {
    category := newTestCategory(
        t,
        "cat-1",
        "CAT",
        CategoryKindGeneral,
    )

    base := category.UpdatedAt()

    if err := category.Activate(base.Add(time.Minute)); err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("Activate() error = %v", err)
    }

    if !category.IsSelectable() {
        t.Fatal("active category should be selectable")
    }

    if err := category.Archive(base.Add(2 * time.Minute)); err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("Archive() error = %v", err)
    }

    if category.IsSelectable() {
        t.Fatal("archived category must not be selectable")
    }

    if err := category.Activate(base.Add(3 * time.Minute)); !errors.Is(err, ErrCategoryArchived) {
        t.Fatalf("expected ErrCategoryArchived, got %v", err)
    }
}

func TestCategoryMutation(t *testing.T) {
    category := newTestCategory(
        t,
        "cat-1",
        "CAT",
        CategoryKindGeneral,
    )

    base := category.UpdatedAt()

    if err := category.Rename("New Name", base.Add(time.Minute)); err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("Rename() error = %v", err)
    }

    if category.Name() != "New Name" {
        t.Fatalf("unexpected name: %s", category.Name())
    }

    if err := category.ChangeParent("parent-1", base.Add(2*time.Minute)); err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("ChangeParent() error = %v", err)
    }

    if category.ParentID() != "parent-1" {
        t.Fatalf("unexpected parent: %s", category.ParentID())
    }

    if err := category.ChangeSortOrder(20, base.Add(3*time.Minute)); err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("ChangeSortOrder() error = %v", err)
    }

    if category.SortOrder() != 20 {
        t.Fatalf("unexpected sort order: %d", category.SortOrder())
    }

    if category.Version() != 4 {
        t.Fatalf("expected version 4, got %d", category.Version())
    }
}

func TestCategoryRejectsSelfParent(t *testing.T) {
    category := newTestCategory(
        t,
        "cat-1",
        "CAT",
        CategoryKindGeneral,
    )

    err := category.ChangeParent("cat-1", category.UpdatedAt().Add(time.Minute))

    if !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidParentCategory) {
        t.Fatalf("expected ErrInvalidParentCategory, got %v", err)
    }
}

func TestCategoryRejectsMutationAfterArchive(t *testing.T) {
    category := newTestCategory(
        t,
        "cat-1",
        "CAT",
        CategoryKindGeneral,
    )

    base := category.UpdatedAt()

    if err := category.Archive(base.Add(time.Minute)); err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("Archive() error = %v", err)
    }

    if err := category.Rename("Forbidden", base.Add(2*time.Minute)); !errors.Is(err, ErrCategoryArchived) {
        t.Fatalf("expected ErrCategoryArchived, got %v", err)
    }
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Mapping between public and specialized categories
# ------------------------------------------------------------

$CategoryMappingGo = @'
package domain

import (
    "errors"
    "strings"
    "time"
)

var (
    ErrInvalidCategoryMappingID = errors.New("invalid category mapping id")
    ErrInvalidCategoryMapping   = errors.New("invalid category mapping")
    ErrCategoryMappingArchived  = errors.New("category mapping is archived")
    ErrCategoryMappingState     = errors.New("invalid category mapping state")
    ErrCategoryKindsMismatch    = errors.New("category kinds do not match mapping direction")
    ErrCategoryNotActive        = errors.New("both categories must be active")
)

type CategoryMappingID string

type CategoryMappingStatus string

const (
    CategoryMappingStatusDraft    CategoryMappingStatus = "draft"
    CategoryMappingStatusActive   CategoryMappingStatus = "active"
    CategoryMappingStatusArchived CategoryMappingStatus = "archived"
)

type CreateCategoryMappingInput struct {
    ID                    CategoryMappingID
    PublicCategoryID      CategoryID
    SpecializedCategoryID CategoryID
    MarketID              string
    Description           string
}

type CategoryMapping struct {
    id                    CategoryMappingID
    publicCategoryID      CategoryID
    specializedCategoryID CategoryID
    marketID              string
    description           string

    status    CategoryMappingStatus
    version   uint64
    createdAt time.Time
    updatedAt time.Time
}

func NewCategoryMapping(
    input CreateCategoryMappingInput,
    now time.Time,
) (*CategoryMapping, error) {
    if strings.TrimSpace(string(input.ID)) == "" {
        return nil, ErrInvalidCategoryMappingID
    }

    if strings.TrimSpace(string(input.PublicCategoryID)) == "" ||
        strings.TrimSpace(string(input.SpecializedCategoryID)) == "" ||
        input.PublicCategoryID == input.SpecializedCategoryID {
        return nil, ErrInvalidCategoryMapping
    }

    if now.IsZero() {
        return nil, ErrInvalidTimestamp
    }

    return &CategoryMapping{
        id:                    input.ID,
        publicCategoryID:      input.PublicCategoryID,
        specializedCategoryID: input.SpecializedCategoryID,
        marketID:              strings.TrimSpace(input.MarketID),
        description:           strings.TrimSpace(input.Description),
        status:                CategoryMappingStatusDraft,
        version:               1,
        createdAt:             now.UTC(),
        updatedAt:             now.UTC(),
    }, nil
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) ID() CategoryMappingID {
    return m.id
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) PublicCategoryID() CategoryID {
    return m.publicCategoryID
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) SpecializedCategoryID() CategoryID {
    return m.specializedCategoryID
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) MarketID() string {
    return m.marketID
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) Description() string {
    return m.description
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) Status() CategoryMappingStatus {
    return m.status
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) Version() uint64 {
    return m.version
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) CreatedAt() time.Time {
    return m.createdAt
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) UpdatedAt() time.Time {
    return m.updatedAt
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) Activate(
    publicCategory *Category,
    specializedCategory *Category,
    now time.Time,
) error {
    if m.status == CategoryMappingStatusArchived {
        return ErrCategoryMappingArchived
    }

    if m.status != CategoryMappingStatusDraft {
        return ErrCategoryMappingState
    }

    if publicCategory == nil || specializedCategory == nil {
        return ErrInvalidCategoryMapping
    }

    if publicCategory.ID() != m.publicCategoryID ||
        specializedCategory.ID() != m.specializedCategoryID {
        return ErrInvalidCategoryMapping
    }

    if publicCategory.Kind() != CategoryKindGeneral ||
        specializedCategory.Kind() != CategoryKindSpecialized {
        return ErrCategoryKindsMismatch
    }

    if !publicCategory.IsSelectable() || !specializedCategory.IsSelectable() {
        return ErrCategoryNotActive
    }

    if now.IsZero() || now.Before(m.updatedAt) {
        return ErrInvalidTimestamp
    }

    m.status = CategoryMappingStatusActive
    m.touch(now)

    return nil
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) Archive(now time.Time) error {
    if m.status == CategoryMappingStatusArchived {
        return ErrCategoryMappingState
    }

    if now.IsZero() || now.Before(m.updatedAt) {
        return ErrInvalidTimestamp
    }

    m.status = CategoryMappingStatusArchived
    m.touch(now)

    return nil
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) IsUsable() bool {
    return m.status == CategoryMappingStatusActive
}

func (m *CategoryMapping) touch(now time.Time) {
    m.updatedAt = now.UTC()
    m.version++
}
'@

$CategoryMappingTest = @'
package domain

import (
    "errors"
    "testing"
    "time"
)

func createMappingTestCategories(t *testing.T) (*Category, *Category, time.Time) {
    t.Helper()

    now := time.Date(2026, 11, 4, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)

    publicCategory, err := NewCategory(CreateCategoryInput{
        ID:   "public-1",
        Code: "PUBLIC",
        Name: "Public Category",
        Kind: CategoryKindGeneral,
    }, now)

    if err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("create public category: %v", err)
    }

    specializedCategory, err := NewCategory(CreateCategoryInput{
        ID:   "special-1",
        Code: "SPECIAL",
        Name: "Specialized Category",
        Kind: CategoryKindSpecialized,
    }, now)

    if err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("create specialized category: %v", err)
    }

    if err := publicCategory.Activate(now.Add(time.Minute)); err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("activate public category: %v", err)
    }

    if err := specializedCategory.Activate(now.Add(time.Minute)); err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("activate specialized category: %v", err)
    }

    return publicCategory, specializedCategory, now
}

func TestNewCategoryMapping(t *testing.T) {
    now := time.Now().UTC()

    mapping, err := NewCategoryMapping(CreateCategoryMappingInput{
        ID:                    "mapping-1",
        PublicCategoryID:      "public-1",
        SpecializedCategoryID: "special-1",
        MarketID:              "market-1",
        Description:           "Public to specialized mapping",
    }, now)

    if err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("NewCategoryMapping() error = %v", err)
    }

    if mapping.Status() != CategoryMappingStatusDraft {
        t.Fatalf("expected draft status, got %s", mapping.Status())
    }

    if mapping.Version() != 1 {
        t.Fatalf("expected version 1, got %d", mapping.Version())
    }
}

func TestNewCategoryMappingRejectsSameCategory(t *testing.T) {
    _, err := NewCategoryMapping(CreateCategoryMappingInput{
        ID:                    "mapping-1",
        PublicCategoryID:      "same",
        SpecializedCategoryID: "same",
    }, time.Now().UTC())

    if !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidCategoryMapping) {
        t.Fatalf("expected ErrInvalidCategoryMapping, got %v", err)
    }
}

func TestCategoryMappingActivation(t *testing.T) {
    publicCategory, specializedCategory, now := createMappingTestCategories(t)

    mapping, err := NewCategoryMapping(CreateCategoryMappingInput{
        ID:                    "mapping-1",
        PublicCategoryID:      publicCategory.ID(),
        SpecializedCategoryID: specializedCategory.ID(),
        MarketID:              "market-1",
    }, now.Add(2*time.Minute))

    if err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("NewCategoryMapping() error = %v", err)
    }

    activationTime := now.Add(3 * time.Minute)

    if err := mapping.Activate(publicCategory, specializedCategory, activationTime); err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("Activate() error = %v", err)
    }

    if !mapping.IsUsable() {
        t.Fatal("mapping should be usable")
    }

    if mapping.Status() != CategoryMappingStatusActive {
        t.Fatalf("unexpected status: %s", mapping.Status())
    }

    if mapping.Version() != 2 {
        t.Fatalf("expected version 2, got %d", mapping.Version())
    }
}

func TestCategoryMappingRejectsWrongKinds(t *testing.T) {
    publicCategory, specializedCategory, now := createMappingTestCategories(t)

    mapping, err := NewCategoryMapping(CreateCategoryMappingInput{
        ID:                    "mapping-1",
        PublicCategoryID:      publicCategory.ID(),
        SpecializedCategoryID: specializedCategory.ID(),
    }, now.Add(2*time.Minute))

    if err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("NewCategoryMapping() error = %v", err)
    }

    err = mapping.Activate(specializedCategory, publicCategory, now.Add(3*time.Minute))

    if !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidCategoryMapping) {
        // The IDs are deliberately reversed, so the mapping identity check
        // must reject the operation before category-kind validation.
        t.Fatalf("expected ErrInvalidCategoryMapping, got %v", err)
    }
}

func TestCategoryMappingRequiresActiveCategories(t *testing.T) {
    now := time.Now().UTC()

    publicCategory, err := NewCategory(CreateCategoryInput{
        ID:   "public-1",
        Code: "PUBLIC",
        Name: "Public",
        Kind: CategoryKindGeneral,
    }, now)

    if err != nil {
        t.Fatal(err)
    }

    specializedCategory, err := NewCategory(CreateCategoryInput{
        ID:   "special-1",
        Code: "SPECIAL",
        Name: "Specialized",
        Kind: CategoryKindSpecialized,
    }, now)

    if err != nil {
        t.Fatal(err)
    }

    mapping, err := NewCategoryMapping(CreateCategoryMappingInput{
        ID:                    "mapping-1",
        PublicCategoryID:      publicCategory.ID(),
        SpecializedCategoryID: specializedCategory.ID(),
    }, now.Add(time.Minute))

    if err != nil {
        t.Fatal(err)
    }

    err = mapping.Activate(publicCategory, specializedCategory, now.Add(2*time.Minute))

    if !errors.Is(err, ErrCategoryNotActive) {
        t.Fatalf("expected ErrCategoryNotActive, got %v", err)
    }
}

func TestCategoryMappingArchive(t *testing.T) {
    publicCategory, specializedCategory, now := createMappingTestCategories(t)

    mapping, err := NewCategoryMapping(CreateCategoryMappingInput{
        ID:                    "mapping-1",
        PublicCategoryID:      publicCategory.ID(),
        SpecializedCategoryID: specializedCategory.ID(),
    }, now.Add(2*time.Minute))

    if err != nil {
        t.Fatal(err)
    }

    if err := mapping.Activate(publicCategory, specializedCategory, now.Add(3*time.Minute)); err != nil {
        t.Fatal(err)
    }

    if err := mapping.Archive(now.Add(4 * time.Minute)); err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("Archive() error = %v", err)
    }

    if mapping.IsUsable() {
        t.Fatal("archived mapping must not be usable")
    }

    if err := mapping.Activate(publicCategory, specializedCategory, now.Add(5*time.Minute)); !errors.Is(err, ErrCategoryMappingArchived) {
        t.Fatalf("expected ErrCategoryMappingArchived, got %v", err)
    }
}

    Write-Info "Running market domain tests..."
    & go test -count=1 -v "./$MarketRelative"

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        Stop-Script "آزمون‌های market/domain ناموفق بودند."
    }

    Write-Success "آزمون‌های market/domain موفق بودند."

    Write-Info "Running catalogue domain tests..."
    & go test -count=1 -v "./$CatalogueRelative"

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        Stop-Script "آزمون‌های catalogue/domain ناموفق بودند."
    }

    Write-Success "آزمون‌های catalogue/domain موفق بودند."

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 8. Run full project tests
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if (-not $SkipFullTest) {
        Write-Section "5. اجرای آزمون کامل پروژه"

        & go test ./...

        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            Write-WarningMessage "آزمون کامل پروژه شکست خورد. این شکست ممکن است مربوط به بخش‌های قبلی باشد."
            Write-WarningMessage "نتیجه آزمون‌های هدفمند دو دامنه را جداگانه بررسی کنید."
        }
        else {
            Write-Success "تمام آزمون‌های پروژه موفق بودند."
        }
    }
    else {
        Write-WarningMessage "آزمون کامل پروژه با درخواست کاربر اجرا نشد."
    }
}
finally {
    Pop-Location
}

# General Market Backend - Architecture Map

## Purpose

The General Market backend coordinates offers, supply requests, orders,
matching, trades, and trade execution workflows. It does not replace the
independent systems that own identity, balances, contracts, services,
ownership records, or financial settlement.

## Logical tree

general-market-backend/
â”œâ”€â”€ cmd/server/                  Application entry point
â”œâ”€â”€ config/                      Environment configuration
â”œâ”€â”€ internal/
â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ access/                  Access boundary and permission checks
â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ catalogue/               Public catalogue, search, filters, pagination
â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ market/                  Market definitions and lifecycle
â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ offerconfig/             Versioned parametric configuration
â”‚   â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ classifications/
â”‚   â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ parameters/
â”‚   â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ rules/
â”‚   â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ calculations/
â”‚   â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ documents/
â”‚   â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ qualification/
â”‚   â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ approvals/
â”‚   â”‚   â””â”€â”€ versions/
â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ eligibility/             Offer and operation eligibility
â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ offers/                  Offer lifecycle
â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ supplyrequests/          Supply requests and proposals
â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ orders/                  Order lifecycle and order book
â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ matching/                Deterministic order matching
â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ trades/                  Trade records and references
â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ tradestates/             Explicit trade-related state machines
â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ execution/               Versioned execution policies

â”‚   â”œâ”€â”€ redis/                   Optional; deferred until justified
â”‚   â””â”€â”€ messaging/               Optional; deferred until justified
â”œâ”€â”€ migrations/                  Versioned database migrations

â”œâ”€â”€ scripts/                     Development and maintenance scripts
â””â”€â”€ docs/architecture/           Architecture documentation

## Systems of record

- Account: identity, authentication context, roles, and permissions.
- My Assets: free balance, buying power, reservation/release, asset positions.
- Contracts: legal contracts and obligations.
- Services: transport, storage, inspection, insurance, installation, delivery.
- Settlement: authoritative financial settlement execution and result.
- Official institutions: authoritative legal registration or official records,
  where applicable.
- General Market: market workflow, offers, orders, matching, trades, references,
  execution policy, and coordination states.

## Mandatory design constraints

1. The client is not authoritative for permissions, buying power, matching,
   or state transitions.
2. Financial and asset balances must not be independently duplicated here.
3. External operations require explicit contracts, timeouts, error handling,
   correlation identifiers, and safe retry behavior.
4. Concurrent order and matching operations require transactional safeguards
   and idempotency.
5. Published configuration and execution policy versions must be traceable.
6. Important transitions must be auditable without copying complete external
   records unnecessarily.
7. Database migrations must be versioned, reviewable, and non-destructive by
   default. Never rebuild or erase an existing database automatically.
8. Redis and message brokers are optional; introduce them only after a
   measured technical requirement.
9. A directory or README is not a completed feature. Each module needs code,
   API contracts where applicable, automated tests, and acceptance checks.
10. Infrastructure adapters must have a single clear location. If an older
    implementation exists under internal/database, review imports and behavior
    before consolidating or moving it to infrastructure/postgres.

## Implementation order

1. Inspect current repository, Go packages, migrations, configuration, and API.
2. Stabilize application bootstrap, PostgreSQL access, errors, logging, health,
   and test harness.
3. Implement catalogue and market/offer configuration.
4. Implement offers and eligibility.
5. Implement supply requests, orders, reservations, and matching.
6. Implement trades, state machines, execution policies, and workflows.
7. Add integrations, commodity aggregation, real-asset workflows, and audit
   coverage as required by the detailed specification.
8. Verify concurrency, security, performance, migrations, and recovery.

This document defines logical responsibilities, not separate deployable
services. Go package boundaries must remain simple and avoid circular imports.

-- General Market Backend
-- Migration: 000002_market_domain_foundation.sql
-- Additive, versioned and non-destructive.
--
-- Ownership boundaries:
-- * No account credentials or authoritative role database.
-- * No free-balance ledger or authoritative reservation ledger.
-- * No official property ownership register.
-- * No legal contract body owned by Contracts service.
-- * No service-delivery records owned by Services.
-- * No final settlement ledger owned by Settlement.
--
-- External identifiers are stored as references, not copied records.

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS markets (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    code VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
    allowed_trade_methods JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
    display_rules JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_by_user_id VARCHAR(160),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'active', 'paused', 'closed'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public_categories (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    parent_id UUID REFERENCES public_categories(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    code VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    sort_order INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS specialized_classifications (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    code VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    name VARCHAR(240) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    domain_code VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (version > 0)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS category_mappings (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    public_category_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public_categories(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    specialized_classification_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES specialized_classifications(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    market_id UUID REFERENCES markets(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    valid_from TIMESTAMPTZ,
    valid_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    UNIQUE (public_category_id, specialized_classification_id, market_id),
    CHECK (valid_until IS NULL OR valid_from IS NULL OR valid_until > valid_from)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS parameter_definitions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    classification_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES specialized_classifications(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    code VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(240) NOT NULL,
    data_type VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL,
    unit_code VARCHAR(60),
    is_required BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    validation_schema JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    sort_order INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    UNIQUE (classification_id, code, version),
    CHECK (data_type IN ('string', 'integer', 'decimal', 'boolean', 'date', 'datetime', 'enum', 'object', 'array')),
    CHECK (version > 0)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS offer_configurations (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    classification_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES specialized_classifications(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    code VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    name VARCHAR(240) NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
    current_version INTEGER,
    created_by_user_id VARCHAR(160),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'active', 'retired'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS offer_config_versions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    configuration_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES offer_configurations(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    version INTEGER NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
    rules_schema JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    calculation_schema JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    qualification_schema JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    document_schema JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_by_user_id VARCHAR(160),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    published_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    UNIQUE (configuration_id, version),
    CHECK (version > 0),
    CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'approved', 'published', 'retired'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS offer_config_parameters (
    configuration_version_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES offer_config_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    parameter_definition_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES parameter_definitions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    is_required BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    overrides JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    PRIMARY KEY (configuration_version_id, parameter_definition_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS offer_rules (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    configuration_version_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES offer_config_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    code VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
    rule_type VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
    definition JSONB NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    UNIQUE (configuration_version_id, code)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS offer_documents (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    offer_id UUID,
    configuration_version_id UUID REFERENCES offer_config_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    document_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    external_document_ref VARCHAR(240),
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'required',
    metadata JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('required', 'submitted', 'verified', 'rejected', 'expired'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS company_qualifications (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    company_ref VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    classification_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES specialized_classifications(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    external_verification_ref VARCHAR(240),
    valid_from TIMESTAMPTZ,
    valid_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
    metadata JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('pending', 'approved', 'rejected', 'suspended', 'expired'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS offers (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    public_code VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    market_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES markets(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    public_category_id UUID REFERENCES public_categories(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    classification_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES specialized_classifications(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    configuration_version_id UUID REFERENCES offer_config_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    owner_ref VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    title VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    summary TEXT,
    offer_type VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'sale',
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
    price_amount NUMERIC(24,8),
    currency_code VARCHAR(12),
    quantity_total NUMERIC(24,8),
    quantity_unit VARCHAR(60),
    attributes JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    valid_from TIMESTAMPTZ,
    valid_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
    submitted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    published_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    closed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_by_user_id VARCHAR(160),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'pending_review', 'approved', 'published', 'suspended', 'rejected', 'expired', 'closed')),
    CHECK (offer_type IN ('sale', 'purchase', 'service', 'physical_commodity', 'real_asset')),
    CHECK (price_amount IS NULL OR price_amount >= 0),
    CHECK (quantity_total IS NULL OR quantity_total >= 0),
    CHECK (valid_until IS NULL OR valid_from IS NULL OR valid_until > valid_from)
);

ALTER TABLE offer_documents
    ADD CONSTRAINT fk_offer_documents_offer
    FOREIGN KEY (offer_id) REFERENCES offers(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS offer_versions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    offer_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES offers(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    version INTEGER NOT NULL,
    snapshot JSONB NOT NULL,
    changed_by_user_id VARCHAR(160),
    change_reason TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    UNIQUE (offer_id, version),
    CHECK (version > 0)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS offer_approvals (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    offer_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES offers(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    configuration_version_id UUID REFERENCES offer_config_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    reviewer_ref VARCHAR(160),
    decision VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL,
    reason TEXT,
    rule_results JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    decided_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (decision IN ('approved', 'rejected', 'changes_requested'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS supply_requests (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    request_code VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    requester_ref VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    market_id UUID REFERENCES markets(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    classification_id UUID REFERENCES specialized_classifications(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    title VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    quantity NUMERIC(24,8),
    quantity_unit VARCHAR(60),
    target_price NUMERIC(24,8),
    currency_code VARCHAR(12),
    requirements JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
    response_deadline TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published', 'receiving_proposals', 'evaluating', 'awarded', 'cancelled', 'expired')),
    CHECK (quantity IS NULL OR quantity > 0),
    CHECK (target_price IS NULL OR target_price >= 0)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS supply_proposals (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    request_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES supply_requests(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    proposer_ref VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    price_amount NUMERIC(24,8),
    currency_code VARCHAR(12),
    quantity NUMERIC(24,8),
    delivery_terms JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    terms JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'submitted',
    valid_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'submitted', 'under_review', 'accepted', 'rejected', 'withdrawn', 'expired')),
    CHECK (price_amount IS NULL OR price_amount >= 0),
    CHECK (quantity IS NULL OR quantity > 0)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS orders (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    order_code VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    offer_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES offers(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    participant_ref VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    side VARCHAR(8) NOT NULL,
    order_type VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'limit',
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending_checks',
    price_amount NUMERIC(24,8),
    currency_code VARCHAR(12),
    quantity_original NUMERIC(24,8) NOT NULL,
    quantity_remaining NUMERIC(24,8) NOT NULL,
    quantity_filled NUMERIC(24,8) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    external_reservation_ref VARCHAR(240),
    idempotency_key VARCHAR(160),
    correlation_id VARCHAR(160),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    cancelled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    CHECK (side IN ('buy', 'sell')),
    CHECK (order_type IN ('limit', 'market', 'offer_directed')),
    CHECK (status IN ('pending_checks', 'pending_reservation', 'open', 'partially_filled', 'filled', 'cancelled', 'rejected', 'expired', 'failed')),
    CHECK (quantity_original > 0),
    CHECK (quantity_remaining >= 0),
    CHECK (quantity_filled >= 0),
    CHECK (quantity_remaining + quantity_filled = quantity_original),
    CHECK (price_amount IS NULL OR price_amount >= 0),
    UNIQUE (participant_ref, idempotency_key)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS order_matches (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    buy_order_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    sell_order_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    matched_quantity NUMERIC(24,8) NOT NULL,
    matched_price NUMERIC(24,8) NOT NULL,
    currency_code VARCHAR(12),
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'recorded',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (buy_order_id <> sell_order_id),
    CHECK (matched_quantity > 0),
    CHECK (matched_price >= 0),
    CHECK (status IN ('recorded', 'accepted', 'rejected', 'trade_created', 'failed'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS trades (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    trade_code VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    match_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES order_matches(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    offer_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES offers(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    buy_order_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    sell_order_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    buyer_ref VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    seller_ref VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    quantity NUMERIC(24,8) NOT NULL,
    price_amount NUMERIC(24,8) NOT NULL,
    currency_code VARCHAR(12),
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'created',
    execution_policy_version_id UUID,
    correlation_id VARCHAR(160),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (quantity > 0),
    CHECK (price_amount >= 0),
    CHECK (status IN ('created', 'in_execution', 'awaiting_contract', 'awaiting_services', 'awaiting_delivery', 'ready_for_settlement', 'settlement_pending', 'settled', 'cancelled', 'failed'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS execution_policies (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    code VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    name VARCHAR(240) NOT NULL,
    market_id UUID REFERENCES markets(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    offer_type VARCHAR(40),
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'active', 'retired'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS execution_policy_versions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    policy_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES execution_policies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    version INTEGER NOT NULL,
    steps JSONB NOT NULL,
    conditions JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    published_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    UNIQUE (policy_id, version),
    CHECK (version > 0)
);

ALTER TABLE trades
    ADD CONSTRAINT fk_trades_execution_policy_version
    FOREIGN KEY (execution_policy_version_id)
    REFERENCES execution_policy_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS trade_workflows (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    trade_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES trades(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    policy_version_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES execution_policy_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    current_step_code VARCHAR(120),
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    last_error_code VARCHAR(100),
    last_error_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    retry_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    correlation_id VARCHAR(160),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    UNIQUE (trade_id),
    CHECK (status IN ('pending', 'running', 'waiting_external', 'completed', 'failed', 'cancelled')),
    CHECK (retry_count >= 0)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS trade_state_history (
    id BIGINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    trade_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES trades(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    state_domain VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
    previous_state VARCHAR(40),
    new_state VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
    actor_ref VARCHAR(160),
    reason TEXT,
    correlation_id VARCHAR(160),
    metadata JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS integration_operations (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    service_code VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    operation_code VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
    external_ref VARCHAR(240),
    correlation_id VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    idempotency_key VARCHAR(160),
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    request_metadata JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    response_metadata JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    error_code VARCHAR(100),
    attempt_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    next_retry_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('pending', 'running', 'succeeded', 'failed', 'retry_wait', 'cancelled')),
    CHECK (attempt_count >= 0),
    UNIQUE (service_code, operation_code, idempotency_key)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS commodity_aggregation_jobs (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    job_code VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    commodity_classification_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES specialized_classifications(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    requested_quantity NUMERIC(24,8),
    quantity_unit VARCHAR(60),
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
    criteria JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    result_summary JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_by_user_id VARCHAR(160),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'evaluating', 'proposed', 'accepted', 'cancelled', 'failed')),
    CHECK (requested_quantity IS NULL OR requested_quantity > 0)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS commodity_aggregation_items (
    aggregation_job_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES commodity_aggregation_jobs(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    offer_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES offers(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    eligible_quantity NUMERIC(24,8) NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'included',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    PRIMARY KEY (aggregation_job_id, offer_id),
    CHECK (eligible_quantity > 0),
    CHECK (status IN ('included', 'excluded', 'reserved_externally', 'fulfilled'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS real_asset_workflows (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    workflow_code VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    trade_id UUID REFERENCES trades(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    offer_id UUID REFERENCES offers(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    external_asset_ref VARCHAR(240),
    external_registry_ref VARCHAR(240),
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    platform_position_ref VARCHAR(240),
    official_ownership_status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'unknown',
    metadata JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    correlation_id VARCHAR(160),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('pending', 'verification_pending', 'contract_pending', 'transfer_pending', 'completed', 'rejected', 'failed', 'cancelled')),
    CHECK (official_ownership_status IN ('unknown', 'not_applicable', 'pending', 'confirmed', 'rejected'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS institutional_requests (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    institution_code VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    request_type VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
    external_request_ref VARCHAR(240),
    correlation_id VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    legal_basis_ref VARCHAR(240),
    access_scope JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    response_metadata JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    requested_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    CHECK (status IN ('pending', 'authorized', 'rejected', 'submitted', 'completed', 'failed', 'cancelled'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS audit_events (
    id BIGINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    event_code VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
    entity_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    entity_ref VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    actor_ref VARCHAR(160),
    outcome VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
    reason_code VARCHAR(100),
    correlation_id VARCHAR(160),
    rule_version_ref VARCHAR(160),
    metadata JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS idempotency_records (
    id BIGINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    scope_code VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
    idempotency_key VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    request_hash VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'in_progress',
    response_status INTEGER,
    response_body JSONB,
    correlation_id VARCHAR(160),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    UNIQUE (scope_code, idempotency_key),
    CHECK (status IN ('in_progress', 'completed', 'failed'))
);

-- Indexes for public catalogue, lifecycle queues, matching and traceability.
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_offers_public_catalogue
    ON offers (market_id, public_category_id, published_at DESC, id DESC)
    WHERE status = 'published';

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_offers_classification_status
    ON offers (classification_id, status, created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_offers_validity
    ON offers (valid_until)
    WHERE status = 'published' AND valid_until IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_category_mappings_public
    ON category_mappings (public_category_id, is_active);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_supply_requests_status_deadline
    ON supply_requests (status, response_deadline);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_supply_proposals_request_status
    ON supply_proposals (request_id, status, created_at);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_orders_offer_status
    ON orders (offer_id, status, created_at);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_orders_order_book
    ON orders (offer_id, side, price_amount, created_at)
    WHERE status IN ('open', 'partially_filled');

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_matches_buy_order
    ON order_matches (buy_order_id, created_at);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_matches_sell_order
    ON order_matches (sell_order_id, created_at);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_trades_status_created
    ON trades (status, created_at);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_trade_workflows_status
    ON trade_workflows (status, updated_at);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_trade_state_history_trade
    ON trade_state_history (trade_id, created_at);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_integration_operations_retry
    ON integration_operations (status, next_retry_at)
    WHERE status IN ('pending', 'retry_wait', 'failed');

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_real_asset_workflows_status
    ON real_asset_workflows (status, updated_at);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_institutional_requests_correlation
    ON institutional_requests (correlation_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_events_entity
    ON audit_events (entity_type, entity_ref, created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_events_correlation
    ON audit_events (correlation_id)
    WHERE correlation_id IS NOT NULL;

FILE: migrations\000003_integrity_hardening.sql
SIZE: 4355 bytes
LAST MODIFIED: 11/04/2026 07:23:48
####################################################################################################
-- General Market migration 000003.
-- Integrity hardening. Additive and transactional.
-- This file must never replace or modify an earlier migration.

-- 1. Specialized classification versioning.
ALTER TABLE specialized_classifications
    DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS specialized_classifications_code_key;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS uq_specialized_classifications_code_version
    ON specialized_classifications (code, version);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS uq_specialized_classifications_one_active_code
    ON specialized_classifications (code)
    WHERE is_active = TRUE;

-- 2. Global category mappings.
-- The original UNIQUE constraint already protects non-NULL market_id rows.
-- This partial index closes PostgreSQL's NULL uniqueness gap.
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS uq_category_mappings_global
    ON category_mappings (public_category_id, specialized_classification_id)
    WHERE market_id IS NULL;

-- 3. Store the offer identity on each match.
ALTER TABLE order_matches
    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS offer_id UUID;

UPDATE order_matches AS om
SET offer_id = buy_order.offer_id
FROM orders AS buy_order
WHERE om.buy_order_id = buy_order.id
  AND om.offer_id IS NULL;

DO $$
BEGIN
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM order_matches AS om
        JOIN orders AS buy_order ON buy_order.id = om.buy_order_id
        JOIN orders AS sell_order ON sell_order.id = om.sell_order_id
        WHERE buy_order.offer_id <> sell_order.offer_id
           OR om.offer_id IS NULL
           OR om.offer_id <> buy_order.offer_id
    ) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION
            'Migration 000003 stopped: an order match references inconsistent offers';
    END IF;
END
$$;

ALTER TABLE order_matches
    ALTER COLUMN offer_id SET NOT NULL;

-- 4. Composite unique keys used by referential integrity constraints.
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS uq_orders_id_offer_id
    ON orders (id, offer_id);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS uq_order_matches_identity
    ON order_matches (id, buy_order_id, sell_order_id, offer_id);

-- 5. Each side of a match must belong to the same offer recorded by the match.
DO $$
BEGIN
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint
        WHERE conrelid = 'order_matches'::regclass
          AND conname = 'fk_match_buy_order_offer'
    ) THEN
        ALTER TABLE order_matches
            ADD CONSTRAINT fk_match_buy_order_offer
            FOREIGN KEY (buy_order_id, offer_id)
            REFERENCES orders (id, offer_id)
            ON DELETE RESTRICT;
    END IF;

    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint
        WHERE conrelid = 'order_matches'::regclass
          AND conname = 'fk_match_sell_order_offer'
    ) THEN
        ALTER TABLE order_matches
            ADD CONSTRAINT fk_match_sell_order_offer
            FOREIGN KEY (sell_order_id, offer_id)
            REFERENCES orders (id, offer_id)
            ON DELETE RESTRICT;
    END IF;
END
$$;

-- 6. A trade must reference the exact match and exact two orders.
DO $$
BEGIN
    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint
        WHERE conrelid = 'trades'::regclass
          AND conname = 'fk_trade_match_order_pair'
    ) THEN
        ALTER TABLE trades
            ADD CONSTRAINT fk_trade_match_order_pair
            FOREIGN KEY (match_id, buy_order_id, sell_order_id, offer_id)
            REFERENCES order_matches (id, buy_order_id, sell_order_id, offer_id)
            ON DELETE RESTRICT;
    END IF;

    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint
        WHERE conrelid = 'trades'::regclass
          AND conname = 'fk_trade_buy_order_offer'
    ) THEN
        ALTER TABLE trades
            ADD CONSTRAINT fk_trade_buy_order_offer
            FOREIGN KEY (buy_order_id, offer_id)
            REFERENCES orders (id, offer_id)
            ON DELETE RESTRICT;
    END IF;

    IF NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint
        WHERE conrelid = 'trades'::regclass
          AND conname = 'fk_trade_sell_order_offer'
    ) THEN
        ALTER TABLE trades
            ADD CONSTRAINT fk_trade_sell_order_offer
            FOREIGN KEY (sell_order_id, offer_id)
            REFERENCES orders (id, offer_id)
            ON DELETE RESTRICT;
    END IF;
END
$$;

    if (Test-Path (Join-Path $Nested "go.mod")) {
        $Root = $Nested

    else {
        throw "Run from general-market-backend or its parent directory."
    }
}

$RequiredFiles = @(
    "go.mod",

        throw "Required file missing: $Relative. No changes made."
    }
}

    throw "Migration 000003 already exists. Script stopped to prevent overwriting."

        $DestinationDir = Split-Path $Destination -Parent
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $DestinationDir -Force | Out-Null
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $Source -Destination $Destination
    }

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. New database.go
#    Opening the application database no longer runs migrations.
# ------------------------------------------------------------

$DatabaseGo = @'
package database

import (
	"context"
	"database/sql"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	_ "github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/stdlib"
)

type Config struct {
	URL             string
	MaxOpenConns    int
	MaxIdleConns    int
	ConnMaxLifetime time.Duration
}

func Open(ctx context.Context, cfg Config) (*sql.DB, error) {
	if cfg.URL == "" {
		return nil, nil
	}

	db, err := sql.Open("pgx", cfg.URL)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("open database: %w", err)
	}

	if cfg.MaxOpenConns > 0 {
		db.SetMaxOpenConns(cfg.MaxOpenConns)
	}
	if cfg.MaxIdleConns >= 0 {
		db.SetMaxIdleConns(cfg.MaxIdleConns)
	}
	if cfg.ConnMaxLifetime > 0 {
		db.SetConnMaxLifetime(cfg.ConnMaxLifetime)
	}

	pingCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 5*time.Second)
	defer cancel()

	if err := db.PingContext(pingCtx); err != nil {
		_ = db.Close()
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("connect to database: %w", err)
	}

	return db, nil
}
'@

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. New migration runner
#    - PostgreSQL session advisory lock
#    - SHA-256 checksums
#    - Legacy checksum adoption
#    - One transaction per migration
# ------------------------------------------------------------

$MigrateGo = @'
package database

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/hex"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/migrations"
)

const migrationLockID int64 = 5270645408566230100

func Migrate(ctx context.Context, db *sql.DB) error {
	if db == nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("database is not configured")
	}

	conn, err := db.Conn(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("acquire migration connection: %w", err)
	}
	defer conn.Close()

	if _, err := conn.ExecContext(
		ctx,
		"SELECT pg_advisory_lock($1)",
		migrationLockID,
	); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("acquire migration advisory lock: %w", err)
	}

	defer func() {
		unlockCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 5*time.Second)
		defer cancel()
		_, _ = conn.ExecContext(
			unlockCtx,
			"SELECT pg_advisory_unlock($1)",
			migrationLockID,
		)
	}()

	_, err = conn.ExecContext(ctx, `
		CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schema_migrations (
			version TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
			checksum TEXT,
			applied_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
		)
	`)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("initialize migration tracking: %w", err)
	}

	_, err = conn.ExecContext(ctx, `
		ALTER TABLE schema_migrations
		ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS checksum TEXT
	`)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("upgrade migration tracking: %w", err)
	}

	files, err := fs.Glob(migrations.Files, "*.sql")
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("list migration files: %w", err)
	}
	sort.Strings(files)

	for _, file := range files {
		if strings.Contains(file, "..") {
			return fmt.Errorf("invalid migration path: %s", file)
		}

		content, err := migrations.Files.ReadFile(file)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("read migration %s: %w", file, err)
		}

		version := strings.TrimSuffix(file, ".sql")
		sum := sha256.Sum256(content)
		checksum := hex.EncodeToString(sum[:])

		var stored sql.NullString
		err = conn.QueryRowContext(
			ctx,
			`SELECT checksum FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = $1`,
			version,
		).Scan(&stored)

		if err == nil {
			if !stored.Valid || stored.String == "" {
				// Existing installations created before checksum support:
				// adopt the currently deployed migration as the baseline.
				_, err = conn.ExecContext(
					ctx,
					`UPDATE schema_migrations
					 SET checksum = $1
					 WHERE version = $2 AND checksum IS NULL`,
					checksum,
					version,
				)
				if err != nil {
					return fmt.Errorf("adopt legacy checksum for %s: %w", version, err)
				}
				continue
			}

			if stored.String != checksum {
				return fmt.Errorf(
					"migration checksum mismatch for %s: applied migration file was modified; restore the original file or create a new migration",
					version,
				)
			}
			continue
		}

		if err != sql.ErrNoRows {
			return fmt.Errorf("check migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		tx, err := conn.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("begin migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		if _, err = tx.ExecContext(ctx, string(content)); err == nil {
			_, err = tx.ExecContext(
				ctx,
				`INSERT INTO schema_migrations (version, checksum)
				 VALUES ($1, $2)`,
				version,
				checksum,
			)
		}

		if err != nil {
			_ = tx.Rollback()
			return fmt.Errorf("apply migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}

		if err := tx.Commit(); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("commit migration %s: %w", version, err)
		}
	}

	return nil
}
'@

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. New additive SQL migration
#    No destructive data rewrite.
#    Existing inconsistent rows cause a safe migration failure.
# ------------------------------------------------------------

$MigrationSQL = @'
-- General Market migration 000003.
-- Integrity hardening. Additive and transactional.
-- This file must never replace or modify an earlier migration.

-- 1. Specialized classification versioning.

    DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS specialized_classifications_code_key;

    ON specialized_classifications (code)

SET offer_id = buy_order.offer_id
FROM orders AS buy_order
WHERE om.buy_order_id = buy_order.id
  AND om.offer_id IS NULL;

DO $$
BEGIN

        SELECT 1
        FROM order_matches AS om
        JOIN orders AS buy_order ON buy_order.id = om.buy_order_id
        JOIN orders AS sell_order ON sell_order.id = om.sell_order_id

           OR om.offer_id <> buy_order.offer_id

END
$$;

-- 4. Composite unique keys used by referential integrity constraints.

          AND conname = 'fk_match_buy_order_offer'
    ) THEN
