# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Dedicated migration command
# ------------------------------------------------------------

$MigrateMain = @'
package main

import (
	"context"
	"log"
	"os"
	"time"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/config"
	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/database"
)

func main() {
	cfg, err := config.Load()
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("invalid configuration: %v", err)
	}

	if cfg.DatabaseURL == "" {
		log.Fatal("DATABASE_URL is required for migration execution")
	}

	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 2*time.Minute)
	defer cancel()

	db, err := database.Open(ctx, database.Config{
		URL:             cfg.DatabaseURL,
		MaxOpenConns:    cfg.DBMaxOpenConns,
		MaxIdleConns:    cfg.DBMaxIdleConns,
		ConnMaxLifetime: cfg.DBConnMaxLifetime,
	})
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("database connection failed: %v", err)
	}
	defer db.Close()

	if err := database.Migrate(ctx, db); err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("migration failed: %v", err)
	}

	log.Print("Database migrations completed successfully.")
	os.Exit(0)
}
'@

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Write files
# ------------------------------------------------------------

try {
    $FilesToWrite = @{
        "internal\database\database.go" = $DatabaseGo
        "internal\database\migrate.go" = $MigrateGo
        "migrations\000003_integrity_hardening.sql" = $MigrationSQL
        "cmd\migrate\main.go" = $MigrateMain
    }

    foreach ($Relative in $FilesToWrite.Keys) {
        $Path = Join-Path $Root $Relative
        $Directory = Split-Path $Path -Parent

        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $Directory -Force | Out-Null
        [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($Path, $FilesToWrite[$Relative], $Utf8)
        $WrittenFiles += $Relative
    }

    Push-Location $Root
    try {
        Write-Host "`n--- gofmt ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan

        & gofmt -w `
            "internal\database\database.go" `
            "internal\database\migrate.go" `
            "cmd\migrate\main.go"

        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            throw "gofmt failed."
        }

        Write-Host "`n--- go test ./... ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan
        & go test ./...

        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            throw "Go tests failed."
        }

        Write-Host "`n--- go vet ./... ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan
        & go vet ./...

        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            throw "go vet failed."
        }

        Write-Host "`n--- go build ./... ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan
        & go build ./...

        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
            throw "Go build failed."
        }

        Write-Host "`nSUCCESS: source changes and Go checks completed." -ForegroundColor Green
        Write-Host "Backup: $BackupDir"
        Write-Host "New migration: migrations\000003_integrity_hardening.sql"
        Write-Host "Database migration was NOT executed." -ForegroundColor Yellow
    }
    finally {
        Pop-Location
    }
}
catch {
    Write-Host "`nERROR: $($_.Exception.Message)" -ForegroundColor Red
    Write-Host "Restoring files from backup..." -ForegroundColor Yellow

    foreach ($Relative in $Targets) {
        $BackupFile = Join-Path $BackupDir $Relative
        $TargetFile = Join-Path $Root $Relative

        if (Test-Path $BackupFile -PathType Leaf) {
            $TargetDirectory = Split-Path $TargetFile -Parent
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $TargetDirectory -Force | Out-Null
            Copy-Item -LiteralPath $BackupFile -Destination $TargetFile -Force
        }
        elseif ($Relative -in $WrittenFiles -and
                $Relative -eq "migrations\000003_integrity_hardening.sql") {
            if (Test-Path $TargetFile) {
                Remove-Item -LiteralPath $TargetFile -Force
            }
        }
        elseif ($Relative -in $WrittenFiles -and
                $Relative -eq "cmd\migrate\main.go") {
            if (Test-Path $TargetFile) {
                Remove-Item -LiteralPath $TargetFile -Force
            }
        }
    }

    Write-Host "Rollback of modified source files attempted." -ForegroundColor Yellow
    Write-Host "Backup directory: $BackupDir"
    throw
}


####################################################################################################
FILE: scripts\Complete-GeneralMarketArchitecture.ps1
SIZE: 24612 bytes
LAST MODIFIED: 10/27/2026 10:25:48

[CmdletBinding()]
param()

Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

# ============================================================
# General Market Backend - Architecture Completion
# Safe, additive, non-destructive PowerShell script.
# Does not move, delete, or overwrite existing files.
# Does not connect to PostgreSQL or run migrations.
# ============================================================

$ProjectRoot = Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot
$ExpectedRootName = "general-market-backend"
$Utf8NoBom = New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)

if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ProjectRoot -PathType Container)) {
    throw "Project root not found: $ProjectRoot"
}

if ((Split-Path -Leaf $ProjectRoot) -ne $ExpectedRootName) {
    throw "Wrong project root. Expected '$ExpectedRootName', got '$ProjectRoot'."
}

$CreatedDirectories = [System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()
$ExistingDirectories = [System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()
$CreatedFiles = [System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()
$ExistingFiles = [System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()

function Ensure-Directory {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)]
        [string]$RelativePath
    )

    $FullPath = Join-Path $ProjectRoot $RelativePath

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $FullPath) {
        if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $FullPath -PathType Container)) {
            throw "Cannot create directory because a file occupies this path: $FullPath"
        }

        $ExistingDirectories.Add($RelativePath)
        return
    }

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $FullPath | Out-Null
    $CreatedDirectories.Add($RelativePath)
}

function Ensure-TextFile {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)]
        [string]$RelativePath,

        [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)]
        [string]$Content
    )

    $FullPath = Join-Path $ProjectRoot $RelativePath
    $ParentPath = Split-Path -Parent $FullPath

    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ParentPath -PathType Container)) {
        throw "Parent directory does not exist: $ParentPath"
    }

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $FullPath) {
        if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $FullPath -PathType Leaf)) {
            throw "Cannot create file because a directory occupies this path: $FullPath"
        }

        $ExistingFiles.Add($RelativePath)
        return
    }

    [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText(
        $FullPath,
        $Content.Trim() + [Environment]::NewLine,
        $Utf8NoBom
    )

    $CreatedFiles.Add($RelativePath)
}

# 1. Complete directory tree
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Module responsibility documents
#    Existing documents are preserved without modification.
# ------------------------------------------------------------

$Responsibilities = [ordered]@{
    "internal\access" = @{
        Title = "Access and Permissions"
        Body = @"
Enforce the market API access boundary.
Validate identity and operation permissions through the Account service
and its approved integration contract.
Do not create a competing identity, role, or credential database.
"@
    }

    "internal\catalogue" = @{
        Title = "Public Catalogue"
        Body = @"
Provide public classification, search, filtering, pagination, and offer
discovery.
Map public classifications to specialized classifications.
Expose only offers eligible for public display.
"@
    }

    "internal\market" = @{
        Title = "Market Management"
        Body = @"
Manage market definitions, market lifecycle states, and public-to-specialized
classification mappings.
Do not duplicate authoritative data owned by independent services.
"@
    }

    "internal\offerconfig" = @{
        Title = "Parametric Offer Configuration"
        Body = @"
Coordinate versioned specialized classifications, parameters, rules,
calculations, documents, company qualification, approval, and publication.
Offer records must reference the applicable configuration version.
"@
    }

    "internal\offerconfig\classifications" = @{
        Title = "Specialized Classifications"
        Body = "Define domain-specific offer classifications and their relationship to public catalogue classifications."
    }

    "internal\offerconfig\parameters" = @{
        Title = "Offer Parameters"
        Body = "Define typed parameters, constraints, required values, and validation metadata for each supported offer type."
    }

    "internal\offerconfig\rules" = @{
        Title = "Configuration Rules"
        Body = "Define explicit, versioned validation and eligibility rules for configured offer types."
    }

    "internal\offerconfig\calculations" = @{
        Title = "Offer Calculations"
        Body = "Define deterministic, validated calculations needed by offer types. Do not treat client calculations as authoritative."
    }

    "internal\offerconfig\documents" = @{
        Title = "Required Documents"
        Body = "Define document requirements, validation metadata, and references to authoritative document storage where applicable."
    }

    "internal\offerconfig\qualification" = @{
        Title = "Company Qualification"
        Body = "Define qualification requirements and coordinate checks with authoritative sources. Do not independently assert legal status without evidence."
    }

    "internal\offerconfig\approvals" = @{
        Title = "Configuration Approval"
        Body = "Coordinate review, approval, rejection, and publication of configuration versions with auditable outcomes."
    }

    "internal\offerconfig\versions" = @{
        Title = "Configuration Versions"
        Body = "Manage immutable published configuration versions and ensure offers and trades can reference the version used."
    }

    "internal\eligibility" = @{
        Title = "Eligibility"
        Body = @"
Validate participant, offer, and operation eligibility using explicit rules
and authoritative service responses.
Keep references and necessary validation outcomes without duplicating
identity, ownership, balances, or legal records.
"@
    }

    "internal\offers" = @{
        Title = "Offer Management"
        Body = @"
Manage the offer lifecycle: draft, pending review, published, suspended,
rejected, expired, and closed as applicable.
Retain references to the configuration version used by each offer.
"@
    }

    "internal\supplyrequests" = @{
        Title = "Supply Requests and Proposals"
        Body = @"
Register supply requests, receive proposals, compare them using defined
business rules, and record the selection of a valid proposal.
"@
    }

    "internal\orders" = @{
        Title = "Orders and Order Book"
        Body = @"
Validate and manage order lifecycle and order-book operations.
Coordinate buying-power checks and reservation/release with the My Assets
service. Enforce idempotency and concurrency safety.
"@
    }

    "internal\matching" = @{
        Title = "Matching Engine"
        Body = @"
Match eligible orders using deterministic price, quantity, and priority rules.
Protect matching against concurrent execution and duplicate processing.
"@
    }

    "internal\trades" = @{
        Title = "Trade Management"
        Body = @"
Create and manage trade records and references to related orders and execution
workflows. Do not replace authoritative contract, service, ownership, or
settlement records.
"@
    }

    "internal\tradestates" = @{
        Title = "Trade-Related State Management"
        Body = @"
Define and validate separate state machines for orders, matching results,
trades, contract progress references, service progress references, delivery,
and settlement readiness.
Coordinate with Workflow without duplicating workflow orchestration.
External systems remain authoritative for their own state.
"@
    }

    "internal\execution" = @{
        Title = "Trade Execution Policies"
        Body = @"
Define versioned execution policies and ordered steps selected by offer
or trade type.
Represent mandatory, optional, and conditional steps explicitly.
"@
    }

    "internal\workflow" = @{
        Title = "Workflow Coordination"
        Body = @"
Coordinate execution steps across contracts, services, asset operations,
delivery, and settlement.
Handle timeouts, incomplete work, retry safety, and recovery.
Do not replace the independent state machines or external systems of record.
"@
    }

    "internal\commodity" = @{
        Title = "Commodity Supply and Demand Aggregation"
        Body = @"
Aggregate eligible small supply or demand quantities and calculate
commercially suitable aggregated proposals.
Define quantity, quality, delivery, and eligibility constraints explicitly.
"@
    }

    "internal\realassets" = @{
        Title = "Real-Asset Workflows"
        Body = @"
Coordinate market workflows for real assets, including property-related
steps where applicable.
Distinguish a platform position or contractual right from legally registered
ownership. Legal registration remains with the appropriate authoritative
institution or system.
"@
    }

    "internal\integrations" = @{
        Title = "Service Integration Boundary"
        Body = @"
Provide controlled clients and contracts for independent services.
Define timeouts, correlation identifiers, error mapping, and safe retry rules.
Do not store duplicate authoritative records from connected services.
"@
    }

    "internal\integrations\account" = @{
        Title = "Account Service Client"
        Body = "Integrate with Account for identity, authentication context, roles, and permissions through an explicit contract."
    }

    "internal\integrations\assets" = @{
        Title = "My Assets Service Client"
        Body = "Request authoritative buying-power checks, reservations, releases, and asset-position references. Market is not the owner of free balances."
    }

    "internal\integrations\contracts" = @{
        Title = "Contracts Service Client"
        Body = "Coordinate contract creation and status queries through the Contracts service. Store references and necessary workflow state only."
    }

    "internal\integrations\services" = @{
        Title = "Services Platform Client"
        Body = "Coordinate required or optional transport, storage, inspection, insurance, installation, and delivery operations through explicit contracts."
    }

    "internal\integrations\settlement" = @{
        Title = "Settlement Service Client"
        Body = "Request settlement operations and confirm their outcomes from the Settlement service. Never infer successful settlement from a request being sent."
    }

    "internal\integrations\institutions" = @{
        Title = "Official and Regulatory Integrations"
        Body = @"
Support legally authorized requests to official or regulatory institutions.
Enforce purpose, permissions, data scope, correlation IDs, response tracking,
and auditable outcomes.
"@
    }

    "internal\audit" = @{
        Title = "Audit and Traceability"
        Body = @"
Record significant changes, actor references, timestamps, outcomes,
rule versions, and correlation identifiers.
Avoid copying full records from other systems without a defined requirement.
"@
    }

    "internal\httpapi" = @{
        Title = "HTTP API"
        Body = @"
Expose versioned API contracts, request validation, consistent errors,
middleware, and health endpoints.
Keep business rules in their responsible domain modules.
The server is authoritative for permissions and state changes.
"@
    }

    "infrastructure\postgres" = @{
        Title = "PostgreSQL Infrastructure"
        Body = @"
Own PostgreSQL connection and persistence infrastructure.
Use bounded connection pools, explicit transactions, indexed queries,
and versioned migrations.
Never reset or destructively rebuild existing databases automatically.
Review any existing internal/database implementation before moving code.
"@
    }

    "infrastructure\redis" = @{
        Title = "Redis Adapter - Deferred"
        Body = @"
Redis is optional and must not be introduced into critical workflows without
a measured technical need.
If later adopted, define cache invalidation and failure behavior explicitly.
"@
    }

    "infrastructure\messaging" = @{
        Title = "Messaging Adapter - Deferred"
        Body = @"
A message broker is optional.
Add one only when measured requirements justify it, with explicit delivery,
idempotency, retry, and recovery semantics.
"@
    }

    "tests" = @{
        Title = "Test Strategy"
        Body = @"
Plan unit, PostgreSQL integration, API, concurrency, idempotency, security,
failure-recovery, and performance tests.
Tests must use controlled test resources, not production data.
"@
    }
}

Write-Host "[2/5] Creating missing module documentation..." -ForegroundColor Cyan

foreach ($RelativeDirectory in $Responsibilities.Keys) {
    $Definition = $Responsibilities[$RelativeDirectory]
    $ReadmePath = "$RelativeDirectory\README.md"

    $Content = @"
# $($Definition.Title)

## Responsibility

$($Definition.Body.Trim())

## Architecture constraints

- Keep one authoritative owner for each category of data.
- Validate inputs and state transitions on the server.
- Use explicit service contracts and safe idempotency where operations
  can be retried.
- Add dependencies only when required by a defined responsibility.

## Implementation status

Structural documentation only. Business implementation and automated tests
must be completed and verified separately.
"@

    Ensure-TextFile -RelativePath $ReadmePath -Content $Content
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Central architecture map
# ------------------------------------------------------------

$ArchitectureMap = @'
# General Market Backend - Architecture Map

## Purpose

The General Market backend coordinates offers, supply requests, orders,
matching, trades, and trade execution workflows. It does not replace the
independent systems that own identity, balances, contracts, services,
ownership records, or financial settlement.

## Logical tree

general-market-backend/
├── cmd/server/                  Application entry point
├── config/                      Environment configuration
├── internal/
│   ├── access/                  Access boundary and permission checks
│   ├── catalogue/               Public catalogue, search, filters, pagination
│   ├── market/                  Market definitions and lifecycle
│   ├── offerconfig/             Versioned parametric configuration
│   │   ├── classifications/
│   │   ├── parameters/
│   │   ├── rules/
│   │   ├── calculations/
│   │   ├── documents/
│   │   ├── qualification/
│   │   ├── approvals/
│   │   └── versions/
│   ├── eligibility/             Offer and operation eligibility
│   ├── offers/                  Offer lifecycle
│   ├── supplyrequests/          Supply requests and proposals
│   ├── orders/                  Order lifecycle and order book
│   ├── matching/                Deterministic order matching
│   ├── trades/                  Trade records and references
│   ├── tradestates/             Explicit trade-related state machines
│   ├── execution/               Versioned execution policies
│   ├── workflow/                Cross-service workflow coordination
│   ├── commodity/               Commodity supply/demand aggregation
│   ├── realassets/              Real-asset and property workflows
│   ├── integrations/
│   │   ├── account/
│   │   ├── assets/
│   │   ├── contracts/
│   │   ├── services/
│   │   ├── settlement/
│   │   └── institutions/
│   ├── audit/                   Audit and traceability
│   └── httpapi/                 Versioned HTTP API
├── infrastructure/
│   ├── postgres/                PostgreSQL adapter
│   ├── redis/                   Optional; deferred until justified
│   └── messaging/               Optional; deferred until justified
├── migrations/                  Versioned database migrations
├── tests/                       Automated test strategy and test suites
├── scripts/                     Development and maintenance scripts
└── docs/architecture/           Architecture documentation

## Systems of record

- Account: identity, authentication context, roles, and permissions.
- My Assets: free balance, buying power, reservation/release, asset positions.
- Contracts: legal contracts and obligations.
- Services: transport, storage, inspection, insurance, installation, delivery.
- Settlement: authoritative financial settlement execution and result.
- Official institutions: authoritative legal registration or official records,
  where applicable.
- General Market: market workflow, offers, orders, matching, trades, references,
  execution policy, and coordination states.

## Mandatory design constraints

1. The client is not authoritative for permissions, buying power, matching,
   or state transitions.
2. Financial and asset balances must not be independently duplicated here.
3. External operations require explicit contracts, timeouts, error handling,
   correlation identifiers, and safe retry behavior.
4. Concurrent order and matching operations require transactional safeguards
   and idempotency.
5. Published configuration and execution policy versions must be traceable.
6. Important transitions must be auditable without copying complete external
   records unnecessarily.
7. Database migrations must be versioned, reviewable, and non-destructive by
   default. Never rebuild or erase an existing database automatically.
8. Redis and message brokers are optional; introduce them only after a
   measured technical requirement.
9. A directory or README is not a completed feature. Each module needs code,
   API contracts where applicable, automated tests, and acceptance checks.
10. Infrastructure adapters must have a single clear location. If an older
    implementation exists under internal/database, review imports and behavior
    before consolidating or moving it to infrastructure/postgres.

## Implementation order

1. Inspect current repository, Go packages, migrations, configuration, and API.
2. Stabilize application bootstrap, PostgreSQL access, errors, logging, health,
   and test harness.
3. Implement catalogue and market/offer configuration.
4. Implement offers and eligibility.
5. Implement supply requests, orders, reservations, and matching.
6. Implement trades, state machines, execution policies, and workflows.
7. Add integrations, commodity aggregation, real-asset workflows, and audit
   coverage as required by the detailed specification.
8. Verify concurrency, security, performance, migrations, and recovery.

This document defines logical responsibilities, not separate deployable
services. Go package boundaries must remain simple and avoid circular imports.
'@

Write-Host "[3/5] Creating architecture map if missing..." -ForegroundColor Cyan

Ensure-TextFile `
    -RelativePath "docs\architecture\GENERAL-MARKET-MODULE-MAP.md" `
    -Content $ArchitectureMap

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Verify all expected paths
# ------------------------------------------------------------

Write-Host "[4/5] Verifying structure..." -ForegroundColor Cyan

$MissingPaths = [System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()

foreach ($RelativeDirectory in $Directories) {
    $FullPath = Join-Path $ProjectRoot $RelativeDirectory

    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $FullPath -PathType Container)) {
        $MissingPaths.Add($RelativeDirectory)
    }
}

foreach ($RelativeDirectory in $Responsibilities.Keys) {
    $RelativePath = "$RelativeDirectory\README.md"
    $FullPath = Join-Path $ProjectRoot $RelativePath

    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $FullPath -PathType Leaf)) {
        $MissingPaths.Add($RelativePath)
    }
}

$MapPath = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "docs\architecture\GENERAL-MARKET-MODULE-MAP.md"

if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $MapPath -PathType Leaf)) {
    $MissingPaths.Add("docs\architecture\GENERAL-MARKET-MODULE-MAP.md")
}

if ($MissingPaths.Count -gt 0) {
    Write-Host "`nVerification failed. Missing paths:" -ForegroundColor Red
    foreach ($Path in $MissingPaths) {
        Write-Host "  - $Path"
    }
    throw "Architecture verification failed."
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Report and highlight legacy database location
# ------------------------------------------------------------

Write-Host "[5/5] Reporting results..." -ForegroundColor Cyan

Write-Host "`n============================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host " General Market Architecture Completion" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "============================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan

Write-Host "Project root: $ProjectRoot"

Write-Host "`nNew directories: $($CreatedDirectories.Count)" -ForegroundColor Green
foreach ($Path in $CreatedDirectories) {
    Write-Host "  + $Path"
}

Write-Host "`nDirectories already present: $($ExistingDirectories.Count)" -ForegroundColor DarkGray

Write-Host "`nNew files: $($CreatedFiles.Count)" -ForegroundColor Green
foreach ($Path in $CreatedFiles) {
    Write-Host "  + $Path"
}

Write-Host "`nExisting files preserved: $($ExistingFiles.Count)" -ForegroundColor DarkGray

$LegacyDatabasePath = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "internal\database"

if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $LegacyDatabasePath -PathType Container) {
    Write-Host "`nATTENTION: internal\database still exists." -ForegroundColor Yellow
    Write-Host "No files were moved. Review this directory before deciding whether"
    Write-Host "to consolidate its implementation under infrastructure\postgres."
}

Write-Host "`nVerification: PASSED" -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "Existing files were not overwritten."
Write-Host "No database connection was opened."
Write-Host "No migration was executed."
Write-Host "No Redis or messaging dependency was installed."

Write-Host "`nNext step: inspect existing Go code and migrations before implementing modules." -ForegroundColor Cyan

[CmdletBinding()]
param()

Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Determine and validate the project root
# ------------------------------------------------------------

$ProjectRoot = Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot
$ExpectedRootName = "general-market-backend"

if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ProjectRoot -PathType Container)) {
    throw "Project root does not exist: $ProjectRoot"
}

$ActualRootName = Split-Path -Leaf $ProjectRoot

if ($ActualRootName -ne $ExpectedRootName) {
    throw "Unexpected project root: $ProjectRoot. Expected folder name: $ExpectedRootName"
}

$Utf8NoBom = New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)

$CreatedDirectories = [System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()
$ExistingDirectories = [System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()
$CreatedFiles = [System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()
$ExistingFiles = [System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Define the complete target directory tree
# ------------------------------------------------------------

$Directories = @(
    "cmd"
    "cmd\server"
    "config"

    "internal"
    "internal\access"
    "internal\catalogue"
    "internal\market"
    "internal\offerconfig"
    "internal\eligibility"
    "internal\offers"
    "internal\supplyrequests"
    "internal\orders"
    "internal\matching"
    "internal\trades"
    "internal\execution"
    "internal\workflow"
    "internal\commodity"
    "internal\integrations"
    "internal\audit"
    "internal\httpapi"

    "infrastructure"
    "infrastructure\postgres"
    "infrastructure\redis"
    "infrastructure\messaging"

    "migrations"
    "tests"
    "scripts"
)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Create only missing directories
# ------------------------------------------------------------

foreach ($RelativePath in $Directories) {
    $FullPath = Join-Path $ProjectRoot $RelativePath

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $FullPath) {
        if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $FullPath -PathType Container)) {
            throw "A file exists where a directory is required: $FullPath"
        }

        $ExistingDirectories.Add($RelativePath)
        continue
    }

    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $FullPath | Out-Null
    $CreatedDirectories.Add($RelativePath)
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Define responsibilities for modules not implemented yet
#    These README files are documentation, not business code.
# ------------------------------------------------------------

$ReadmeFiles = [ordered]@{
    "internal\access\README.md" = @'
# Access and Permissions

Responsibility:
- Enforce the market API's access boundary.
- Validate authenticated identity and operation permissions through the
  Account service or its approved integration contract.
- Do not become a second identity or role database.

Implementation status: Not implemented.
'@

    "internal\catalogue\README.md" = @'
# Public Catalogue

Responsibility:
- Public classifications, browsing, filtering, and pagination.
- Map public classifications to the applicable specialized classification.
- Return only eligible, published market offers.

Implementation status: Not implemented.
'@

    "internal\market\README.md" = @'
# Market Management

Responsibility:
- Market definitions, configuration, and lifecycle states.
- Link public classifications to specialized offer classifications.
- Do not duplicate the authoritative data owned by other services.

Implementation status: Not implemented.
'@

    "internal\offerconfig\README.md" = @'
# Parametric Offer Configuration

Responsibility:
- Specialized classifications, parameters, validation rules, required
  documents, and versioned configuration.

- Keep configuration distinct from individual offers.

Implementation status: Not implemented.
'@

    "internal\eligibility\README.md" = @'
# Eligibility

Responsibility:

  authoritative rules and approved service contracts.

    "internal\offers\README.md" = @'
# Offer Management

- Create and manage offers and their lifecycle.
- Support draft, review, published, suspended, rejected, expired,

- Reference authoritative configuration versions.

Implementation status: Not implemented.
'@

    "internal\supplyrequests\README.md" = @'

- Record proposal selection and its audit references.

    "internal\orders\README.md" = @'
# Orders and Order Book

Responsibility:
- Validate and manage orders and their lifecycle.
- Coordinate authoritative buying-power checks and reservations with
  the My Assets service.
- Enforce idempotency, transactional consistency, and concurrency safety.

Implementation status: Not implemented.
'@

    "internal\matching\README.md" = @'
# Matching Engine

Responsibility:
- Match eligible orders according to versioned price and quantity rules.
- Define deterministic ordering and transaction boundaries.
- Prevent duplicate matches and race-condition errors.

Implementation status: Not implemented.
'@

    "internal\trades\README.md" = @'
# Trade Management

Responsibility:
- Record trades and references linking orders and execution workflows.
- Track trade state independently from contract, service, and settlement
  states owned by their respective systems.

Implementation status: Not implemented.
'@

    "internal\execution\README.md" = @'
# Trade Execution Policies

Responsibility:
- Define versioned execution policies and ordered execution steps.
- Support policy selection by offer or trade type.
- Keep external service operations behind explicit integration contracts.

Implementation status: Not implemented.
'@

    "internal\workflow\README.md" = @'
# Workflow and State Coordination

Responsibility:
- Coordinate trade execution across contracts, services, asset positions,
  and settlement.
- Track progress, failures, retries, and recoverable incomplete workflows.
- Do not replace the authoritative state of external services.

Implementation status: Not implemented.
'@

    "internal\commodity\README.md" = @'
# Commodity Supply and Demand Aggregation

Responsibility:
- Aggregate eligible small supply and demand quantities.
- Calculate commercially suitable aggregated proposals.
- Define quantity, quality, delivery, and eligibility constraints explicitly.

Implementation status: Not implemented.
'@

    "internal\integrations\README.md" = @'
# External Integrations

Responsibility:
- Define controlled clients and contracts for independent services.
- Apply timeouts, correlation identifiers, error mapping, and safe retry
  policies where appropriate.
- Never treat an unconfirmed external operation as successful.

Implementation status: Not implemented.
'@

    "internal\audit\README.md" = @'
# Audit and Traceability

Responsibility:
- Record significant state changes, actor references, timestamps,
  rule versions, outcomes, and correlation identifiers.
- Avoid copying complete records from other services without a clear need.

Implementation status: Not implemented.
'@

    "internal\httpapi\README.md" = @'
# HTTP API

Responsibility:
- Define versioned routes, request validation, response formats,
  middleware, and consistent error responses.
- Keep business rules in their responsible domain modules.
- Do not trust client-provided authorization or financial decisions.

Implementation status: Existing code, if present, is preserved.
'@

    "infrastructure\postgres\README.md" = @'
# PostgreSQL Infrastructure

Responsibility:
- Own the market backend's PostgreSQL connection and persistence adapter.
- Use bounded connection pools, explicit transactions, and indexed queries.
- Apply versioned migrations without destructive automatic database resets.

Implementation status: Structural placeholder only.
Review any existing internal/database implementation before consolidating
or moving database code. This script does not move or overwrite files.
'@

    "infrastructure\redis\README.md" = @'
# Redis Adapter — Deferred

Potential responsibility:
- Provide caching or other measured low-latency support if benchmarks
  demonstrate a concrete need.

Current policy:
- Redis is not required for the initial implementation.
- Do not add a Redis dependency or make core correctness depend on it
  until the technical need is demonstrated.

Implementation status: Deferred.
'@

    "infrastructure\messaging\README.md" = @'
# Messaging Adapter — Deferred

Potential responsibility:
- Provide asynchronous messaging if measured requirements justify it.
- Define delivery, idempotency, retry, and recovery semantics before use.

Current policy:
- A broker is not required for the initial implementation.
- Do not add Kafka, RabbitMQ, or another broker without a documented need.

Implementation status: Deferred.
'@

    "tests\README.md" = @'
# Test Strategy

Planned coverage:
- Unit tests for business rules.
- PostgreSQL integration tests.
- Concurrency and idempotency tests.
- API, security, and failure-recovery tests.
- Performance benchmarks for critical queries and matching operations.

Tests must run against controlled test resources, not production data.
'@
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Create only missing documentation files
# ------------------------------------------------------------

foreach ($Entry in $ReadmeFiles.GetEnumerator()) {
    $FullPath = Join-Path $ProjectRoot $Entry.Key
    $ParentPath = Split-Path -Parent $FullPath

    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ParentPath -PathType Container)) {
        throw "Required parent directory is missing: $ParentPath"
    }

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $FullPath) {
        $ExistingFiles.Add($Entry.Key)
        continue
    }

    [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText(
        $FullPath,
        [string]$Entry.Value.Trim() + [Environment]::NewLine,
        $Utf8NoBom
    )

    $CreatedFiles.Add($Entry.Key)
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Verify the expected directories and documentation
# ------------------------------------------------------------

$MissingPaths = [System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]::new()

foreach ($RelativePath in $Directories) {
    $FullPath = Join-Path $ProjectRoot $RelativePath

    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $FullPath -PathType Container)) {
        $MissingPaths.Add($RelativePath)
    }
}

foreach ($RelativePath in $ReadmeFiles.Keys) {
    $FullPath = Join-Path $ProjectRoot $RelativePath

    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $FullPath -PathType Leaf)) {
        $MissingPaths.Add($RelativePath)
    }
}

if ($MissingPaths.Count -gt 0) {
    Write-Host "`nVerification failed. Missing paths:" -ForegroundColor Red
    $MissingPaths | ForEach-Object { Write-Host "  - $_" }
    throw "Structure verification failed."
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Report results
# ------------------------------------------------------------

Write-Host "`n============================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host " General Market structure verification" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "============================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Project root: $ProjectRoot"

Write-Host "`nDirectories created: $($CreatedDirectories.Count)" -ForegroundColor Green
$CreatedDirectories | ForEach-Object { Write-Host "  + $_" }

Write-Host "`nDirectories already present: $($ExistingDirectories.Count)" -ForegroundColor DarkGray
$ExistingDirectories | ForEach-Object { Write-Host "  = $_" }

Write-Host "`nREADME files created: $($CreatedFiles.Count)" -ForegroundColor Green
$CreatedFiles | ForEach-Object { Write-Host "  + $_" }

Write-Host "`nREADME files already present: $($ExistingFiles.Count)" -ForegroundColor DarkGray
$ExistingFiles | ForEach-Object { Write-Host "  = $_" }

Write-Host "`nVerification: PASSED" -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "No existing file was overwritten by this script." -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "No database connection or migration was executed." -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "`nNext step: inspect the tree and reconcile existing database code before implementing domain modules." -ForegroundColor Cyan

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

# Resolve the backend root from the current working directory.
$BackendRoot = (Get-Location).Path

if ((Split-Path -Leaf $BackendRoot) -ne "general-market-backend") {
    $Candidate = Join-Path $BackendRoot "general-market-backend"

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Candidate -PathType Container) {
        $BackendRoot = (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $Candidate).Path
    }
    else {
        throw "Run this script from the general-market-backend directory."
    }
}

$ProjectRoot = Split-Path -Parent $BackendRoot
$ScriptsDir = Join-Path $BackendRoot "scripts"
$Timestamp = Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd-HHmmss"
$ReportPath = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "general-market-architecture-$Timestamp.txt"
$Encoding = New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)
$Lines = New-Object 'System.Collections.Generic.List[string]'

function Add-Line {
    param([string]$Text = "")
    $Lines.Add($Text)
}

function Get-RelativePath {
    param(
        [string]$BasePath,
        [string]$FullPath
    )

    $BaseFull = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($BasePath).TrimEnd('\') + '\'
    $FileFull = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($FullPath)

    if ($FileFull.StartsWith($BaseFull, [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) {
        return $FileFull.Substring($BaseFull.Length)
    }

    return $FileFull
}

$ExcludedDirectories = @(
    ".git", "vendor", "node_modules", "bin", "dist", "build",
    ".idea", ".vscode", "coverage"
)

$Files = @(
    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $BackendRoot -Recurse -File |
        Where-Object {
            $Relative = Get-RelativePath -BasePath $BackendRoot -FullPath $_.FullName
            $Parts = $Relative -split '[\\/]'
            -not ($Parts | Where-Object { $ExcludedDirectories -contains $_ })
        } |
        Sort-Object FullName
)

$Directories = @(
    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $BackendRoot -Recurse -Directory |
        Where-Object {
            $Relative = Get-RelativePath -BasePath $BackendRoot -FullPath $_.FullName
            $Parts = $Relative -split '[\\/]'
            -not ($Parts | Where-Object { $ExcludedDirectories -contains $_ })
        } |
        Sort-Object FullName
)

Add-Line "GENERAL MARKET BACKEND - ARCHITECTURE AUDIT"
Add-Line "Generated: $(Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss')"
Add-Line "Backend root: $BackendRoot"
Add-Line "Project root: $ProjectRoot"
Add-Line "Read-only report; source files and database were not modified."
Add-Line ""

Add-Line "============================================================"
Add-Line "1. ACTUAL DIRECTORY TREE"
Add-Line "============================================================"
Add-Line "general-market-backend\"

foreach ($Directory in $Directories) {
    $Relative = Get-RelativePath -BasePath $BackendRoot -FullPath $Directory.FullName
    $Depth = ($Relative -split '[\\/]').Count
    Add-Line (("  " * $Depth) + "+ " + (Split-Path -Leaf $Directory.FullName) + "\")
}

foreach ($File in $Files) {
    $Relative = Get-RelativePath -BasePath $BackendRoot -FullPath $File.FullName
    if ($Relative -notmatch '[\\/]') {
        Add-Line ("  " + $Relative)
    }
}

Add-Line ""
Add-Line "============================================================"
Add-Line "2. GO SOURCE FILES"
Add-Line "============================================================"

$GoFiles = @($Files | Where-Object { $_.Extension -eq ".go" })

foreach ($File in $GoFiles) {
    $Relative = Get-RelativePath -BasePath $BackendRoot -FullPath $File.FullName
    Add-Line ""
    Add-Line "FILE: $Relative"
    Add-Line "SIZE: $($File.Length) bytes"

    $SourceLines = @(Get-Content -LiteralPath $File.FullName)
    foreach ($SourceLine in $SourceLines) {
        if ($SourceLine -match '^\s*package\s+\w+' -or
            $SourceLine -match '^\s*import\s*(\(|")' -or
            $SourceLine -match '^\s*(type\s+\w+|func\s+(\([^)]*\)\s*)?\w+|var\s+\(|const\s+\()') {
            Add-Line ("  " + $SourceLine.Trim())
        }
    }
}

Add-Line ""
Add-Line "============================================================"
Add-Line "3. SQL MIGRATIONS"
Add-Line "============================================================"

$SqlFiles = @($Files | Where-Object { $_.Extension -eq ".sql" })

if ($SqlFiles.Count -eq 0) {
    Add-Line "No SQL files found."
}

foreach ($File in $SqlFiles) {
    $Relative = Get-RelativePath -BasePath $BackendRoot -FullPath $File.FullName
    Add-Line ""
    Add-Line "FILE: $Relative"
    Add-Line "SIZE: $($File.Length) bytes"

    foreach ($SqlLine in Get-Content -LiteralPath $File.FullName) {
        Add-Line $SqlLine
    }
}

Add-Line ""
Add-Line "============================================================"
Add-Line "4. IMPORTANT PROJECT FILES"
Add-Line "============================================================"

$ImportantNames = @(
    "go.mod", "README.md", "GENERAL-MARKET-MODULE-MAP.md",
    "Dockerfile", "docker-compose.yml", "compose.yml",
    ".env.example", ".gitignore", ".dockerignore"
)

foreach ($File in ($Files | Where-Object { $ImportantNames -contains $_.Name })) {
    $Relative = Get-RelativePath -BasePath $BackendRoot -FullPath $File.FullName
    Add-Line ""
    Add-Line "FILE: $Relative"
    Add-Line "SIZE: $($File.Length) bytes"

    foreach ($ContentLine in Get-Content -LiteralPath $File.FullName) {
        Add-Line $ContentLine
    }
}

Add-Line ""
Add-Line "============================================================"
Add-Line "5. INVENTORY"
Add-Line "============================================================"
Add-Line "Total files: $($Files.Count)"
Add-Line "Go files: $($GoFiles.Count)"
Add-Line "SQL files: $($SqlFiles.Count)"
Add-Line "Directories: $($Directories.Count)"

foreach ($Group in ($Files | Group-Object Extension | Sort-Object Name)) {
    $ExtensionName = if ($Group.Name) { $Group.Name } else { "(no extension)" }
    Add-Line ("{0}: {1}" -f $ExtensionName, $Group.Count)
}

Add-Line ""
Add-Line "============================================================"
Add-Line "6. GIT STATUS"
Add-Line "============================================================"

$Git = Get-Command git -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue

if ($Git) {
    $GitRoot = & git -C $ProjectRoot rev-parse --show-toplevel 2>$null

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) {
        Add-Line "Repository root: $GitRoot"
        Add-Line "git status --short:"
        foreach ($StatusLine in (& git -C $ProjectRoot status --short 2>&1)) {
            Add-Line ([string]$StatusLine)
        }
    }
    else {
        Add-Line "Git repository not detected."
    }
}
else {
    Add-Line "Git is not installed or is not on PATH."
}

[System.IO.File]::WriteAllLines($ReportPath, $Lines, $Encoding)

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "Report created:" -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host $ReportPath -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "No source files or database objects were changed."

# ============================================================================
# IGD - GENERAL MARKET BACKEND
# PROJECT FORENSIC REPORT EXPORTER
#
# Version: 2.0
# Runtime: Windows PowerShell 5.1+ / PowerShell 7+
#
# PURPOSE:
#   Extract project structure, source code, architecture references,
#   Go packages, tests, Git status and configuration into one TXT report.
#
# SAFETY:
#   - Read-only inspection of project files.
#   - Does not modify Go source files.
#   - Does not execute migrations.
#   - Does not connect to databases.
#   - Does not modify Docker or infrastructure.
#   - Excludes secrets, binaries and large files.
#   - Redacts common secret patterns.
# ============================================================================

Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

# ============================================================================
# 1. CONFIGURATION
# ============================================================================

$ExcludedDirectories = @(
    ".git",
    ".idea",
    ".vscode",
    "vendor",
    "node_modules",
    "bin",
    "obj",
    "dist",
    "build",
    "coverage",
    ".next",
    ".cache",
    "tmp",
    "temp"
)

$ExcludedFileNames = @(
    ".env",
    ".env.local",
    ".env.production",
    ".env.development",
    "id_rsa",
    "id_ed25519",
    "credentials.json",
    "secrets.json"
)

$ExcludedExtensions = @(
    ".exe", ".dll", ".so", ".dylib",
    ".a", ".o", ".obj", ".bin",
    ".zip", ".7z", ".rar", ".gz",
    ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".gif",
    ".webp", ".ico", ".pdf",
    ".mp4", ".mp3", ".wav",
    ".db", ".sqlite", ".sqlite3",
    ".adi", ".iso"
)

$SourceExtensions = @(
    ".go",
    ".ps1",
    ".psm1",
    ".psd1",
    ".sh",
    ".bat",
    ".cmd",
    ".sql",
    ".py",
    ".js",
    ".ts",
    ".tsx",
    ".jsx",
    ".cs",
    ".java",
    ".c",
    ".cpp",
    ".h",
    ".hpp",
    ".rs",
    ".yaml",
    ".yml",
    ".toml",
    ".json",
    ".xml",
    ".conf",
    ".ini",
    ".md"
)

$ManifestNames = @(
    "go.mod",
    "go.sum",

    "docker-compose.yaml",
    "compose.yml",
    "compose.yaml",

    "Taskfile.yml",
    "README.md"
)

$MaxSourceFileBytes = 2MB
$MaxFilesInReport = 10000
$MaxDirectoryDepth = 100

$Timestamp = Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd-HHmmss"
$ReportName = "GENERAL-MARKET-BACKEND-REPORT-$Timestamp.txt"

$ProjectRoot = $null
$ReportPath = $null
$Writer = $null

$GlobalErrors = New-Object 'System.Collections.Generic.List[string]'
$FileInventory = @()
$GoFiles = @()
$ApplicationFiles = @()
$TestFiles = @()

# ============================================================================
# 2. PROJECT ROOT DISCOVERY
# ============================================================================

function Find-ProjectRoot {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)]
        [string]$StartPath
    )

    $Current = (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $StartPath).Path

    while (-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Current)) {

        $GoMod = Join-Path $Current "go.mod"
        $Application = Join-Path $Current "internal\application"

        if (
            (Test-Path -LiteralPath $GoMod -PathType Leaf) -and
            (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Application -PathType Container)
        ) {
            return $Current
        }

        $Parent = Split-Path -Parent $Current

        if (
            [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($Parent) -or
            $Parent -eq $Current
        ) {
            break
        }

        $Current = $Parent
    }

    throw "Project root not found. Run this script inside general-market-backend or one of its subdirectories."
}

# ============================================================================
# 3. REPORT WRITING
# ============================================================================

function Write-Report {
    param(
        [AllowEmptyString()]
        [string]$Text = ""
    )

    if ($null -ne $Writer) {
        $Writer.WriteLine($Text)
    }
}

function Write-Section {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)]
        [string]$Title
    )

    Write-Report ""
    Write-Report ("=" * 100)
    Write-Report $Title
    Write-Report ("=" * 100)
}

function Write-ReportError {
    param(
        [string]$Context,
        [string]$Message
    )

    $Entry = "$Context : $Message"

    $GlobalErrors.Add($Entry)

    Write-Report "[ERROR] $Entry"
}

# ============================================================================
# 4. RELATIVE PATH HANDLING
# ============================================================================

function Get-RelativePath {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)]
        [string]$FullPath
    )

    $RootUri = New-Object System.Uri(
        ($ProjectRoot.TrimEnd('\') + '\')
    )

    $FileUri = New-Object System.Uri($FullPath)

    $Relative = [System.Uri]::UnescapeDataString(
        $RootUri.MakeRelativeUri($FileUri).ToString()
    )

    return $Relative.Replace("/", "\")
}

# ============================================================================
# 5. SECRET REDACTION
# ============================================================================

function Protect-SourceContent {
    param(
        [AllowEmptyString()]
        [string]$Content
    )

    if ([string]::IsNullOrEmpty($Content)) {
        return $Content
    }

    $Patterns = @(
        '(?im)(password|passwd|secret|client_secret|api[_-]?key|access[_-]?token|refresh[_-]?token|private[_-]?key|authorization)(\s*[:=]\s*)(["'']?)[^"'',;\s}]+',
        '(?i)(Bearer\s+)[A-Za-z0-9._~+/=-]+',
        '(?i)(-----BEGIN [A-Z ]*PRIVATE KEY-----)'
    )

    foreach ($Pattern in $Patterns) {

        if ($Pattern -match 'PRIVATE KEY') {

            $Content = [regex]::Replace(
                $Content,
                $Pattern,
                '[PRIVATE KEY REDACTED]'
            )

        }
        elseif ($Pattern -match 'Bearer') {

            $Content = [regex]::Replace(
                $Content,
                $Pattern,
                '$1[REDACTED]'
            )

        }
        else {

            $Content = [regex]::Replace(
                $Content,
                $Pattern,
                '$1$2$3[REDACTED]'
            )
        }
    }

    return $Content
}

# ============================================================================
# 6. FILE EXCLUSION
# ============================================================================

function Test-ExcludedPath {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)]
        [string]$Path
    )

    $Relative = Get-RelativePath -FullPath $Path
    $Parts = $Relative -split '[\\/]'

    foreach ($Part in $Parts) {

        if ($ExcludedDirectories -contains $Part) {
            return $true
        }
    }

    return $false
}

function Test-ExcludedFile {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)]
        [System.IO.FileInfo]$File
    )

    if ($ExcludedFileNames -contains $File.Name) {
        return $true
    }

    if ($ExcludedExtensions -contains $File.Extension.ToLowerInvariant()) {
        return $true
    }

    if ($File.Name -match '(?i)^\.env($|\.)') {
        return $true
    }

    if ($File.Extension -match '(?i)^\.(pem|key|p12|pfx|crt|cer)$') {
        return $true
    }

    if ($File.Length -gt $MaxSourceFileBytes) {
        return $true
    }

    if (Test-ExcludedPath -Path $File.FullName) {
        return $true
    }

    return $false
}

# ============================================================================
# 7. SAFE FILE DISCOVERY
# ============================================================================

function Get-ProjectFiles {

    $Results = New-Object 'System.Collections.Generic.List[object]'

    $Stack = New-Object 'System.Collections.Generic.Stack[object]'

    $Stack.Push(
        [PSCustomObject]@{
            Path = $ProjectRoot
            Depth = 0
        }
    )

    while ($Stack.Count -gt 0) {

        $Current = $Stack.Pop()

        if ($Current.Depth -gt $MaxDirectoryDepth) {
            continue
        }

        try {

            $Entries = @(Get-ChildItem `
                -LiteralPath $Current.Path `
                -Force `
                -ErrorAction Stop)

        }
        catch {

            $GlobalErrors.Add(
                "Directory enumeration failed: $($Current.Path) : $($_.Exception.Message)"
            )

            continue
        }

        foreach ($Entry in $Entries) {

            if ($Entry.PSIsContainer) {

                if ($ExcludedDirectories -contains $Entry.Name) {
                    continue
                }

                $Stack.Push(
                    [PSCustomObject]@{
                        Path = $Entry.FullName
                        Depth = $Current.Depth + 1
                    }
                )

                continue
            }

            if (Test-ExcludedFile -File $Entry) {
                continue
            }

            $Relative = Get-RelativePath -FullPath $Entry.FullName

            $Results.Add(
                [PSCustomObject]@{
                    FullName = $Entry.FullName
                    Relative = $Relative
                    Name = $Entry.Name
                    Extension = $Entry.Extension.ToLowerInvariant()
                    Length = $Entry.Length
                    LastWriteTime = $Entry.LastWriteTime
                }
            )

            if ($Results.Count -ge $MaxFilesInReport) {

                $GlobalErrors.Add(
                    "File limit reached: $MaxFilesInReport. Remaining files were not inventoried."
                )

                return @($Results.ToArray())
            }
        }
    }

    return @($Results.ToArray())
}

# ============================================================================
# 8. SAFE FILE READING
# ============================================================================

function Read-TextFile {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)]
        [string]$Path
    )

    try {

        $Content = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($Path)

        return Protect-SourceContent -Content $Content

    }
    catch {

        Write-ReportError `
            -Context "File read failed: $Path" `
            -Message $_.Exception.Message

        return "[FILE READ ERROR]"
    }
}

function Write-FileContent {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)]
        [PSCustomObject]$File
    )

    Write-Report ""
    Write-Report ("#" * 100)
    Write-Report "FILE: $($File.Relative)"
    Write-Report "SIZE: $($File.Length) bytes"
    Write-Report "LAST MODIFIED: $($File.LastWriteTime)"
    Write-Report ("#" * 100)

    $Content = Read-TextFile -Path $File.FullName

    Write-Report $Content
}

# ============================================================================
# 9. INITIALIZATION
# ============================================================================

$ProjectRoot = Find-ProjectRoot -StartPath (Get-Location).Path

$ReportPath = Join-Path $ProjectRoot $ReportName

if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $ReportPath) {
    throw "Report path already exists. Refusing to overwrite: $ReportPath"
}

$Utf8NoBom = New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)

$Writer = New-Object System.IO.StreamWriter(
    $ReportPath,
    $false,
    $Utf8NoBom
)

# ============================================================================
# 10. MAIN REPORT GENERATION
# ============================================================================

try {

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 10.1 HEADER
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Write-Report "IGD - GENERAL MARKET BACKEND"
    Write-Report "PROJECT STRUCTURE AND SOURCE CODE FORENSIC REPORT"
    Write-Report "Version: 2.0"
    Write-Report "Generated: $(Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss K')"
    Write-Report "Project Root: $ProjectRoot"
    Write-Report "Computer: $env:COMPUTERNAME"
    Write-Report "PowerShell: $($PSVersionTable.PSVersion)"
    Write-Report ""
    Write-Report "READ-ONLY INSPECTION"
    Write-Report "No source files modified."
    Write-Report "No migrations executed."
    Write-Report "No database connections established."
    Write-Report "No infrastructure changes performed."
    Write-Report "Sensitive-looking values redacted where detected."
    Write-Report "Binary files, secrets and excluded directories omitted."

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 10.2 ENVIRONMENT
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Write-Section "1. DEVELOPMENT ENVIRONMENT"

    try {

        $GoVersion = & go version 2>&1

        Write-Report "GO VERSION:"
        Write-Report ($GoVersion | Out-String)

    }
    catch {

        Write-ReportError "Go version" $_.Exception.Message
    }

    try {

        $GoEnv = & go env GOMOD GOPATH GOROOT GOOS GOARCH CGO_ENABLED 2>&1

        Write-Report "GO ENVIRONMENT:"
        Write-Report ($GoEnv | Out-String)

    }
    catch {

        Write-ReportError "Go environment" $_.Exception.Message
    }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 10.3 GIT
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Write-Section "2. GIT STATUS AND HISTORY"

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $ProjectRoot ".git")) {

        $GitCommands = @(
            @{ Title = "GIT STATUS"; Args = @("status", "--short", "--branch") },
            @{ Title = "CURRENT BRANCH"; Args = @("branch", "--show-current") },
            @{ Title = "LATEST 20 COMMITS"; Args = @("log", "-20", "--oneline", "--decorate") },
            @{ Title = "DIFF STAT"; Args = @("diff", "--stat") },
            @{ Title = "STAGED DIFF STAT"; Args = @("diff", "--cached", "--stat") }
        )

        foreach ($Command in $GitCommands) {

            Write-Report ""
            Write-Report $Command.Title

            try {

                $Output = & git -C $ProjectRoot @($Command.Args) 2>&1

                Write-Report ($Output | Out-String)

            }
            catch {

                Write-ReportError $Command.Title $_.Exception.Message
            }
        }

    }
    else {

        Write-Report "No Git repository detected at project root."
    }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 10.4 FILE DISCOVERY
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Write-Section "3. PROJECT FILE DISCOVERY"

    $FileInventory = @(Get-ProjectFiles)

    Write-Report "Included file count: $($FileInventory.Count)"
    Write-Report "Maximum file size: $MaxSourceFileBytes bytes"
    Write-Report "Maximum file count: $MaxFilesInReport"

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 10.5 DIRECTORY STRUCTURE
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Write-Section "4. PROJECT DIRECTORY STRUCTURE"

    $Directories = @(
        Get-ChildItem `
            -LiteralPath $ProjectRoot `
            -Directory `
            -Recurse `
            -Force `
            -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Where-Object {
            -not (Test-ExcludedPath -Path $_.FullName)
        } |
        Sort-Object FullName
    )

    Write-Report "[PROJECT ROOT]"
    Write-Report "."

    foreach ($Directory in $Directories) {

        $Relative = Get-RelativePath -FullPath $Directory.FullName

        Write-Report "DIR  $Relative\"
    }

    foreach ($File in ($FileInventory | Sort-Object Relative)) {

        Write-Report "FILE $($File.Relative) [$($File.Length) bytes]"
    }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 10.6 INVENTORY
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Write-Section "5. FILE INVENTORY AND EXTENSION COUNTS"

    Write-Report "Total files: $($FileInventory.Count)"

    $ExtensionGroups = @(
        $FileInventory | Group-Object Extension | Sort-Object Name
    )

    foreach ($Group in $ExtensionGroups) {

        $ExtensionName = $Group.Name

        if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($ExtensionName)) {
            $ExtensionName = "[NO EXTENSION]"
        }

        Write-Report (
            "{0,-20} {1,8}" -f $ExtensionName, $Group.Count
        )
    }

    Write-Report ""
    Write-Report "FILE DETAILS"

    foreach ($File in ($FileInventory | Sort-Object Relative)) {

        Write-Report (
            "{0} | {1} bytes | {2}" -f
            $File.Relative,
            $File.Length,
            $File.LastWriteTime.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")
        )
    }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 10.7 MANIFESTS
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Write-Section "6. PROJECT MANIFESTS AND BUILD CONFIGURATION"

    $ManifestFiles = @(
        $FileInventory | Where-Object {
            ($ManifestNames -contains $_.Name) -or
            ($_.Name -eq "go.mod") -or
            ($_.Name -eq "go.sum")
        } | Sort-Object Relative
    )

    foreach ($File in $ManifestFiles) {

        Write-FileContent -File $File
    }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 10.8 GO PACKAGES
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Write-Section "7. GO PACKAGE DISCOVERY"

    try {

        Push-Location $ProjectRoot

        try {

            $PackageOutput = & go list ./... 2>&1

            Write-Report ($PackageOutput | Out-String)

        }
        finally {

            Pop-Location
        }

    }
    catch {

        Write-ReportError "Go package discovery" $_.Exception.Message
    }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 10.9 APPLICATION LAYER
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Write-Section "8. APPLICATION LAYER - COMPLETE SOURCE"

    $ApplicationFiles = @(
        $FileInventory | Where-Object {
            $_.Relative -match '(^|\\)internal\\application\\'
        } | Sort-Object Relative
    )

    Write-Report "Application files found: $($ApplicationFiles.Count)"

    if ($ApplicationFiles.Count -eq 0) {

        Write-Report "[WARNING] internal/application source files not found."
    }

    foreach ($File in $ApplicationFiles) {

        Write-FileContent -File $File
    }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 10.10 ALL GO SOURCE
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Write-Section "9. ALL GO SOURCE FILES"

    $GoFiles = @(
        $FileInventory | Where-Object {
            $_.Extension -eq ".go"
        } | Sort-Object Relative
    )

    Write-Report "Total Go source files: $($GoFiles.Count)"

    foreach ($File in $GoFiles) {

        Write-FileContent -File $File
    }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 10.11 OTHER SOURCE AND CONFIGURATION
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Write-Section "10. OTHER SOURCE AND CONFIGURATION FILES"

    $OtherFiles = @(
        $FileInventory | Where-Object {
            $_.Extension -ne ".go" -and
            $_.Extension -in $SourceExtensions -and
            $_.Name -notin $ManifestNames -and
            $_.Name -ne "go.sum" -and
            $_.Name -ne "go.mod"
        } | Sort-Object Relative
    )

    Write-Report "Other source/config files: $($OtherFiles.Count)"

    foreach ($File in $OtherFiles) {

        Write-FileContent -File $File
    }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 10.12 ARCHITECTURAL MODULE INVENTORY
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Write-Section "11. ARCHITECTURAL MODULE INVENTORY"

    $ImportantPaths = @(
        "internal\application",
        "internal\market",
        "internal\catalogue",
        "internal\marketflow",
        "internal\orders",
        "internal\matching",
        "internal\trades",
        "internal\execution",
        "internal\offerconfig",
        "internal\eligibility",
        "internal\commodity",
        "internal\infrastructure",
        "internal\interfaces",
        "cmd",
        "config",
        "scripts",
        "migrations"
    )

    foreach ($Pattern in $ImportantPaths) {

        Write-Report ""
        Write-Report "MODULE/PATH: $Pattern"

        $Matched = @(
            $FileInventory | Where-Object {
                $_.Relative -like "$Pattern\*" -or
                $_.Relative -eq $Pattern
            }
        )

        if ($Matched.Count -eq 0) {

            Write-Report "  No matching files found."

        }
        else {

            foreach ($Item in $Matched) {

                Write-Report "  $($Item.Relative)"
            }
        }
    }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 10.13 TEST DISCOVERY
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Write-Section "12. GO TEST FILES AND TEST FUNCTION INDEX"

    $TestFiles = @(
        $GoFiles | Where-Object {
            $_.Name -like "*_test.go"
        } | Sort-Object Relative
    )

    Write-Report "Total Go test files: $($TestFiles.Count)"

    foreach ($File in $TestFiles) {

        Write-Report ""
        Write-Report "TEST FILE: $($File.Relative)"

        try {

            $Content = Read-TextFile -Path $File.FullName

            $TestFunctions = @(
                [regex]::Matches(
                    $Content,
                    '(?m)^func\s+(Test|Benchmark|Example)[A-Za-z0-9_]*'
                )
            )

            if ($TestFunctions.Count -eq 0) {

                Write-Report "  No test functions discovered."

            }
            else {

                foreach ($Match in $TestFunctions) {

                    Write-Report "  $($Match.Value)"
                }
            }

        }
        catch {

            Write-ReportError "Test discovery: $($File.Relative)" $_.Exception.Message
        }
    }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 10.14 ARCHITECTURAL KEYWORD INDEX
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Write-Section "13. ARCHITECTURAL KEYWORD INDEX"

    $Keywords = @(
        "Transaction",
        "Repository",
        "Gateway",
        "Validator",
        "Idempotency",
        "Correlation",
        "Audit",
        "ExpectedVersion",
        "Revision",
        "GetForUpdate",
        "Save",
        "context.Context",
        "context.Canceled",
        "context.DeadlineExceeded",
        "Authorize",
        "Permission",
        "Reserve",
        "Release",
        "Matching",
        "MatchOrders",
        "Execution",
        "Settlement",
        "Contract",
        "Delivery"
    )

    foreach ($Keyword in $Keywords) {

        Write-Report ""
        Write-Report "KEYWORD: $Keyword"

        $Found = 0

        foreach ($File in $GoFiles) {

            try {

                $Matches = @(
                    Select-String `
                        -LiteralPath $File.FullName `
                        -Pattern ([regex]::Escape($Keyword)) `
                        -AllMatches `
                        -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                )

                foreach ($Match in $Matches) {

                    Write-Report (
                        "  {0}:{1}: {2}" -f
                        $File.Relative,
                        $Match.LineNumber,
                        $Match.Line.Trim()
                    )

                    $Found++

                    if ($Found -ge 40) {
                        break
                    }
                }

            }
            catch {

                continue
            }

            if ($Found -ge 40) {

                Write-Report "  [Additional matches omitted.]"
                break
            }
        }

        if ($Found -eq 0) {

            Write-Report "  No matches found."
        }
    }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 10.15 DATABASE AND MIGRATION INVENTORY
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Write-Section "14. DATABASE AND MIGRATION INVENTORY"

    $DatabaseFiles = @(
        $FileInventory | Where-Object {
            $_.Extension -eq ".sql" -or
            $_.Relative -match '(?i)migration|migrations|schema'
        } | Sort-Object Relative
    )

    Write-Report "Database-related files: $($DatabaseFiles.Count)"
    Write-Report "SQL file content intentionally excluded."

    foreach ($File in $DatabaseFiles) {

        Write-Report (
            "{0} | {1} bytes | {2}" -f
            $File.Relative,
            $File.Length,
            $File.LastWriteTime.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")
        )
    }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 10.16 DOCKER AND INFRASTRUCTURE INVENTORY
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Write-Section "15. DOCKER AND INFRASTRUCTURE FILE INVENTORY"

    $DockerFiles = @(
        $FileInventory | Where-Object {
            $_.Name -match '(?i)docker|compose|container'
        } | Sort-Object Relative
    )

    Write-Report "Docker-related files: $($DockerFiles.Count)"

    foreach ($File in $DockerFiles) {

        Write-Report $File.Relative
    }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 10.17 ERRORS
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Write-Section "16. REPORT GENERATION ERRORS AND WARNINGS"

    if ($GlobalErrors.Count -eq 0) {

        Write-Report "No report-generation errors were recorded."

    }
    else {

        Write-Report "Total errors/warnings: $($GlobalErrors.Count)"

        foreach ($ErrorEntry in $GlobalErrors) {

            Write-Report $ErrorEntry
        }
    }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 10.18 SUMMARY
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Write-Section "17. FINAL SUMMARY"

    Write-Report "Project Root: $ProjectRoot"
    Write-Report "Included Files: $($FileInventory.Count)"
    Write-Report "Go Source Files: $($GoFiles.Count)"
    Write-Report "Application Files: $($ApplicationFiles.Count)"
    Write-Report "Go Test Files: $($TestFiles.Count)"
    Write-Report "Other Source/Configuration Files: $($OtherFiles.Count)"
    Write-Report "Database/Migration Files: $($DatabaseFiles.Count)"
    Write-Report "Docker Files: $($DockerFiles.Count)"
    Write-Report "Report Errors/Warnings: $($GlobalErrors.Count)"
    Write-Report ""
    Write-Report "Report Path: $ReportPath"
    Write-Report ""
    Write-Report "END OF REPORT"

}
catch {

    $FatalMessage = $_.Exception.Message

    try {

        Write-Section "FATAL REPORT GENERATION ERROR"
        Write-Report $FatalMessage
        Write-Report $_.ScriptStackTrace

    }
    catch {
        # Preserve the original failure.
    }

    throw

}
finally {

    if ($null -ne $Writer) {

        $Writer.Flush()
        $Writer.Dispose()

        $Writer = $null
    }
}

# ============================================================================
# 11. FINAL CONSOLE OUTPUT
# ============================================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "GENERAL MARKET BACKEND REPORT GENERATED" -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "============================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "Project Root:" -ForegroundColor White
Write-Host $ProjectRoot -ForegroundColor Gray

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "Report File:" -ForegroundColor White
Write-Host $ReportPath -ForegroundColor Yellow

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "No project source files were modified." -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "No database or migration operations were executed." -ForegroundColor Green

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan

# Open report using the default text editor.
try {

    Invoke-Item -LiteralPath $ReportPath

}
catch {

    Write-Warning "Could not open report automatically. Open it manually."
}

# ============================================================================
# END
# ============================================================================


####################################################################################################
FILE: scripts\Fix-CatalogueValidationOrder.ps1
SIZE: 3327 bytes
LAST MODIFIED: 10/27/2026 11:01:42
####################################################################################################

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
Set-StrictMode -Version Latest

$ProjectRoot = (Get-Location).Path

if (!(Test-Path (Join-Path $ProjectRoot "go.mod"))) {
    $nested = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "general-market-backend"

    if (Test-Path (Join-Path $nested "go.mod")) {
        $ProjectRoot = $nested
    }
    else {
        throw "Run this script from general-market-backend or its parent directory."
    }
}

$File = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "internal\catalogue\handler.go"

if (!(Test-Path $File)) {
    throw "Handler file not found: $File"
}

$Source = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($File)

$oldBlock = '(?s)\tif h\.DB == nil \{\r?\n\t\twriteError\(w, http\.StatusServiceUnavailable, "database_unavailable", "Catalogue storage is unavailable\."\)\r?\n\t\treturn\r?\n\t\}\r?\n\r?\n(?=\tpage, err := parsePositiveInt)'

$anchorPattern = '(?m)^\tcategoryID := strings\.TrimSpace\(r\.URL\.Query\(\)\.Get\("category_id"\)\)\r?\n\tmarketID := strings\.TrimSpace\(r\.URL\.Query\(\)\.Get\("market_id"\)\)'

$oldMatches = [regex]::Matches($Source, $oldBlock)

if ($oldMatches.Count -ne 1) {
    throw "Expected database check block was not found exactly once. No changes written."
}

$Candidate = [regex]::Replace($Source, $oldBlock, "", 1)

$anchorMatches = [regex]::Matches($Candidate, $anchorPattern)

if ($anchorMatches.Count -ne 1) {
    throw "Expected category and market query lines were not found exactly once. No changes written."
}

$anchorMatch = $anchorMatches[0]

$Newline = "`r`n"
if (!$Source.Contains("`r`n")) {
    $Newline = "`n"
}

$dbCheck = @'
    if h.DB == nil {
        writeError(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, "database_unavailable", "Catalogue storage is unavailable.")
        return
    }
'@

$insert = $anchorMatch.Value + $Newline + $Newline + $dbCheck.TrimEnd("`r", "`n")

$Candidate = $Candidate.Remove(
    $anchorMatch.Index,
    $anchorMatch.Length
).Insert(
    $anchorMatch.Index,
    $insert
)

$Backup = "$File.backup-$(Get-Date -Format 'yyyyMMdd-HHmmss')"
[System.IO.File]::Copy($File, $Backup, $false)

$Utf8NoBom = New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)

Push-Location $ProjectRoot

try {
    [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($File, $Candidate, $Utf8NoBom)

    & gofmt -w "internal\catalogue\handler.go"

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "gofmt failed."
    }

    Write-Host "`n--- Catalogue tests ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    & go test ./internal/catalogue

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Catalogue tests failed."
    }

    Write-Host "`n--- All project tests ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    & go test ./...

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "Project tests failed."
    }

    Write-Host "`n--- Static analysis ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    & go vet ./...

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "go vet failed."
    }

    Write-Host "`nSUCCESS: all requested checks passed." -ForegroundColor Green
    Write-Host "Backup: $Backup"
}
catch {
    [System.IO.File]::Copy($Backup, $File, $true)
    & gofmt -w "internal\catalogue\handler.go"
    Write-Host "The original handler was restored because validation failed." -ForegroundColor Yellow
    Write-Host "Backup: $Backup"
    throw
}
finally {
    Pop-Location
}

$GoModule = (Get-Content (Join-Path $Root "go.mod") -TotalCount 1).Trim()

if ($GoModule -ne "module $ModulePath") {

        throw "Target file already exists: $File. No existing file will be overwritten."

Push-Location $Root
