$CreatedFiles = @()

function Write-GoFile {
    param(
        [string]$RelativePath,
        [string]$Content
    )

    $FullPath = Join-Path $Root $RelativePath
    $Parent = Split-Path -Parent $FullPath

    if (-not (Test-Path $Parent)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $Parent -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path $FullPath) {
        throw "Refusing to overwrite existing file: $RelativePath"
    }

    $Encoding = New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)
    [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($FullPath, $Content, $Encoding)

    $CreatedFiles += $FullPath
    Write-Host "Created: $RelativePath" -ForegroundColor Green
}

$Ports = @'
package application

import (
    "context"
    "time"

    cataloguedomain "github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/catalogue/domain"
    marketdomain "github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/market/domain"
    "github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/marketflow"
)

// TransactionManager must bind all repositories used inside the callback
// to one database transaction. It must commit or roll back atomically.
type TransactionManager interface {
    WithinTransaction(context.Context, func(context.Context) error) error
}

type Clock interface {
    Now() time.Time
}

type IDGenerator interface {
    NewID(context.Context, string) (marketflow.ID, error)
}

// Market and Catalogue integration.

type MarketReader interface {
    GetMarket(context.Context, marketdomain.MarketID) (*marketdomain.Market, error)
}

type CategoryReader interface {
    GetCategory(context.Context, cataloguedomain.CategoryID) (*cataloguedomain.Category, error)
}

type CategoryMappingReader interface {
    GetMapping(
        context.Context,
        marketdomain.MarketID,
        cataloguedomain.CategoryID,
        cataloguedomain.CategoryID,
    ) (*cataloguedomain.CategoryMapping, error)
}

// Offer readiness must validate the immutable configuration version,
// parameter values, required documents and specialized qualification.
type OfferBusinessValidator interface {
    ValidateConfigurationAndEligibility(context.Context, marketflow.Offer) error
}

// Supply request repositories must lock rows and enforce expected revisions.

type SupplyRequestCommandRepository interface {
    GetForUpdate(context.Context, marketflow.ID) (marketflow.SupplyRequest, uint64, error)
    Save(context.Context, marketflow.SupplyRequest, uint64) error
}

type ProposalCommandRepository interface {
    GetForUpdate(context.Context, marketflow.ID) (marketflow.SupplyProposal, uint64, error)
    Save(context.Context, marketflow.SupplyProposal, uint64) error
}

type ProposalValidator interface {
    ValidateProposal(context.Context, marketflow.SupplyRequest, marketflow.SupplyProposal) error
}

// Order resource reservation must be idempotent by key.
// The adapter must support recovery of reservations whose order write
// did not complete. Buy and sell reservations are handled according to side.
type OrderResourceGateway interface {
    ReserveForOrder(context.Context, marketflow.Order, int64, string) (marketflow.ID, error)
    ReleaseOrderResources(context.Context, marketflow.ID, string) error
}

type OrderAdmissionValidator interface {
    ValidateOrder(context.Context, marketflow.Order) error
}

type OrderCommandRepository interface {
    GetByIdempotencyKey(context.Context, string) (marketflow.Order, bool, error)
    CreateOrder(context.Context, marketflow.Order, string, marketflow.ID) (marketflow.Order, error)
}

// The following methods must execute in the same transaction.
// LockBestCrossingPair must lock both orders and return a valid crossing pair.
type OrderBookCommandRepository interface {
    LockBestCrossingPair(context.Context, marketflow.ID, marketflow.ID) (marketflow.Order, marketflow.Order, error)
    SaveOrder(context.Context, marketflow.Order, uint64) error
    SaveMatch(context.Context, MatchRecord, string) error
}

type ExecutionProgressCommandRepository interface {
    GetForUpdate(context.Context, marketflow.ID) (marketflow.ExecutionProgress, uint64, error)
    Save(context.Context, marketflow.ExecutionProgress, uint64) error
}

type TradeCommandRepository interface {
    GetForUpdate(context.Context, marketflow.ID) (marketflow.Trade, error)
    Save(context.Context, marketflow.Trade, uint64) error
}

// LegalGateway adapters must enforce the request's idempotency key,
// authorized scope and correlation identifier at the external boundary.
type LegalGateway interface {
    SubmitAuthorizedRequest(context.Context, marketflow.ExternalRequest) (string, error)
    GetRequestResult(context.Context, string) (string, error)
}

// MatchRecord is an immutable result of one committed match.
type MatchRecord struct {
    ID          marketflow.ID
    MarketID    marketflow.ID
    OfferID     marketflow.ID
    BuyOrderID  marketflow.ID
    SellOrderID marketflow.ID
    BuyerID     marketflow.ID
    SellerID    marketflow.ID
    Quantity    int64
    Price       int64
    CreatedAt   time.Time
}
'@

$Services = @'
package application

import (
    "context"
    "errors"
    "fmt"
    "math"
    "strings"
    "time"

    cataloguedomain "github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/catalogue/domain"
    marketdomain "github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/market/domain"
    "github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/marketflow"
)

var (
    ErrDependencyMissing = errors.New("application dependency missing")
    ErrVersionConflict = errors.New("application version conflict")
    ErrIdempotencyConflict = errors.New("idempotency key conflict")
    ErrNoCrossingOrders = errors.New("no crossing orders")
    ErrNotSettlementReady = errors.New("trade is not ready for settlement")
    ErrInvalidPolicyGraph = errors.New("invalid execution policy dependency graph")
    ErrInvalidTradeDimension = errors.New("invalid trade status dimension")
)

type MarketCatalogueGuard struct {
    Markets   MarketReader
    Categories CategoryReader
    Mappings  CategoryMappingReader
}

func (g MarketCatalogueGuard) ValidateOffer(ctx context.Context, offer marketflow.Offer) error {
    if g.Markets == nil || g.Categories == nil || g.Mappings == nil {
        return ErrDependencyMissing
    }

    market, err := g.Markets.GetMarket(ctx, marketdomain.MarketID(offer.MarketID))
    if err != nil {
        return err
    }
    if market == nil || !market.IsOperational() {
        return marketflow.ErrNotEligible
    }

    publicCategory, err := g.Categories.GetCategory(
        ctx,
        cataloguedomain.CategoryID(offer.PublicCategoryID),
    )
    if err != nil {
        return err
    }

    specializedCategory, err := g.Categories.GetCategory(
        ctx,
        cataloguedomain.CategoryID(offer.SpecializedCategoryID),
    )
    if err != nil {
        return err
    }

    if publicCategory == nil ||
        specializedCategory == nil ||
        !publicCategory.IsSelectable() ||
        !specializedCategory.IsSelectable() ||
        publicCategory.Kind() != cataloguedomain.CategoryKindGeneral ||
        specializedCategory.Kind() != cataloguedomain.CategoryKindSpecialized {
        return marketflow.ErrNotEligible
    }

    mapping, err := g.Mappings.GetMapping(
        ctx,
        marketdomain.MarketID(offer.MarketID),
        cataloguedomain.CategoryID(offer.PublicCategoryID),
        cataloguedomain.CategoryID(offer.SpecializedCategoryID),
    )
    if err != nil {
        return err
    }

    if mapping == nil || !mapping.IsUsable() {
        return marketflow.ErrNotEligible
    }

    return nil
}

type OfferReadiness interface {
    ValidateOffer(context.Context, marketflow.Offer) error
}

type CompositeOfferReadiness struct {
    Catalogue MarketCatalogueGuard
    Business  OfferBusinessValidator
}

func (v CompositeOfferReadiness) ValidateOffer(
    ctx context.Context,
    offer marketflow.Offer,
) error {
    if v.Business == nil {
        return ErrDependencyMissing
    }
    if err := v.Catalogue.ValidateOffer(ctx, offer); err != nil {
        return err
    }
    return v.Business.ValidateConfigurationAndEligibility(ctx, offer)
}

type OfferCommand struct {
    ActorID         marketflow.ID
    OfferID         marketflow.ID
    ExpectedVersion uint64
    CorrelationID   marketflow.ID
}

type OfferLifecycleService struct {
    Transactions TransactionManager
    Offers       marketflow.OfferRepository
    Identity     marketflow.IdentityGateway
    Readiness    OfferReadiness
    Audit        marketflow.AuditRepository
    IDs          IDGenerator
    Clock        Clock
}

func (s *OfferLifecycleService) SubmitForReview(ctx context.Context, cmd OfferCommand) (marketflow.Offer, error) {
    return s.transition(ctx, cmd, "offers.submit", "submit_for_review")
}

func (s *OfferLifecycleService) Approve(ctx context.Context, cmd OfferCommand) (marketflow.Offer, error) {
    return s.transition(ctx, cmd, "offers.approve", "approve")
}

func (s *OfferLifecycleService) Publish(ctx context.Context, cmd OfferCommand) (marketflow.Offer, error) {
    return s.transition(ctx, cmd, "offers.publish", "publish")
}

func (s *OfferLifecycleService) transition(
    ctx context.Context,
    cmd OfferCommand,
    permission string,
    action string,
) (marketflow.Offer, error) {
    if s == nil || s.Transactions == nil || s.Offers == nil ||
        s.Identity == nil || s.Readiness == nil ||
        s.Audit == nil || s.IDs == nil || s.Clock == nil {
        return marketflow.Offer{}, ErrDependencyMissing
    }
    if strings.TrimSpace(string(cmd.ActorID)) == "" ||
        strings.TrimSpace(string(cmd.OfferID)) == "" ||
        strings.TrimSpace(string(cmd.CorrelationID)) == "" ||
        cmd.ExpectedVersion == 0 {
        return marketflow.Offer{}, marketflow.ErrInvalidInput
    }

    if err := s.Identity.VerifyIdentity(ctx, cmd.ActorID); err != nil {
        return marketflow.Offer{}, err
    }
    if err := s.Identity.Authorize(ctx, cmd.ActorID, permission); err != nil {
        return marketflow.Offer{}, err
    }

    var result marketflow.Offer

    err := s.Transactions.WithinTransaction(ctx, func(txctx context.Context) error {
        offer, err := s.Offers.GetOffer(txctx, cmd.OfferID)
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }
        if offer.Version != cmd.ExpectedVersion {
            return ErrVersionConflict
        }

        previousVersion := offer.Version
        now := s.Clock.Now().UTC()

        switch action {
        case "submit_for_review":
            if err := s.Readiness.ValidateOffer(txctx, offer); err != nil {
                return err
            }
            err = offer.SubmitForReview(now)
        case "approve":
            err = offer.Approve(now)
        case "publish":
            if err := s.Readiness.ValidateOffer(txctx, offer); err != nil {
                return err
            }
            err = offer.Publish(now)
        default:
            return marketflow.ErrInvalidInput
        }
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }

        if err := s.Offers.SaveOffer(txctx, offer, previousVersion); err != nil {
            return err
        }

        eventID, err := s.IDs.NewID(txctx, "audit")
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }

        event := marketflow.AuditEvent{
            ID:              eventID,
            AggregateType:   "offer",
            AggregateID:     offer.ID,
            Action:          action,
            ActorID:         cmd.ActorID,
            CorrelationID:   cmd.CorrelationID,
            PreviousVersion: previousVersion,
            NewVersion:      offer.Version,
            OccurredAt:      now,
        }
        if err := s.Audit.Append(txctx, event); err != nil {
            return err
        }

        result = offer
        return nil
    })

    return result, err
}

// Supply request selection.

type SelectProposalCommand struct {
    ActorID         marketflow.ID
    RequestID       marketflow.ID
    ProposalID      marketflow.ID
    ExpectedVersion uint64
    CorrelationID   marketflow.ID
}

type SupplyRequestService struct {
    Transactions TransactionManager
    Requests     SupplyRequestCommandRepository
    Proposals    ProposalCommandRepository
    Validator    ProposalValidator
    Identity     marketflow.IdentityGateway
    Audit        marketflow.AuditRepository
    IDs          IDGenerator
    Clock        Clock
}

func (s *SupplyRequestService) SelectProposal(
    ctx context.Context,
    cmd SelectProposalCommand,
) (marketflow.SupplyRequest, error) {
    if s == nil || s.Transactions == nil || s.Requests == nil ||
        s.Proposals == nil || s.Validator == nil || s.Identity == nil ||
        s.Audit == nil || s.IDs == nil || s.Clock == nil {
        return marketflow.SupplyRequest{}, ErrDependencyMissing
    }
    if cmd.ExpectedVersion == 0 ||
        strings.TrimSpace(string(cmd.ActorID)) == "" ||
        strings.TrimSpace(string(cmd.RequestID)) == "" ||
        strings.TrimSpace(string(cmd.ProposalID)) == "" ||
        strings.TrimSpace(string(cmd.CorrelationID)) == "" {
        return marketflow.SupplyRequest{}, marketflow.ErrInvalidInput
    }

    if err := s.Identity.VerifyIdentity(ctx, cmd.ActorID); err != nil {
        return marketflow.SupplyRequest{}, err
    }
    if err := s.Identity.Authorize(ctx, cmd.ActorID, "supply_requests.select_proposal"); err != nil {
        return marketflow.SupplyRequest{}, err
    }

    var result marketflow.SupplyRequest

    err := s.Transactions.WithinTransaction(ctx, func(txctx context.Context) error {
        request, requestRevision, err := s.Requests.GetForUpdate(txctx, cmd.RequestID)
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }
        if requestRevision != cmd.ExpectedVersion {
            return ErrVersionConflict
        }

        proposal, proposalRevision, err := s.Proposals.GetForUpdate(txctx, cmd.ProposalID)
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }

        if err := s.Validator.ValidateProposal(txctx, request, proposal); err != nil {
            return err
        }
        if err := marketflow.SelectProposal(&request, proposal); err != nil {
            return err
        }

        proposal.State = marketflow.ProposalAccepted

        if err := s.Proposals.Save(txctx, proposal, proposalRevision); err != nil {
            return err
        }
        if err := s.Requests.Save(txctx, request, requestRevision); err != nil {
            return err
        }

        eventID, err := s.IDs.NewID(txctx, "audit")
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }

        if err := s.Audit.Append(txctx, marketflow.AuditEvent{
            ID:              eventID,
            AggregateType:   "supply_request",
            AggregateID:     request.ID,
            Action:          "proposal_selected",
            ActorID:         cmd.ActorID,
            CorrelationID:   cmd.CorrelationID,
            PreviousVersion: requestRevision,
            NewVersion:      requestRevision + 1,
            OccurredAt:      s.Clock.Now().UTC(),
            Metadata: map[string]string{
                "proposal_id": string(proposal.ID),
            },
        }); err != nil {
            return err
        }

        result = request
        return nil
    })

    return result, err
}

// Order admission and resource reservation.

type PlaceOrderCommand struct {
    ActorID        marketflow.ID
    Order          marketflow.Order
    IdempotencyKey string
    CorrelationID  marketflow.ID
}

type OrderAdmissionService struct {
    Transactions TransactionManager
    Orders       OrderCommandRepository
    Validator    OrderAdmissionValidator
    Resources    OrderResourceGateway
    Identity     marketflow.IdentityGateway
    Audit        marketflow.AuditRepository
    IDs          IDGenerator
    Clock        Clock
}

func CalculateOrderNotional(order marketflow.Order) (int64, error) {
    if order.Side != marketflow.Buy {
        return 0, nil
    }
    if order.Price <= 0 || order.Quantity <= 0 {
        return 0, marketflow.ErrInvalidInput
    }
    if order.Price > math.MaxInt64/order.Quantity {
        return 0, marketflow.ErrInvalidInput
    }
    return order.Price * order.Quantity, nil
}

func sameOrderIntent(a, b marketflow.Order) bool {
    return a.ID == b.ID &&
        a.OfferID == b.OfferID &&
        a.MarketID == b.MarketID &&
        a.UserID == b.UserID &&
        a.Side == b.Side &&
        a.Price == b.Price &&
        a.Quantity == b.Quantity &&
        a.CreatedAt.Equal(b.CreatedAt)
}

func (s *OrderAdmissionService) Place(
    ctx context.Context,
    cmd PlaceOrderCommand,
) (marketflow.Order, error) {
    if s == nil || s.Transactions == nil || s.Orders == nil ||
        s.Validator == nil || s.Resources == nil ||
        s.Identity == nil || s.Audit == nil ||
        s.IDs == nil || s.Clock == nil {
        return marketflow.Order{}, ErrDependencyMissing
    }

    key := strings.TrimSpace(cmd.IdempotencyKey)
    if key == "" || len(key) > 200 ||
        strings.TrimSpace(string(cmd.ActorID)) == "" ||
        strings.TrimSpace(string(cmd.CorrelationID)) == "" {
        return marketflow.Order{}, marketflow.ErrInvalidInput
    }

    order := cmd.Order
    if err := order.Validate(); err != nil {
        return marketflow.Order{}, err
    }
    if order.UserID != cmd.ActorID || order.State != marketflow.OrderPending {
        return marketflow.Order{}, marketflow.ErrNotEligible
    }

    if err := s.Identity.VerifyIdentity(ctx, cmd.ActorID); err != nil {
        return marketflow.Order{}, err
    }
    if err := s.Identity.Authorize(ctx, cmd.ActorID, "orders.place"); err != nil {
        return marketflow.Order{}, err
    }
    if err := s.Validator.ValidateOrder(ctx, order); err != nil {
        return marketflow.Order{}, err
    }

    // Fast idempotency check avoids repeating an external reservation.
    existing, found, err := s.Orders.GetByIdempotencyKey(ctx, key)
    if err != nil {
        return marketflow.Order{}, err
    }
    if found {
        if !sameOrderIntent(existing, order) {
            return marketflow.Order{}, ErrIdempotencyConflict
        }
        return existing, nil
    }

    notional, err := CalculateOrderNotional(order)
    if err != nil {
        return marketflow.Order{}, err
    }

    reservationID, err := s.Resources.ReserveForOrder(ctx, order, notional, key)
    if err != nil {
        return marketflow.Order{}, err
    }
    if strings.TrimSpace(string(reservationID)) == "" {
        return marketflow.Order{}, marketflow.ErrInvalidInput
    }

    var result marketflow.Order
    replayed := false

    txErr := s.Transactions.WithinTransaction(ctx, func(txctx context.Context) error {
        current, exists, err := s.Orders.GetByIdempotencyKey(txctx, key)
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }
        if exists {
            if !sameOrderIntent(current, order) {
                return ErrIdempotencyConflict
            }
            replayed = true
            result = current
            return nil
        }

        orderToSave := order
        if err := orderToSave.Open(); err != nil {
            return err
        }

        created, err := s.Orders.CreateOrder(
            txctx,
            orderToSave,
            key,
            reservationID,
        )
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }

        eventID, err := s.IDs.NewID(txctx, "audit")
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }

        if err := s.Audit.Append(txctx, marketflow.AuditEvent{
            ID:              eventID,
            AggregateType:   "order",
            AggregateID:     created.ID,
            Action:          "placed",
            ActorID:         cmd.ActorID,
            CorrelationID:   cmd.CorrelationID,
            PreviousVersion: order.Version,
            NewVersion:      created.Version,
            OccurredAt:      s.Clock.Now().UTC(),
            Metadata: map[string]string{
                "reservation_id": string(reservationID),
                "idempotency_key": key,
            },
        }); err != nil {
            return err
        }

        result = created
        return nil
    })

    if txErr != nil && !replayed {
        releaseErr := s.Resources.ReleaseOrderResources(
            ctx,
            reservationID,
            key,
        )
        if releaseErr != nil {
            return marketflow.Order{}, errors.Join(
                txErr,
                fmt.Errorf("reservation compensation failed: %w", releaseErr),
            )
        }
        return marketflow.Order{}, txErr
    }

    return result, txErr
}

// Matching is performed only inside a transaction that locks both orders.
// MatchOrders mutates local copies; those copies are persisted atomically here.

type MatchingService struct {
    Transactions TransactionManager
    Book         OrderBookCommandRepository
    IDs          IDGenerator
    Clock        Clock
}

func (s *MatchingService) MatchNext(
    ctx context.Context,
    marketID marketflow.ID,
    offerID marketflow.ID,
) (MatchRecord, error) {
    if s == nil || s.Transactions == nil || s.Book == nil ||
        s.IDs == nil || s.Clock == nil {
        return MatchRecord{}, ErrDependencyMissing
    }
    if strings.TrimSpace(string(marketID)) == "" ||
        strings.TrimSpace(string(offerID)) == "" {
        return MatchRecord{}, marketflow.ErrInvalidInput
    }

    var result MatchRecord

    err := s.Transactions.WithinTransaction(ctx, func(txctx context.Context) error {
        buy, sell, err := s.Book.LockBestCrossingPair(txctx, marketID, offerID)
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }

        originalBuyVersion := buy.Version
        originalSellVersion := sell.Version

        match, err := marketflow.MatchOrders(&buy, &sell)
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }

        matchID, err := s.IDs.NewID(txctx, "match")
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }

        now := s.Clock.Now().UTC()

        record := MatchRecord{
            ID:          matchID,
            MarketID:    buy.MarketID,
            OfferID:     buy.OfferID,
            BuyOrderID:  match.BuyOrderID,
            SellOrderID: match.SellOrderID,
            BuyerID:     buy.UserID,
            SellerID:    sell.UserID,
            Quantity:    match.Quantity,
            Price:       match.Price,
            CreatedAt:   now,
        }

        if err := s.Book.SaveOrder(txctx, buy, originalBuyVersion); err != nil {
            return err
        }
        if err := s.Book.SaveOrder(txctx, sell, originalSellVersion); err != nil {
            return err
        }
        if err := s.Book.SaveMatch(txctx, record, "match:"+string(matchID)); err != nil {
            return err
        }

        result = record
        return nil
    })

    if errors.Is(err, ErrNoCrossingOrders) {
        return MatchRecord{}, ErrNoCrossingOrders
    }
    return result, err
}

// Trade state dimensions are updated independently.
// An external contract/service/delivery response must be verified before
// the corresponding status update is submitted.

type TradeDimension string

const (
    DimensionContract TradeDimension = "contract"
    DimensionServices TradeDimension = "services"
    DimensionDelivery TradeDimension = "delivery"
)

type TradeStatusCommand struct {
    TradeID         marketflow.ID
    ExpectedVersion uint64
    Dimension       TradeDimension
    Value           string
}

type TradeWorkflowService struct {
    Transactions TransactionManager
    Trades       TradeCommandRepository
    Clock        Clock
}

func (s *TradeWorkflowService) UpdateStatus(
    ctx context.Context,
    cmd TradeStatusCommand,
) (marketflow.Trade, error) {
    if s == nil || s.Transactions == nil || s.Trades == nil || s.Clock == nil {
        return marketflow.Trade{}, ErrDependencyMissing
    }
    if cmd.ExpectedVersion == 0 || strings.TrimSpace(string(cmd.TradeID)) == "" {
        return marketflow.Trade{}, marketflow.ErrInvalidInput
    }

    var result marketflow.Trade

    err := s.Transactions.WithinTransaction(ctx, func(txctx context.Context) error {
        trade, err := s.Trades.GetForUpdate(txctx, cmd.TradeID)
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }
        if trade.Version != cmd.ExpectedVersion {
            return ErrVersionConflict
        }

        switch cmd.Dimension {
        case DimensionContract:
            value := marketflow.ContractState(cmd.Value)
            switch value {
            case marketflow.ContractPending,
                marketflow.ContractCreated,
                marketflow.ContractFailed,
                marketflow.ContractNotRequired:
                trade.Contract = value
            default:
                return ErrInvalidTradeDimension
            }

        case DimensionServices:
            value := marketflow.ServiceState(cmd.Value)
            switch value {
            case marketflow.ServicePending,
                marketflow.ServiceInProgress,
                marketflow.ServiceCompleted,
                marketflow.ServiceFailed,
                marketflow.ServiceNotRequired:
                trade.Services = value
            default:
                return ErrInvalidTradeDimension
            }

        case DimensionDelivery:
            value := marketflow.DeliveryState(cmd.Value)
            switch value {
            case marketflow.DeliveryPending,
                marketflow.DeliveryConfirmed,
                marketflow.DeliveryFailed,
                marketflow.DeliveryNotRequired:
                trade.Delivery = value
            default:
                return ErrInvalidTradeDimension
            }

        default:
            return ErrInvalidTradeDimension
        }

        trade.RecalculateSettlementReadiness()
        trade.Version++
        trade.UpdatedAt = s.Clock.Now().UTC()

        if err := trade.Validate(); err != nil {
            return err
        }
        if err := s.Trades.Save(txctx, trade, cmd.ExpectedVersion); err != nil {
            return err
        }

        result = trade
        return nil
    })

    return result, err
}

func RequireSettlementReadiness(trade marketflow.Trade) error {
    if err := trade.Validate(); err != nil {
        return err
    }
    trade.RecalculateSettlementReadiness()
    if trade.SettlementReadiness != marketflow.SettlementReady {
        return ErrNotSettlementReady
    }
    return nil
}

// Execution policy validation includes cycle detection and ordering checks.

func ValidateExecutionPolicy(policy marketflow.ExecutionPolicy) error {
    if err := policy.Validate(); err != nil {
        return err
    }

    steps := make(map[string]marketflow.ExecutionStep, len(policy.Steps))
    orders := make(map[int]bool, len(policy.Steps))

    for _, step := range policy.Steps {
        if orders[step.Order] {
            return ErrInvalidPolicyGraph
        }
        orders[step.Order] = true
        steps[step.Code] = step
    }

    state := make(map[string]uint8, len(steps))

    var visit func(string) error
    visit = func(code string) error {
        switch state[code] {
        case 1:
            return ErrInvalidPolicyGraph
        case 2:
            return nil
        }

        step, exists := steps[code]
        if !exists {
            return ErrInvalidPolicyGraph
        }

        state[code] = 1

        for _, dependency := range step.DependsOn {
            dependencyStep, exists := steps[dependency]
            if !exists || dependencyStep.Order >= step.Order {
                return ErrInvalidPolicyGraph
            }
            if err := visit(dependency); err != nil {
                return err
            }
        }

        state[code] = 2
        return nil
    }

    for code := range steps {
        if err := visit(code); err != nil {
            return err
        }
    }

    return nil
}

type CompleteExecutionStepCommand struct {
    TradeID         marketflow.ID
    StepCode        string
    ExpectedRevision uint64
}

type ExecutionProgressService struct {
    Transactions TransactionManager
    Progress     ExecutionProgressCommandRepository
}

func (s *ExecutionProgressService) CompleteStep(
    ctx context.Context,
    policy marketflow.ExecutionPolicy,
    cmd CompleteExecutionStepCommand,
) error {
    if s == nil || s.Transactions == nil || s.Progress == nil {
        return ErrDependencyMissing
    }
    if strings.TrimSpace(string(cmd.TradeID)) == "" ||
        strings.TrimSpace(cmd.StepCode) == "" ||
        cmd.ExpectedRevision == 0 {
        return marketflow.ErrInvalidInput
    }
    if !policy.Active {
        return marketflow.ErrNotEligible
    }
    if err := ValidateExecutionPolicy(policy); err != nil {
        return err
    }

    return s.Transactions.WithinTransaction(ctx, func(txctx context.Context) error {
        progress, revision, err := s.Progress.GetForUpdate(txctx, cmd.TradeID)
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }
        if revision != cmd.ExpectedRevision {
            return ErrVersionConflict
        }

        if progress.CompletedSteps[cmd.StepCode] {
            return nil
        }
        if err := progress.CanComplete(policy, cmd.StepCode); err != nil {
            return err
        }

        if progress.CompletedSteps == nil {
            progress.CompletedSteps = make(map[string]bool)
        }
        progress.CompletedSteps[cmd.StepCode] = true

        return s.Progress.Save(txctx, progress, revision)
    })
}

// Overflow-safe aggregation. The source marketflow function remains unchanged
// for backward compatibility; application use cases should call this function.

func AggregateCommoditySafe(
    id marketflow.ID,
    lots []marketflow.CommodityLot,
    minimumQuantity int64,
    now time.Time,
) (marketflow.CommodityAggregation, error) {
    if strings.TrimSpace(string(id)) == "" ||
        len(lots) == 0 ||
        minimumQuantity <= 0 ||
        now.IsZero() {
        return marketflow.CommodityAggregation{}, marketflow.ErrInvalidInput
    }

    first := lots[0]
    if strings.TrimSpace(first.CommodityCode) == "" ||
        strings.TrimSpace(first.Unit) == "" {
        return marketflow.CommodityAggregation{}, marketflow.ErrInvalidInput
    }

    result := marketflow.CommodityAggregation{
        ID:            id,
        CommodityCode: first.CommodityCode,
        Unit:          first.Unit,
        CreatedAt:     now.UTC(),
    }

    seen := make(map[marketflow.ID]struct{}, len(lots))

    for _, lot := range lots {
        if strings.TrimSpace(string(lot.ID)) == "" ||
            strings.TrimSpace(string(lot.OfferID)) == "" ||
            lot.Quantity <= 0 ||
            lot.CommodityCode != first.CommodityCode ||
            lot.Unit != first.Unit ||
            !lot.Verified ||
            !lot.AggregationAllowed {
            return marketflow.CommodityAggregation{}, marketflow.ErrNotEligible
        }

        if _, exists := seen[lot.ID]; exists {
            return marketflow.CommodityAggregation{}, marketflow.ErrConflict
        }
        seen[lot.ID] = struct{}{}

        if lot.Quantity > math.MaxInt64-result.TotalQuantity {
            return marketflow.CommodityAggregation{}, marketflow.ErrInvalidInput
        }

        result.TotalQuantity += lot.Quantity
        result.LotIDs = append(result.LotIDs, lot.ID)
    }

    if result.TotalQuantity < minimumQuantity {
        return marketflow.CommodityAggregation{}, marketflow.ErrNotEligible
    }

    return result, nil
}

// External legal requests require identity, permission and explicit scopes.
// The concrete LegalGateway adapter must enforce idempotency at the provider boundary.

type LegalRequestService struct {
    Identity marketflow.IdentityGateway
    Gateway  LegalGateway
}

func (s *LegalRequestService) Submit(
    ctx context.Context,
    actorID marketflow.ID,
    request marketflow.ExternalRequest,
    grantedScopes []string,
) (string, error) {
    if s == nil || s.Identity == nil || s.Gateway == nil {
        return "", ErrDependencyMissing
    }
    if strings.TrimSpace(string(actorID)) == "" {
        return "", marketflow.ErrInvalidInput
    }

    if err := s.Identity.VerifyIdentity(ctx, actorID); err != nil {
        return "", err
    }
    if err := s.Identity.Authorize(ctx, actorID, "legal.external.submit"); err != nil {
        return "", err
    }
    if err := request.ValidateAuthorization(grantedScopes); err != nil {
        return "", err
    }

    return s.Gateway.SubmitAuthorizedRequest(ctx, request)
}

// Platform position is deliberately independent from legal registration.

func RecordPlatformPosition(
    workflow *marketflow.PropertyWorkflow,
    confirmedTradeID marketflow.ID,
    now time.Time,
) error {
    if workflow == nil {
        return marketflow.ErrInvalidInput
    }

    legalStateBefore := workflow.LegalRegistration

    if err := workflow.RecordPlatformPosition(confirmedTradeID, now); err != nil {
        return err
    }

    if workflow.LegalRegistration != legalStateBefore {
        return fmt.Errorf("%w: platform position changed legal state", marketflow.ErrPolicyViolation)
    }

    return nil
}
'@

$Tests = @'
package application

import (
    "errors"
    "math"
    "testing"
    "time"

    "github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/marketflow"
)

func TestValidateExecutionPolicyAcceptsOrderedDependencies(t *testing.T) {
    now := time.Now().UTC()

    policy := marketflow.ExecutionPolicy{
        ID:              "policy-1",
        Name:            "Physical delivery",
        Version:         1,
        OfferCategoryID: "category-1",
        Active:          true,
        CreatedAt:       now,
        Steps: []marketflow.ExecutionStep{
            {
                Code:        "contract",
                Order:       1,
                Requirement: marketflow.StepRequired,
            },
            {
                Code:        "delivery",
                Order:       2,
                Requirement: marketflow.StepRequired,
                DependsOn:   []string{"contract"},
            },
            {
                Code:        "settlement",
                Order:       3,
                Requirement: marketflow.StepRequired,
                DependsOn:   []string{"delivery"},
            },
        },
    }

    if err := ValidateExecutionPolicy(policy); err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("expected valid policy, got %v", err)
    }
}

func TestValidateExecutionPolicyRejectsCycle(t *testing.T) {
    now := time.Now().UTC()

    policy := marketflow.ExecutionPolicy{
        ID:              "policy-2",
        Name:            "Invalid cycle",
        Version:         1,
        OfferCategoryID: "category-1",
        Active:          true,
        CreatedAt:       now,
        Steps: []marketflow.ExecutionStep{
            {
                Code:        "a",
                Order:       1,
                Requirement: marketflow.StepRequired,
                DependsOn:   []string{"b"},
            },
            {
                Code:        "b",
                Order:       2,
                Requirement: marketflow.StepRequired,
                DependsOn:   []string{"a"},
            },
        },
    }

    if err := ValidateExecutionPolicy(policy); !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidPolicyGraph) {
        t.Fatalf("expected ErrInvalidPolicyGraph, got %v", err)
    }
}

func TestValidateExecutionPolicyRejectsDuplicateOrder(t *testing.T) {
    now := time.Now().UTC()

    policy := marketflow.ExecutionPolicy{
        ID:              "policy-3",
        Name:            "Duplicate order",
        Version:         1,
        OfferCategoryID: "category-1",
        Active:          true,
        CreatedAt:       now,
        Steps: []marketflow.ExecutionStep{
            {Code: "a", Order: 1, Requirement: marketflow.StepRequired},
            {Code: "b", Order: 1, Requirement: marketflow.StepOptional},
        },
    }

    if err := ValidateExecutionPolicy(policy); !errors.Is(err, ErrInvalidPolicyGraph) {
        t.Fatalf("expected ErrInvalidPolicyGraph, got %v", err)
    }
}

func TestAggregateCommoditySafe(t *testing.T) {
    now := time.Now().UTC()

    result, err := AggregateCommoditySafe(
        "aggregation-1",
        []marketflow.CommodityLot{
            {
                ID:                 "lot-1",
                OfferID:            "offer-1",
                CommodityCode:      "WHEAT",
                Quantity:           100,
                Unit:               "kg",
                Verified:           true,
                AggregationAllowed: true,
            },
            {
                ID:                 "lot-2",
                OfferID:            "offer-2",
                CommodityCode:      "WHEAT",
                Quantity:           200,
                Unit:               "kg",
                Verified:           true,
                AggregationAllowed: true,
            },
        },
        250,
        now,
    )

    if err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("unexpected aggregation error: %v", err)
    }
    if result.TotalQuantity != 300 {
        t.Fatalf("expected total 300, got %d", result.TotalQuantity)
    }
    if len(result.LotIDs) != 2 {
        t.Fatalf("expected 2 lots, got %d", len(result.LotIDs))
    }
}

func TestAggregateCommodityRejectsOverflow(t *testing.T) {
    now := time.Now().UTC()

    _, err := AggregateCommoditySafe(
        "aggregation-2",
        []marketflow.CommodityLot{
            {
                ID:                 "lot-1",
                OfferID:            "offer-1",
                CommodityCode:      "WHEAT",
                Quantity:           math.MaxInt64,
                Unit:               "kg",
                Verified:           true,
                AggregationAllowed: true,
            },
            {
                ID:                 "lot-2",
                OfferID:            "offer-2",
                CommodityCode:      "WHEAT",
                Quantity:           1,
                Unit:               "kg",
                Verified:           true,
                AggregationAllowed: true,
            },
        },
        1,
        now,
    )

    if err == nil {
        t.Fatal("expected overflow rejection")
    }
}

func TestAggregateCommodityRejectsDuplicateLot(t *testing.T) {
    now := time.Now().UTC()

    lot := marketflow.CommodityLot{
        ID:                 "lot-1",
        OfferID:            "offer-1",
        CommodityCode:      "WHEAT",
        Quantity:           100,
        Unit:               "kg",
        Verified:           true,
        AggregationAllowed: true,
    }

    _, err := AggregateCommoditySafe(
        "aggregation-3",
        []marketflow.CommodityLot{lot, lot},
        1,
        now,
    )

    if !errors.Is(err, marketflow.ErrConflict) {
        t.Fatalf("expected conflict, got %v", err)
    }
}

func TestCalculateOrderNotional(t *testing.T) {
    order := marketflow.Order{
        Side:     marketflow.Buy,
        Price:    250,
        Quantity: 4,
    }

    amount, err := CalculateOrderNotional(order)
    if err != nil {
        t.Fatal(err)
    }
    if amount != 1000 {
        t.Fatalf("expected 1000, got %d", amount)
    }
}

func TestCalculateOrderNotionalRejectsOverflow(t *testing.T) {
    order := marketflow.Order{
        Side:     marketflow.Buy,
        Price:    math.MaxInt64,
        Quantity: 2,
    }

    if _, err := CalculateOrderNotional(order); err == nil {
        t.Fatal("expected overflow rejection")
    }
}

func TestPlatformPositionDoesNotImplyLegalRegistration(t *testing.T) {
    workflow := &marketflow.PropertyWorkflow{
        ID:                "property-flow-1",
        OfferID:           "offer-1",
        TradeID:           "trade-1",
        PlatformPosition:  marketflow.PositionNotRecorded,
        LegalRegistration: marketflow.LegalPending,
        State:             marketflow.PropertyRegistration,
    }

    if err := RecordPlatformPosition(
        workflow,
        "trade-1",
        time.Now().UTC(),
    ); err != nil {
        t.Fatal(err)
    }

    if workflow.PlatformPosition != marketflow.PositionRecorded {
        t.Fatal("platform position was not recorded")
    }
    if workflow.LegalRegistration != marketflow.LegalPending {
        t.Fatal("platform position must not imply legal registration")
    }
}

    Select-Object -First 1

    throw "Script execution failed. Check the preceding output."

Write-Host "Module:  $ModulePath" -ForegroundColor Cyan

# Only add new files. Never overwrite existing files.

    "internal/marketflow/capabilities_test.go"

$Utf8NoBom = New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)

function Write-GoSource {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)][string]$RelativePath,
        [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)][string]$Content
    )

    $FullPath = Join-Path $ProjectRoot $RelativePath
    $Directory = Split-Path -Parent $FullPath

    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Directory)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $Directory -Force | Out-Null
    }

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $FullPath) {
    throw "Script execution failed. Check the preceding output."
    }

    [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($FullPath, $Content, $Utf8NoBom)
    Write-Host "Created: $RelativePath" -ForegroundColor Green
}

# ============================================================
# 1. DOMAIN CAPABILITIES
# ============================================================

$Capabilities = @'
package marketflow

import (
    "errors"
    "fmt"
    "sort"
    "strings"
    "time"
)

var (
    ErrInvalidInput       = errors.New("invalid input")
    ErrInvalidTransition  = errors.New("invalid state transition")
    ErrNotEligible        = errors.New("operation is not eligible")
    ErrNotFound           = errors.New("entity not found")
    ErrConflict           = errors.New("conflict")
    ErrPolicyViolation    = errors.New("execution policy violation")
    ErrExternalNotAllowed = errors.New("external operation is not authorized")
)

type ID string

func validID(id ID) bool {
    return strings.TrimSpace(string(id)) != ""
}

func validTime(t time.Time) bool {
    return !t.IsZero()
}

// ============================================================
// MARKET AND OFFER CONFIGURATION
// ============================================================

type MarketState string

const (
    MarketDraft     MarketState = "draft"
    MarketActive    MarketState = "active"
    MarketSuspended MarketState = "suspended"
    MarketClosed    MarketState = "closed"
)

type OfferState string

const (
    OfferDraft      OfferState = "draft"
    OfferReview     OfferState = "pending_review"
    OfferApproved   OfferState = "approved"
    OfferPublished  OfferState = "published"
    OfferSuspended  OfferState = "suspended"
    OfferRejected   OfferState = "rejected"
    OfferExpired    OfferState = "expired"
    OfferClosed     OfferState = "closed"
)

type ParameterType string

const (
    ParamString  ParameterType = "string"
    ParamNumber  ParameterType = "number"
    ParamBoolean ParameterType = "boolean"
)

type ParameterDefinition struct {
    Code       string
    Type       ParameterType
    Required   bool
    Min        *float64
    Max        *float64
    Allowed    []string
}

type OfferConfiguration struct {
    ID          ID
    CategoryID  ID
    Version     uint64
    Parameters  []ParameterDefinition
    RequiredDocs []string
    Active      bool
    CreatedAt   time.Time
}

type OfferParameters map[string]any

func (c OfferConfiguration) Validate(values OfferParameters) error {
    if !validID(c.ID) || !validID(c.CategoryID) ||
        c.Version == 0 || !validTime(c.CreatedAt) {
        return ErrInvalidInput
    }

    for _, definition := range c.Parameters {
        value, exists := values[definition.Code]

        if !exists {
            if definition.Required {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: missing parameter %s",
                    ErrInvalidInput, definition.Code)
            }
            continue
        }

        switch definition.Type {
        case ParamString:
            if _, ok := value.(string); !ok {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: parameter %s must be string",
                    ErrInvalidInput, definition.Code)
            }

        case ParamBoolean:
            if _, ok := value.(bool); !ok {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: parameter %s must be boolean",
                    ErrInvalidInput, definition.Code)
            }

        case ParamNumber:
            number, ok := value.(float64)
            if !ok {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: parameter %s must be number",
                    ErrInvalidInput, definition.Code)
            }
            if definition.Min != nil && number < *definition.Min {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: parameter %s below minimum",
                    ErrInvalidInput, definition.Code)
            }
            if definition.Max != nil && number > *definition.Max {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: parameter %s above maximum",
                    ErrInvalidInput, definition.Code)
            }

        default:
            return ErrInvalidInput
        }

        if len(definition.Allowed) > 0 {
            text, ok := value.(string)
            if !ok {
                return ErrInvalidInput
            }

            allowed := false
            for _, candidate := range definition.Allowed {
                if candidate == text {
                    allowed = true
                    break
                }
            }
            if !allowed {
                return fmt.Errorf("%w: parameter %s is not allowed",
                    ErrInvalidInput, definition.Code)
            }
        }
    }

    return nil
}

// ============================================================
// COMPANY ELIGIBILITY
// ============================================================

type QualificationState string

const (
    QualificationPending  QualificationState = "pending"
    QualificationApproved QualificationState = "approved"
    QualificationRejected QualificationState = "rejected"
    QualificationExpired  QualificationState = "expired"
)

type CompanyQualification struct {
    CompanyID ID
    CategoryID ID
    ActivityCode string
    State QualificationState
    Documents []string
    ValidUntil time.Time
    Version uint64
}

func (q CompanyQualification) IsEligible(
    categoryID ID,
    activityCode string,
    requiredDocuments []string,
    now time.Time,
) error {
    if !validID(q.CompanyID) || !validID(categoryID) ||
        !validTime(now) || q.Version == 0 {
        return ErrInvalidInput
    }

    if q.State != QualificationApproved ||
        q.CategoryID != categoryID ||
        q.ActivityCode != activityCode {
        return ErrNotEligible
    }

    if !q.ValidUntil.IsZero() && now.After(q.ValidUntil) {
        return ErrNotEligible
    }

    available := make(map[string]bool, len(q.Documents))
    for _, document := range q.Documents {
        available[document] = true
    }

    for _, document := range requiredDocuments {
        if !available[document] {
            return fmt.Errorf("%w: missing document %s",
                ErrNotEligible, document)
        }
    }

    return nil
}

// ============================================================
// OFFER LIFECYCLE
// ============================================================

type Offer struct {
    ID ID
    MarketID ID
    OwnerID ID
    PublicCategoryID ID
    SpecializedCategoryID ID
    ConfigurationID ID
    ConfigurationVersion uint64
    Title string
    Description string
    Quantity int64
    Remaining int64
    Unit string
    Price int64
    Currency string
    State OfferState
    ValidUntil time.Time
    Version uint64
    CreatedAt time.Time
    UpdatedAt time.Time
}

func (o *Offer) Validate() error {
    if o == nil || !validID(o.ID) || !validID(o.MarketID) ||
        !validID(o.OwnerID) || !validID(o.PublicCategoryID) ||
        !validID(o.SpecializedCategoryID) ||
        !validID(o.ConfigurationID) ||
        o.ConfigurationVersion == 0 ||
        strings.TrimSpace(o.Title) == "" ||
        o.Quantity <= 0 || o.Remaining < 0 ||
        o.Remaining > o.Quantity || o.Price < 0 ||
        strings.TrimSpace(o.Unit) == "" ||
        strings.TrimSpace(o.Currency) == "" ||
        o.Version == 0 || !validTime(o.CreatedAt) ||
        !validTime(o.UpdatedAt) {
        return ErrInvalidInput
    }
    return nil
}

func (o *Offer) SubmitForReview(now time.Time) error {
    if err := o.Validate(); err != nil {
        return err
    }
    if o.State != OfferDraft || !validTime(now) {
        return ErrInvalidTransition
    }
    o.State = OfferReview
    o.touch(now)
    return nil
}

func (o *Offer) Approve(now time.Time) error {
    if o.State != OfferReview || !validTime(now) {
        return ErrInvalidTransition
    }
    o.State = OfferApproved
    o.touch(now)
    return nil
}

func (o *Offer) Publish(now time.Time) error {
    if o.State != OfferApproved || !validTime(now) {
        return ErrInvalidTransition
    }
    if o.Remaining <= 0 {
        return ErrNotEligible
    }
    if !o.ValidUntil.IsZero() && !now.Before(o.ValidUntil) {
        return ErrNotEligible
    }
    o.State = OfferPublished
    o.touch(now)
    return nil
}

func (o *Offer) Suspend(now time.Time) error {
    if o.State != OfferPublished || !validTime(now) {
        return ErrInvalidTransition
    }
    o.State = OfferSuspended
    o.touch(now)
    return nil
}

func (o *Offer) Close(now time.Time) error {
    if o.State == OfferClosed || !validTime(now) {
        return ErrInvalidTransition
    }
    o.State = OfferClosed
    o.touch(now)
    return nil
}

func (o *Offer) touch(now time.Time) {
    o.UpdatedAt = now.UTC()
    o.Version++
}

// ============================================================
// SUPPLY REQUESTS AND PROPOSALS
// ============================================================

type RequestState string

const (
    RequestOpen     RequestState = "open"
    RequestSelected RequestState = "selected"
    RequestClosed   RequestState = "closed"
    RequestCancelled RequestState = "cancelled"
)

type SupplyRequest struct {
    ID ID
    RequesterID ID
    CategoryID ID
    Quantity int64
    Unit string
    MaxPrice int64
    Currency string
    State RequestState
    SelectedProposalID ID
    CreatedAt time.Time
}

type ProposalState string

const (
    ProposalSubmitted ProposalState = "submitted"
    ProposalAccepted  ProposalState = "accepted"
    ProposalRejected  ProposalState = "rejected"
    ProposalWithdrawn ProposalState = "withdrawn"
)

type SupplyProposal struct {
    ID ID
    RequestID ID
    SupplierID ID
    OfferID ID
    Quantity int64
    UnitPrice int64
    Currency string
    DeliveryTerms string
    State ProposalState
    CreatedAt time.Time
}

func (r SupplyRequest) Validate() error {
    if !validID(r.ID) || !validID(r.RequesterID) ||
        !validID(r.CategoryID) || r.Quantity <= 0 ||
        r.Unit == "" || r.Currency == "" ||
        !validTime(r.CreatedAt) {
        return ErrInvalidInput
    }
    return nil
}

func (p SupplyProposal) IsCompatible(r SupplyRequest) bool {
    return p.RequestID == r.ID &&
        p.Quantity >= r.Quantity &&
        p.UnitPrice > 0 &&
        p.Currency == r.Currency &&
        p.State == ProposalSubmitted
}

func SelectProposal(
    request *SupplyRequest,
    proposal SupplyProposal,
) error {
    if request == nil || request.State != RequestOpen {
        return ErrInvalidTransition
    }
    if !validID(proposal.ID) || !proposal.IsCompatible(*request) {
        return ErrNotEligible
    }
    if request.MaxPrice > 0 && proposal.UnitPrice > request.MaxPrice {
        return ErrNotEligible
    }

    request.SelectedProposalID = proposal.ID
    request.State = RequestSelected
    return nil
}

// ============================================================
// ORDERS AND ORDER BOOK
// All prices are integer minor units. No float arithmetic.
// ============================================================

type OrderSide string

const (
    Buy  OrderSide = "buy"
    Sell OrderSide = "sell"
)

type OrderState string

const (
    OrderPending  OrderState = "pending"
    OrderOpen     OrderState = "open"
    OrderPartial  OrderState = "partially_filled"
    OrderFilled   OrderState = "filled"
    OrderCancelled OrderState = "cancelled"
    OrderRejected OrderState = "rejected"
)

type Order struct {
    ID ID
    OfferID ID
    MarketID ID
    UserID ID
    Side OrderSide
    Price int64
    Quantity int64
    Filled int64
    State OrderState
    CreatedAt time.Time
    Version uint64
}

func (o Order) Validate() error {
    if !validID(o.ID) || !validID(o.OfferID) ||
        !validID(o.MarketID) || !validID(o.UserID) ||
        (o.Side != Buy && o.Side != Sell) ||
        o.Price <= 0 || o.Quantity <= 0 ||
        o.Filled < 0 || o.Filled > o.Quantity ||
        !validTime(o.CreatedAt) || o.Version == 0 {
        return ErrInvalidInput
    }
    return nil
}

func (o *Order) Open() error {
    if err := o.Validate(); err != nil {
        return err
    }
    if o.State != OrderPending {
        return ErrInvalidTransition
    }
    o.State = OrderOpen
    o.Version++
    return nil
}

func (o *Order) Cancel() error {
    if o.State != OrderOpen && o.State != OrderPartial {
        return ErrInvalidTransition
    }
    o.State = OrderCancelled
    o.Version++
    return nil
}

func (o *Order) Remaining() int64 {
    return o.Quantity - o.Filled
}

type MatchResult struct {
    BuyOrderID ID
    SellOrderID ID
    Quantity int64
    Price int64
}

func MatchOrders(buy, sell *Order) (MatchResult, error) {
    if buy == nil || sell == nil {
        return MatchResult{}, ErrInvalidInput
    }
    if err := buy.Validate(); err != nil {
        return MatchResult{}, err
    }
    if err := sell.Validate(); err != nil {
        return MatchResult{}, err
    }

    if buy.Side != Buy || sell.Side != Sell ||
        buy.State != OrderOpen && buy.State != OrderPartial ||
        sell.State != OrderOpen && sell.State != OrderPartial {
        return MatchResult{}, ErrInvalidTransition
    }

    if buy.MarketID != sell.MarketID ||
        buy.OfferID != sell.OfferID ||
        buy.UserID == sell.UserID ||
        buy.Price < sell.Price {
        return MatchResult{}, ErrNotEligible
    }

    quantity := buy.Remaining()
    if sell.Remaining() < quantity {
        quantity = sell.Remaining()
    }
    if quantity <= 0 {
        return MatchResult{}, ErrNotEligible
    }

    // Price-time rule: older order is the maker.
    executionPrice := buy.Price
    if sell.CreatedAt.Before(buy.CreatedAt) ||
        sell.CreatedAt.Equal(buy.CreatedAt) {
        executionPrice = sell.Price
    }

    buy.Filled += quantity
    sell.Filled += quantity

    if buy.Remaining() == 0 {
        buy.State = OrderFilled
    } else {
        buy.State = OrderPartial
    }

    if sell.Remaining() == 0 {
        sell.State = OrderFilled
    } else {
        sell.State = OrderPartial
    }

    buy.Version++
    sell.Version++

    return MatchResult{
        BuyOrderID: buy.ID,
        SellOrderID: sell.ID,
        Quantity: quantity,
        Price: executionPrice,
    }, nil
}

func SortOrderBook(orders []Order) {
    sort.SliceStable(orders, func(i, j int) bool {
        a, b := orders[i], orders[j]

        if a.Side != b.Side {
            return a.Side == Buy
        }
        if a.Price != b.Price {
            if a.Side == Buy {
                return a.Price > b.Price
            }
            return a.Price < b.Price
        }
        return a.CreatedAt.Before(b.CreatedAt)
    })
}

// ============================================================
// TRADE - independent status dimensions
// ============================================================

type TradeState string
type ContractState string
type ServiceState string
type DeliveryState string
type SettlementReadiness string
type SettlementState string

const (
    TradeCreated   TradeState = "created"
    TradeConfirmed TradeState = "confirmed"
    TradeExecuting TradeState = "executing"
    TradeCompleted TradeState = "completed"
    TradeCancelled TradeState = "cancelled"

    ContractNotRequired ContractState = "not_required"
    ContractPending     ContractState = "pending"
    ContractCreated     ContractState = "created"
    ContractFailed      ContractState = "failed"

    ServiceNotRequired ServiceState = "not_required"
    ServicePending     ServiceState = "pending"
    ServiceInProgress  ServiceState = "in_progress"
    ServiceCompleted   ServiceState = "completed"
    ServiceFailed      ServiceState = "failed"

    DeliveryNotRequired DeliveryState = "not_required"
    DeliveryPending     DeliveryState = "pending"
    DeliveryConfirmed   DeliveryState = "confirmed"
    DeliveryFailed      DeliveryState = "failed"

    SettlementNotReady SettlementReadiness = "not_ready"
    SettlementReady    SettlementReadiness = "ready"

    SettlementPending SettlementState = "pending"
    SettlementDone    SettlementState = "completed"
    SettlementFailed  SettlementState = "failed"
)

type Trade struct {
    ID ID
    MatchID ID
    BuyOrderID ID
    SellOrderID ID
    BuyerID ID
    SellerID ID
    OfferID ID
    Quantity int64
    Price int64

    State TradeState
    Contract ContractState
    Services ServiceState
    Delivery DeliveryState
    SettlementReadiness SettlementReadiness
    Settlement SettlementState

    CreatedAt time.Time
    UpdatedAt time.Time
    Version uint64
}

func (t Trade) Validate() error {
    if !validID(t.ID) || !validID(t.BuyOrderID) ||
        !validID(t.SellOrderID) || !validID(t.BuyerID) ||
        !validID(t.SellerID) || !validID(t.OfferID) ||
        t.BuyerID == t.SellerID || t.Quantity <= 0 ||
        t.Price <= 0 || !validTime(t.CreatedAt) ||
        !validTime(t.UpdatedAt) || t.Version == 0 {
        return ErrInvalidInput
    }
    return nil
}

func (t *Trade) RecalculateSettlementReadiness() {
    if t.Contract == ContractFailed ||
        t.Services == ServiceFailed ||
        t.Delivery == DeliveryFailed {
        t.SettlementReadiness = SettlementNotReady
        return
    }

    contractOK := t.Contract == ContractNotRequired ||
        t.Contract == ContractCreated
    servicesOK := t.Services == ServiceNotRequired ||
        t.Services == ServiceCompleted
    deliveryOK := t.Delivery == DeliveryNotRequired ||
        t.Delivery == DeliveryConfirmed

    if contractOK && servicesOK && deliveryOK {
        t.SettlementReadiness = SettlementReady
    } else {
        t.SettlementReadiness = SettlementNotReady
    }
}

// ============================================================
// VERSIONED EXECUTION POLICIES
// ============================================================

type StepRequirement string

const (
    StepRequired StepRequirement = "required"
    StepOptional StepRequirement = "optional"
    StepConditional StepRequirement = "conditional"
)

type ExecutionStep struct {
    Code string
    Order int
    Requirement StepRequirement
    RequiredDocuments []string
    DependsOn []string
}

type ExecutionPolicy struct {
    ID ID
    Name string
    Version uint64
    OfferCategoryID ID
    Steps []ExecutionStep
    Active bool
    CreatedAt time.Time
}

func (p ExecutionPolicy) Validate() error {
    if !validID(p.ID) || !validID(p.OfferCategoryID) ||
        strings.TrimSpace(p.Name) == "" ||
        p.Version == 0 || !validTime(p.CreatedAt) ||
        len(p.Steps) == 0 {
        return ErrInvalidInput
    }

    seen := make(map[string]bool)
    for _, step := range p.Steps {
        if strings.TrimSpace(step.Code) == "" ||
            step.Order < 1 || seen[step.Code] {
            return ErrInvalidInput
        }
        if step.Requirement != StepRequired &&
            step.Requirement != StepOptional &&
            step.Requirement != StepConditional {
            return ErrInvalidInput
        }
        seen[step.Code] = true
    }

    for _, step := range p.Steps {
        for _, dependency := range step.DependsOn {
            if !seen[dependency] || dependency == step.Code {
                return ErrPolicyViolation
            }
        }
    }

    return nil
}

type ExecutionProgress struct {
    TradeID ID
    PolicyID ID
    PolicyVersion uint64
    CompletedSteps map[string]bool
}

func (p ExecutionProgress) CanComplete(
    policy ExecutionPolicy,
    stepCode string,
) error {
    if p.PolicyID != policy.ID ||
        p.PolicyVersion != policy.Version {
        return ErrConflict
    }

    var selected *ExecutionStep
    for i := range policy.Steps {
        if policy.Steps[i].Code == stepCode {
            selected = &policy.Steps[i]
            break
        }
    }
    if selected == nil {
        return ErrNotFound
    }

    for _, dependency := range selected.DependsOn {
        if !p.CompletedSteps[dependency] {
            return fmt.Errorf("%w: dependency %s",
                ErrPolicyViolation, dependency)
        }
    }
    return nil
}

// ============================================================
// PHYSICAL COMMODITY AGGREGATION
// ============================================================

type CommodityLot struct {
    ID ID
    OfferID ID
    CommodityCode string
    Quantity int64
    Unit string
    Verified bool
    AggregationAllowed bool
}

type CommodityAggregation struct {
    ID ID
    CommodityCode string
    Unit string
    LotIDs []ID
    TotalQuantity int64
    CreatedAt time.Time
}

func AggregateCommodity(
    id ID,
    lots []CommodityLot,
    minimumQuantity int64,
    now time.Time,
) (CommodityAggregation, error) {
    if !validID(id) || len(lots) == 0 ||
        minimumQuantity <= 0 || !validTime(now) {
        return CommodityAggregation{}, ErrInvalidInput
    }

    first := lots[0]
    if first.CommodityCode == "" || first.Unit == "" {
        return CommodityAggregation{}, ErrInvalidInput
    }

    result := CommodityAggregation{
        ID: id,
        CommodityCode: first.CommodityCode,
        Unit: first.Unit,
        CreatedAt: now.UTC(),
    }

    seen := make(map[ID]bool)

    for _, lot := range lots {
        if !validID(lot.ID) || lot.Quantity <= 0 ||
            lot.CommodityCode != first.CommodityCode ||
            lot.Unit != first.Unit ||
            !lot.Verified || !lot.AggregationAllowed ||
            seen[lot.ID] {
            return CommodityAggregation{}, ErrNotEligible
        }

        seen[lot.ID] = true
        result.LotIDs = append(result.LotIDs, lot.ID)
        result.TotalQuantity += lot.Quantity
    }

    if result.TotalQuantity < minimumQuantity {
        return CommodityAggregation{}, ErrNotEligible
    }

    return result, nil
}

// ============================================================
// REAL ESTATE - platform position != legal ownership
// ============================================================

type PropertyWorkflowState string
type PlatformPositionState string
type LegalRegistrationState string

const (
    PropertyPending PropertyWorkflowState = "pending"
    PropertyTrading PropertyWorkflowState = "trading"
    PropertyContracting PropertyWorkflowState = "contracting"
    PropertyRegistration PropertyWorkflowState = "registration"
    PropertyCompleted PropertyWorkflowState = "completed"

    PositionNotRecorded PlatformPositionState = "not_recorded"
    PositionRecorded PlatformPositionState = "recorded"
    PositionRejected PlatformPositionState = "rejected"

    LegalNotRequired LegalRegistrationState = "not_required"
    LegalPending LegalRegistrationState = "pending"
    LegalSubmitted LegalRegistrationState = "submitted"
    LegalConfirmed LegalRegistrationState = "confirmed"
    LegalRejected LegalRegistrationState = "rejected"
)

type PropertyWorkflow struct {
    ID ID
    OfferID ID
    TradeID ID
    PropertyReference string
    PlatformPosition PlatformPositionState
    LegalRegistration LegalRegistrationState
    State PropertyWorkflowState
    UpdatedAt time.Time
}

func (p *PropertyWorkflow) RecordPlatformPosition(
    confirmedTradeID ID,
    now time.Time,
) error {
    if p == nil || !validID(confirmedTradeID) ||
        confirmedTradeID != p.TradeID ||
        !validTime(now) {
        return ErrNotEligible
    }

    p.PlatformPosition = PositionRecorded
    p.UpdatedAt = now.UTC()
    return nil
}

func (p PropertyWorkflow) IsLegallyRegistered() bool {
    return p.LegalRegistration == LegalConfirmed
}

// ============================================================
// LEGAL / EXTERNAL INTEGRATION TRACKING
// ============================================================

type ExternalRequestState string

const (
    ExternalPending ExternalRequestState = "pending"
    ExternalSent ExternalRequestState = "sent"
    ExternalSucceeded ExternalRequestState = "succeeded"
    ExternalFailed ExternalRequestState = "failed"
)

type ExternalRequest struct {
    ID ID
    CorrelationID ID
    IdempotencyKey string
    InstitutionCode string
    Purpose string
    Scope []string
    PayloadReference string
    State ExternalRequestState
    ExternalTrackingID string
    ResponseReference string
    Attempt int
    CreatedAt time.Time
    UpdatedAt time.Time
}

func (r ExternalRequest) ValidateAuthorization(
    grantedScopes []string,
) error {
    if !validID(r.ID) || !validID(r.CorrelationID) ||
        strings.TrimSpace(r.IdempotencyKey) == "" ||
        strings.TrimSpace(r.InstitutionCode) == "" ||
        strings.TrimSpace(r.Purpose) == "" ||
        len(r.Scope) == 0 || !validTime(r.CreatedAt) {
        return ErrInvalidInput
    }

    allowed := make(map[string]bool)
    for _, scope := range grantedScopes {
        allowed[scope] = true
    }

    for _, scope := range r.Scope {
        if !allowed[scope] {
            return ErrExternalNotAllowed
        }
    }
    return nil
}

// ============================================================
// AUDIT
// ============================================================

type AuditEvent struct {
    ID ID
    AggregateType string
    AggregateID ID
    Action string
    ActorID ID
    CorrelationID ID
    PreviousVersion uint64
    NewVersion uint64
    OccurredAt time.Time
    Metadata map[string]string
}

func (e AuditEvent) Validate() error {
    if !validID(e.ID) || !validID(e.AggregateID) ||
        !validID(e.ActorID) || !validID(e.CorrelationID) ||
        strings.TrimSpace(e.AggregateType) == "" ||
        strings.TrimSpace(e.Action) == "" ||
        !validTime(e.OccurredAt) ||
        e.NewVersion < e.PreviousVersion {
        return ErrInvalidInput
    }
    return nil
}
'@

$Ports = @'
package marketflow

import "context"

// These are permanent contracts.
// Implementations can later use PostgreSQL and real service clients.
// No service below is treated as operational until an adapter exists.

type OfferRepository interface {
    GetOffer(context.Context, ID) (Offer, error)
    SaveOffer(context.Context, Offer, uint64) error
    ListPublishedOffers(context.Context, int, int) ([]Offer, error)
}

type OrderRepository interface {
    GetOrder(context.Context, ID) (Order, error)
    SaveOrder(context.Context, Order, uint64) error
    GetOpenBook(context.Context, ID, ID) ([]Order, error)
}

type TradeRepository interface {
    GetTrade(context.Context, ID) (Trade, error)
    SaveTrade(context.Context, Trade, uint64) error
}

type SupplyRequestRepository interface {
    GetSupplyRequest(context.Context, ID) (SupplyRequest, error)
    SaveSupplyRequest(context.Context, SupplyRequest) error
}

type ExecutionPolicyRepository interface {
    GetActivePolicy(context.Context, ID) (ExecutionPolicy, error)
}

type AuditRepository interface {
    Append(context.Context, AuditEvent) error
    ListByAggregate(context.Context, string, ID, int) ([]AuditEvent, error)
}

// Account service is the authority for identity and base permissions.
type IdentityGateway interface {
    VerifyIdentity(context.Context, ID) error
    Authorize(context.Context, ID, string) error
}

// My Assets is the authority for buying power and reservations.
type AssetGateway interface {
    CheckBuyingPower(context.Context, ID, int64, string) error
    Reserve(context.Context, ID, ID, int64, string) (ID, error)
    Release(context.Context, ID, ID) error
    RegisterPlatformPosition(context.Context, ID, ID, int64) error
}

// Independent authoritative services.
type ContractGateway interface {
    CreateContract(context.Context, ID, ID) (ID, error)
    GetContractStatus(context.Context, ID) (string, error)
}

type ServiceGateway interface {
    RequestService(context.Context, ID, string, map[string]string) (ID, error)
    GetServiceStatus(context.Context, ID) (string, error)
}

type SettlementGateway interface {
    RequestSettlement(context.Context, ID, ID) (ID, error)
    GetSettlementStatus(context.Context, ID) (string, error)
}

type LegalGateway interface {
    SubmitAuthorizedRequest(context.Context, ExternalRequest) (string, error)
    GetRequestResult(context.Context, string) (string, error)
}

// A repository adapter must enforce optimistic concurrency,
// atomic writes, and idempotency where applicable.
// The domain package itself does not pretend to provide persistence.
'@

package marketflow

import (
    "errors"
    "testing"
    "time"
)

func TestOfferLifecycle(t *testing.T) {
    now := time.Date(2026, 11, 4, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)

    offer := &Offer{
        ID: "offer-1",
        MarketID: "market-1",
        OwnerID: "owner-1",
        PublicCategoryID: "public-1",
        SpecializedCategoryID: "special-1",
        ConfigurationID: "config-1",
        ConfigurationVersion: 1,
        Title: "Test offer",
        Quantity: 100,
        Remaining: 100,
        Unit: "unit",
        Price: 1000,
        Currency: "IRR",
        State: OfferDraft,
        Version: 1,
        CreatedAt: now,
        UpdatedAt: now,
    }

    if err := offer.SubmitForReview(now.Add(time.Minute)); err != nil {
        t.Fatal(err)
    }
    if err := offer.Approve(now.Add(2 * time.Minute)); err != nil {
        t.Fatal(err)
    }
    if err := offer.Publish(now.Add(3 * time.Minute)); err != nil {
        t.Fatal(err)
    }
    if offer.State != OfferPublished {
        t.Fatalf("unexpected state: %s", offer.State)
    }
}

func TestOrderMatchingPartialFill(t *testing.T) {
    now := time.Date(2026, 11, 4, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)

    buy := &Order{
        ID: "buy-1", OfferID: "offer-1", MarketID: "market-1",
        UserID: "buyer", Side: Buy, Price: 1200,
        Quantity: 100, State: OrderOpen,
        CreatedAt: now, Version: 1,
    }

    sell := &Order{
        ID: "sell-1", OfferID: "offer-1", MarketID: "market-1",
        UserID: "seller", Side: Sell, Price: 1100,
        Quantity: 40, State: OrderOpen,
        CreatedAt: now.Add(time.Minute), Version: 1,
    }

    result, err := MatchOrders(buy, sell)
    if err != nil {
        t.Fatal(err)
    }

    if result.Quantity != 40 {
        t.Fatalf("expected 40, got %d", result.Quantity)
    }
    if buy.State != OrderPartial || sell.State != OrderFilled {
        t.Fatal("incorrect order states after partial fill")
    }
    if buy.Remaining() != 60 {
        t.Fatalf("expected 60 remaining, got %d", buy.Remaining())
    }
}

func TestOrdersCannotMatchTheirOwnUser(t *testing.T) {
    now := time.Now().UTC()

    buy := &Order{
        ID: "b", OfferID: "o", MarketID: "m",
        UserID: "same", Side: Buy, Price: 100,
        Quantity: 10, State: OrderOpen,
        CreatedAt: now, Version: 1,
    }

    sell := &Order{
        ID: "s", OfferID: "o", MarketID: "m",
        UserID: "same", Side: Sell, Price: 90,
        Quantity: 10, State: OrderOpen,
        CreatedAt: now, Version: 1,
    }

    _, err := MatchOrders(buy, sell)
    if !errors.Is(err, ErrNotEligible) {
        t.Fatalf("expected ErrNotEligible, got %v", err)
    }
}

func TestProposalSelection(t *testing.T) {
    request := &SupplyRequest{
        ID: "request-1",
        RequesterID: "buyer",
        CategoryID: "category-1",
        Quantity: 50,
        Unit: "ton",
        MaxPrice: 200,
        Currency: "IRR",
        State: RequestOpen,
        CreatedAt: time.Now().UTC(),
    }

    proposal := SupplyProposal{
        ID: "proposal-1",
        RequestID: "request-1",
        SupplierID: "supplier",
        OfferID: "offer-1",
        Quantity: 60,
        UnitPrice: 180,
        Currency: "IRR",
        State: ProposalSubmitted,
        CreatedAt: time.Now().UTC(),
    }

    if err := SelectProposal(request, proposal); err != nil {
        t.Fatal(err)
    }

    if request.State != RequestSelected ||
        request.SelectedProposalID != proposal.ID {
        t.Fatal("proposal was not selected")
    }
}

func TestExternalRequestRequiresGrantedScope(t *testing.T) {
    request := ExternalRequest{
        ID: "external-1",
        CorrelationID: "correlation-1",
        IdempotencyKey: "idempotency-1",
        InstitutionCode: "registry",
        Purpose: "property verification",
        Scope: []string{"property.read"},
        State: ExternalPending,
        CreatedAt: time.Now().UTC(),
    }

    if err := request.ValidateAuthorization([]string{"property.read"}); err != nil {
        t.Fatal(err)
    }

    if err := request.ValidateAuthorization([]string{"identity.read"}); !errors.Is(err, ErrExternalNotAllowed) {
        t.Fatalf("expected authorization failure, got %v", err)
    }
}

func TestExecutionPolicyDependencies(t *testing.T) {
    policy := ExecutionPolicy{
        ID: "policy-1",
        Name: "Property transaction",
        Version: 1,
        OfferCategoryID: "property",
        Active: true,
        CreatedAt: time.Now().UTC(),
        Steps: []ExecutionStep{
            {Code: "contract", Order: 1, Requirement: StepRequired},
            {Code: "position", Order: 2, Requirement: StepRequired, DependsOn: []string{"contract"}},
        },
    }

    if err := policy.Validate(); err != nil {
        t.Fatal(err)
    }

    progress := ExecutionProgress{
        TradeID: "trade-1",
        PolicyID: policy.ID,
        PolicyVersion: 1,
        CompletedSteps: map[string]bool{},
    }

    if err := progress.CanComplete(policy, "position"); !errors.Is(err, ErrPolicyViolation) {
        t.Fatalf("expected dependency failure, got %v", err)
    }

    progress.CompletedSteps["contract"] = true
    if err := progress.CanComplete(policy, "position"); err != nil {
        t.Fatal(err)
    }
}

func TestCommodityAggregation(t *testing.T) {
    now := time.Now().UTC()

    lots := []CommodityLot{
        {
            ID: "lot-1", CommodityCode: "WHEAT",
            Quantity: 30, Unit: "ton",
            Verified: true, AggregationAllowed: true,
        },
        {
            ID: "lot-2", CommodityCode: "WHEAT",
            Quantity: 40, Unit: "ton",
            Verified: true, AggregationAllowed: true,
        },
    }

    result, err := AggregateCommodity("aggregation-1", lots, 60, now)
    if err != nil {
        t.Fatal(err)
    }
    if result.TotalQuantity != 70 {
        t.Fatalf("expected 70, got %d", result.TotalQuantity)
    }
}

func TestPlatformPositionDoesNotMeanLegalRegistration(t *testing.T) {
    workflow := PropertyWorkflow{
        ID: "property-flow-1",
        OfferID: "offer-1",
        TradeID: "trade-1",
        PlatformPosition: PositionNotRecorded,
        LegalRegistration: LegalPending,
        State: PropertyRegistration,
    }

    if err := workflow.RecordPlatformPosition("trade-1", time.Now()); err != nil {
        t.Fatal(err)
    }

    if workflow.IsLegallyRegistered() {
        t.Fatal("platform position must not imply legal registration")
    }
}

function Write-Section {
    param([string]$Message)
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host ("=" * 72) -ForegroundColor DarkCyan
    Write-Host $Message -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Write-Host ("=" * 72) -ForegroundColor DarkCyan
}

function Write-Info {
    param([string]$Message)
    Write-Host "[INFO] $Message" -ForegroundColor Gray
}

function Write-Success {
    param([string]$Message)
    Write-Host "[OK] $Message" -ForegroundColor Green
}

function Write-WarningMessage {
    param([string]$Message)
    Write-Host "[WARNING] $Message" -ForegroundColor Yellow
}

function Stop-Script {
    param([string]$Message)
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "[STOP] $Message" -ForegroundColor Red
    throw $Message
}

function Find-ProjectRoot {
    param([string]$InitialPath)

    if (-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($InitialPath)) {
        $candidate = (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $InitialPath).Path

        if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $candidate "go.mod"))) {
            Stop-Script "go.mod در مسیر تعیین‌شده پیدا نشد: $candidate"
        }

        return $candidate
    }

    $current = (Get-Location).Path

    while (-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($current)) {
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $current "go.mod")) {
            return $current
        }

        $parent = Split-Path -Parent $current

        if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($parent) -or $parent -eq $current) {
            break
        }

        $current = $parent
    }

    Stop-Script "پروژه Go پیدا نشد. اسکریپت را از ریشه پروژه اجرا کنید یا ProjectRoot بدهید."
}

function Resolve-ModuleDirectory {
    param(
        [string]$Root,
        [string[]]$Candidates,
        [string]$ModuleName
    )

    $found = @()

    foreach ($candidate in $Candidates) {
        $fullPath = Join-Path $Root $candidate

        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $fullPath -PathType Container) {
            $found += $fullPath
        }
    }

    if ($found.Count -eq 0) {
        Stop-Script "ماژول '$ModuleName' پیدا نشد. مسیرهای بررسی‌شده: $($Candidates -join ', ')"
    }

    if ($found.Count -gt 1) {
        Stop-Script "برای ماژول '$ModuleName' بیش از یک مسیر معتبر وجود دارد: $($found -join ', '). مسیر باید دستی تعیین تکلیف شود."
    }

    return $found[0]
}

function Write-Utf8NoBom {
    param(
        [string]$Path,
        [string]$Content
    )

    $encoding = New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)
    [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($Path, $Content, $encoding)
}

function Add-NewFile {
    param(
        [string]$Path,
        [string]$Content
    )

    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Path) {
        Stop-Script "فایل مقصد از قبل وجود دارد و برای جلوگیری از بازنویسی، اجرا متوقف شد: $Path"
    }

    if ($WhatIf) {
        Write-Host "[WHATIF] ایجاد فایل: $Path" -ForegroundColor Yellow
        return
    }

    $parent = Split-Path -Parent $Path

    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $parent)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $parent -Force | Out-Null
    }

    Write-Utf8NoBom -Path $Path -Content $Content
    Write-Success "ایجاد شد: $Path"
}
