# Initialize-GeneralMarketImplementation.ps1
# General Market Backend - Phase 1
# Go + PostgreSQL
# Non-destructive: adds a migration and implements public catalogue reads.
# Existing files are backed up before any overwrite.

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
Set-StrictMode -Version Latest

function Write-Step([string]$Message) {
    Write-Host "`n[STEP] $Message" -ForegroundColor Cyan
}

function Write-OK([string]$Message) {
    Write-Host "[OK] $Message" -ForegroundColor Green
}

function Write-Warn([string]$Message) {
    Write-Host "[WARN] $Message" -ForegroundColor Yellow
}

function Write-TextNoBom {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)][string]$Path,
        [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)][string]$Content
    )

    $parent = Split-Path -Parent $Path
    if ($parent -and !(Test-Path -LiteralPath $parent)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $parent -Force | Out-Null
    }

    $utf8NoBom = New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)
    [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($Path, $Content, $utf8NoBom)
}

function Find-BackendRoot {
    $cwd = (Get-Location).Path

    $candidates = @(
        $cwd,
        (Join-Path $cwd "general-market-backend"),
        (Join-Path $cwd "Real-Asset-Management-Platform\general-market-backend"),
        (Join-Path $cwd "Real-Asset-Management-Platform")
    )

    foreach ($candidate in $candidates) {
        if (!(Test-Path -LiteralPath $candidate)) {
            continue
        }

        $full = (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $candidate).Path

        if (
            (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $full "go.mod")) -and
            (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $full "cmd\server\main.go")) -and
            (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $full "migrations"))
        ) {
            return $full
        }

        $nested = Join-Path $full "general-market-backend"
        if (
            (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $nested "go.mod")) -and
            (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $nested "cmd\server\main.go")) -and
            (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $nested "migrations"))
        ) {
            return (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $nested).Path
        }
    }

    throw @"
Backend root was not found.
Run this script from general-market-backend or its parent project directory.
The script requires go.mod, cmd\server\main.go and migrations\.
"@
}

$ProjectRoot = Find-BackendRoot
$MigrationPath = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "migrations\000002_market_domain_foundation.sql"
$RouterPath = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "internal\httpapi\router.go"
$CataloguePath = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "internal\catalogue\handler.go"
$CatalogueTestPath = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "internal\catalogue\handler_test.go"

Write-Host "General Market Backend - Phase 1" -ForegroundColor White
Write-Host "Root: $ProjectRoot" -ForegroundColor Gray

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Preflight checks. Stop before changing anything if a migration name clashes.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

Write-Step "Checking prerequisites"

if (!(Get-Command go -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
    throw "Go was not found in PATH. Install/configure Go before running this script."
}

$goVersion = (& go version 2>&1 | Out-String).Trim()
Write-Host $goVersion

$moduleFile = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "go.mod"
$moduleText = Get-Content -LiteralPath $moduleFile -Raw

if ($moduleText -notmatch "github\.com/jackc/pgx/v5") {
    throw "The expected PostgreSQL driver pgx/v5 is not declared in go.mod. No files were changed."
}

if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $MigrationPath) {
    throw @"
The target migration already exists:
$MigrationPath

No files were changed. Inspect that migration and choose a new version number
rather than overwriting an existing migration.
"@
}

if (!(Test-Path -LiteralPath $RouterPath)) {
    throw "Expected router file was not found: $RouterPath"
}

if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $CataloguePath) {
    throw @"
The target catalogue handler already exists:
$CataloguePath

No files were changed. Review the existing implementation and integrate it
instead of overwriting it.
"@
}

if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $CatalogueTestPath) {
    throw @"
The target catalogue test file already exists:
$CatalogueTestPath

No files were changed. Review the existing test file before continuing.
"@
}

# Confirm the existing migration mechanism is capable of discovering SQL files.
$migrationsGo = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "migrations\migrations.go"
if (!(Test-Path -LiteralPath $migrationsGo)) {
    throw "migrations\migrations.go was not found. No files were changed."
}

$migrationsSource = Get-Content -LiteralPath $migrationsGo -Raw
if ($migrationsSource -notmatch 'go:embed') {
    throw @"
migrations\migrations.go does not appear to embed migration files.
The new migration would not necessarily be applied by the existing migration runner.
No files were changed. Review the migration mechanism first.
"@
}

Write-OK "Prerequisites passed."

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Backup all existing files this script may replace.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

Write-Step "Creating a timestamped backup"

$timestamp = Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd-HHmmss"
$backupRoot = Join-Path $ProjectRoot ".backups\phase1-$timestamp"

New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $backupRoot -Force | Out-Null

$relativeRouter = "internal\httpapi\router.go"
$routerBackup = Join-Path $backupRoot $relativeRouter
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path (Split-Path -Parent $routerBackup) -Force | Out-Null
Copy-Item -LiteralPath $RouterPath -Destination $routerBackup

$gitStatusPath = Join-Path $backupRoot "git-status-before.txt"
$gitDiffPath = Join-Path $backupRoot "git-diff-before.txt"

Push-Location $ProjectRoot
try {
    if (Get-Command git -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue) {
        (& git status --short 2>&1 | Out-String) |
            Set-Content -LiteralPath $gitStatusPath -Encoding UTF8

        (& git diff -- . 2>&1 | Out-String) |
            Set-Content -LiteralPath $gitDiffPath -Encoding UTF8
    }
    else {
        "Git executable not found; source files were still backed up." |
            Set-Content -LiteralPath $gitStatusPath -Encoding UTF8
    }
}
finally {
    Pop-Location
}

Write-OK "Backup created: $backupRoot"

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Additive PostgreSQL migration.
#    This creates foundational tables across the domain.
#    It does not create balances, ownership ledgers, contracts or settlement
#    records owned by independent services.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

Write-Step "Writing the domain foundation migration"

$migration = @'
-- General Market Backend
-- Migration: 000002_market_domain_foundation.sql
-- Additive, versioned and non-destructive.
--
-- Ownership boundaries:
-- * No account credentials or authoritative role database.
-- * No free-balance ledger or authoritative reservation ledger.
-- * No official property ownership register.
-- * No legal contract body owned by Contracts service.
-- * No service-delivery records owned by Services.
-- * No final settlement ledger owned by Settlement.
--
-- External identifiers are stored as references, not copied records.

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS markets (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    code VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
    allowed_trade_methods JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
    display_rules JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_by_user_id VARCHAR(160),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'active', 'paused', 'closed'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public_categories (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    parent_id UUID REFERENCES public_categories(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    code VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    sort_order INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS specialized_classifications (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    code VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    name VARCHAR(240) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    domain_code VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (version > 0)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS category_mappings (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    public_category_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public_categories(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    specialized_classification_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES specialized_classifications(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    market_id UUID REFERENCES markets(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    valid_from TIMESTAMPTZ,
    valid_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    UNIQUE (public_category_id, specialized_classification_id, market_id),
    CHECK (valid_until IS NULL OR valid_from IS NULL OR valid_until > valid_from)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS parameter_definitions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    classification_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES specialized_classifications(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    code VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(240) NOT NULL,
    data_type VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL,
    unit_code VARCHAR(60),
    is_required BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    validation_schema JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    sort_order INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    UNIQUE (classification_id, code, version),
    CHECK (data_type IN ('string', 'integer', 'decimal', 'boolean', 'date', 'datetime', 'enum', 'object', 'array')),
    CHECK (version > 0)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS offer_configurations (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    classification_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES specialized_classifications(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    code VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    name VARCHAR(240) NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
    current_version INTEGER,
    created_by_user_id VARCHAR(160),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'active', 'retired'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS offer_config_versions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    configuration_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES offer_configurations(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    version INTEGER NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
    rules_schema JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    calculation_schema JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    qualification_schema JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    document_schema JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_by_user_id VARCHAR(160),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    published_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    UNIQUE (configuration_id, version),
    CHECK (version > 0),
    CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'approved', 'published', 'retired'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS offer_config_parameters (
    configuration_version_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES offer_config_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    parameter_definition_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES parameter_definitions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    is_required BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    overrides JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    PRIMARY KEY (configuration_version_id, parameter_definition_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS offer_rules (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    configuration_version_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES offer_config_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    code VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
    rule_type VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
    definition JSONB NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    UNIQUE (configuration_version_id, code)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS offer_documents (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    offer_id UUID,
    configuration_version_id UUID REFERENCES offer_config_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    document_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    external_document_ref VARCHAR(240),
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'required',
    metadata JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('required', 'submitted', 'verified', 'rejected', 'expired'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS company_qualifications (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    company_ref VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    classification_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES specialized_classifications(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    external_verification_ref VARCHAR(240),
    valid_from TIMESTAMPTZ,
    valid_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
    metadata JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('pending', 'approved', 'rejected', 'suspended', 'expired'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS offers (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    public_code VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    market_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES markets(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    public_category_id UUID REFERENCES public_categories(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    classification_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES specialized_classifications(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    configuration_version_id UUID REFERENCES offer_config_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    owner_ref VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    title VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    summary TEXT,
    offer_type VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'sale',
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
    price_amount NUMERIC(24,8),
    currency_code VARCHAR(12),
    quantity_total NUMERIC(24,8),
    quantity_unit VARCHAR(60),
    attributes JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    valid_from TIMESTAMPTZ,
    valid_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
    submitted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    published_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    closed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_by_user_id VARCHAR(160),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'pending_review', 'approved', 'published', 'suspended', 'rejected', 'expired', 'closed')),
    CHECK (offer_type IN ('sale', 'purchase', 'service', 'physical_commodity', 'real_asset')),
    CHECK (price_amount IS NULL OR price_amount >= 0),
    CHECK (quantity_total IS NULL OR quantity_total >= 0),
    CHECK (valid_until IS NULL OR valid_from IS NULL OR valid_until > valid_from)
);

ALTER TABLE offer_documents
    ADD CONSTRAINT fk_offer_documents_offer
    FOREIGN KEY (offer_id) REFERENCES offers(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS offer_versions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    offer_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES offers(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    version INTEGER NOT NULL,
    snapshot JSONB NOT NULL,
    changed_by_user_id VARCHAR(160),
    change_reason TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    UNIQUE (offer_id, version),
    CHECK (version > 0)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS offer_approvals (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    offer_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES offers(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    configuration_version_id UUID REFERENCES offer_config_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    reviewer_ref VARCHAR(160),
    decision VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL,
    reason TEXT,
    rule_results JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    decided_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (decision IN ('approved', 'rejected', 'changes_requested'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS supply_requests (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    request_code VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    requester_ref VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    market_id UUID REFERENCES markets(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    classification_id UUID REFERENCES specialized_classifications(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    title VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    quantity NUMERIC(24,8),
    quantity_unit VARCHAR(60),
    target_price NUMERIC(24,8),
    currency_code VARCHAR(12),
    requirements JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
    response_deadline TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published', 'receiving_proposals', 'evaluating', 'awarded', 'cancelled', 'expired')),
    CHECK (quantity IS NULL OR quantity > 0),
    CHECK (target_price IS NULL OR target_price >= 0)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS supply_proposals (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    request_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES supply_requests(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    proposer_ref VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    price_amount NUMERIC(24,8),
    currency_code VARCHAR(12),
    quantity NUMERIC(24,8),
    delivery_terms JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    terms JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'submitted',
    valid_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'submitted', 'under_review', 'accepted', 'rejected', 'withdrawn', 'expired')),
    CHECK (price_amount IS NULL OR price_amount >= 0),
    CHECK (quantity IS NULL OR quantity > 0)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS orders (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    order_code VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    offer_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES offers(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    participant_ref VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    side VARCHAR(8) NOT NULL,
    order_type VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'limit',
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending_checks',
    price_amount NUMERIC(24,8),
    currency_code VARCHAR(12),
    quantity_original NUMERIC(24,8) NOT NULL,
    quantity_remaining NUMERIC(24,8) NOT NULL,
    quantity_filled NUMERIC(24,8) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    external_reservation_ref VARCHAR(240),
    idempotency_key VARCHAR(160),
    correlation_id VARCHAR(160),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    cancelled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    CHECK (side IN ('buy', 'sell')),
    CHECK (order_type IN ('limit', 'market', 'offer_directed')),
    CHECK (status IN ('pending_checks', 'pending_reservation', 'open', 'partially_filled', 'filled', 'cancelled', 'rejected', 'expired', 'failed')),
    CHECK (quantity_original > 0),
    CHECK (quantity_remaining >= 0),
    CHECK (quantity_filled >= 0),
    CHECK (quantity_remaining + quantity_filled = quantity_original),
    CHECK (price_amount IS NULL OR price_amount >= 0),
    UNIQUE (participant_ref, idempotency_key)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS order_matches (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    buy_order_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    sell_order_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    matched_quantity NUMERIC(24,8) NOT NULL,
    matched_price NUMERIC(24,8) NOT NULL,
    currency_code VARCHAR(12),
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'recorded',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (buy_order_id <> sell_order_id),
    CHECK (matched_quantity > 0),
    CHECK (matched_price >= 0),
    CHECK (status IN ('recorded', 'accepted', 'rejected', 'trade_created', 'failed'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS trades (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    trade_code VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    match_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES order_matches(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    offer_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES offers(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    buy_order_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    sell_order_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    buyer_ref VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    seller_ref VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    quantity NUMERIC(24,8) NOT NULL,
    price_amount NUMERIC(24,8) NOT NULL,
    currency_code VARCHAR(12),
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'created',
    execution_policy_version_id UUID,
    correlation_id VARCHAR(160),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (quantity > 0),
    CHECK (price_amount >= 0),
    CHECK (status IN ('created', 'in_execution', 'awaiting_contract', 'awaiting_services', 'awaiting_delivery', 'ready_for_settlement', 'settlement_pending', 'settled', 'cancelled', 'failed'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS execution_policies (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    code VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    name VARCHAR(240) NOT NULL,
    market_id UUID REFERENCES markets(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    offer_type VARCHAR(40),
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'active', 'retired'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS execution_policy_versions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    policy_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES execution_policies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    version INTEGER NOT NULL,
    steps JSONB NOT NULL,
    conditions JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    published_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    UNIQUE (policy_id, version),
    CHECK (version > 0)
);

ALTER TABLE trades
    ADD CONSTRAINT fk_trades_execution_policy_version
    FOREIGN KEY (execution_policy_version_id)
    REFERENCES execution_policy_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS trade_workflows (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    trade_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES trades(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    policy_version_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES execution_policy_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    current_step_code VARCHAR(120),
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    last_error_code VARCHAR(100),
    last_error_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    retry_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    correlation_id VARCHAR(160),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    UNIQUE (trade_id),
    CHECK (status IN ('pending', 'running', 'waiting_external', 'completed', 'failed', 'cancelled')),
    CHECK (retry_count >= 0)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS trade_state_history (
    id BIGINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    trade_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES trades(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    state_domain VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
    previous_state VARCHAR(40),
    new_state VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
    actor_ref VARCHAR(160),
    reason TEXT,
    correlation_id VARCHAR(160),
    metadata JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS integration_operations (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    service_code VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    operation_code VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
    external_ref VARCHAR(240),
    correlation_id VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    idempotency_key VARCHAR(160),
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    request_metadata JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    response_metadata JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    error_code VARCHAR(100),
    attempt_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    next_retry_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('pending', 'running', 'succeeded', 'failed', 'retry_wait', 'cancelled')),
    CHECK (attempt_count >= 0),
    UNIQUE (service_code, operation_code, idempotency_key)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS commodity_aggregation_jobs (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    job_code VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    commodity_classification_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES specialized_classifications(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    requested_quantity NUMERIC(24,8),
    quantity_unit VARCHAR(60),
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
    criteria JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    result_summary JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_by_user_id VARCHAR(160),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'evaluating', 'proposed', 'accepted', 'cancelled', 'failed')),
    CHECK (requested_quantity IS NULL OR requested_quantity > 0)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS commodity_aggregation_items (
    aggregation_job_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES commodity_aggregation_jobs(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    offer_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES offers(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    eligible_quantity NUMERIC(24,8) NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'included',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    PRIMARY KEY (aggregation_job_id, offer_id),
    CHECK (eligible_quantity > 0),
    CHECK (status IN ('included', 'excluded', 'reserved_externally', 'fulfilled'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS real_asset_workflows (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    workflow_code VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    trade_id UUID REFERENCES trades(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    offer_id UUID REFERENCES offers(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    external_asset_ref VARCHAR(240),
    external_registry_ref VARCHAR(240),
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    platform_position_ref VARCHAR(240),
    official_ownership_status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'unknown',
    metadata JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    correlation_id VARCHAR(160),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CHECK (status IN ('pending', 'verification_pending', 'contract_pending', 'transfer_pending', 'completed', 'rejected', 'failed', 'cancelled')),
    CHECK (official_ownership_status IN ('unknown', 'not_applicable', 'pending', 'confirmed', 'rejected'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS institutional_requests (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    institution_code VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    request_type VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
    external_request_ref VARCHAR(240),
    correlation_id VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    legal_basis_ref VARCHAR(240),
    access_scope JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
    response_metadata JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    requested_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    CHECK (status IN ('pending', 'authorized', 'rejected', 'submitted', 'completed', 'failed', 'cancelled'))
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS audit_events (
    id BIGINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    event_code VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
    entity_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    entity_ref VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    actor_ref VARCHAR(160),
    outcome VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
    reason_code VARCHAR(100),
    correlation_id VARCHAR(160),
    rule_version_ref VARCHAR(160),
    metadata JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS idempotency_records (
    id BIGINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    scope_code VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
    idempotency_key VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
    request_hash VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(24) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'in_progress',
    response_status INTEGER,
    response_body JSONB,
    correlation_id VARCHAR(160),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    UNIQUE (scope_code, idempotency_key),
    CHECK (status IN ('in_progress', 'completed', 'failed'))
);

-- Indexes for public catalogue, lifecycle queues, matching and traceability.
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_offers_public_catalogue
    ON offers (market_id, public_category_id, published_at DESC, id DESC)
    WHERE status = 'published';

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_offers_classification_status
    ON offers (classification_id, status, created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_offers_validity
    ON offers (valid_until)
    WHERE status = 'published' AND valid_until IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_category_mappings_public
    ON category_mappings (public_category_id, is_active);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_supply_requests_status_deadline
    ON supply_requests (status, response_deadline);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_supply_proposals_request_status
    ON supply_proposals (request_id, status, created_at);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_orders_offer_status
    ON orders (offer_id, status, created_at);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_orders_order_book
    ON orders (offer_id, side, price_amount, created_at)
    WHERE status IN ('open', 'partially_filled');

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_matches_buy_order
    ON order_matches (buy_order_id, created_at);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_matches_sell_order
    ON order_matches (sell_order_id, created_at);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_trades_status_created
    ON trades (status, created_at);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_trade_workflows_status
    ON trade_workflows (status, updated_at);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_trade_state_history_trade
    ON trade_state_history (trade_id, created_at);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_integration_operations_retry
    ON integration_operations (status, next_retry_at)
    WHERE status IN ('pending', 'retry_wait', 'failed');

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_real_asset_workflows_status
    ON real_asset_workflows (status, updated_at);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_institutional_requests_correlation
    ON institutional_requests (correlation_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_events_entity
    ON audit_events (entity_type, entity_ref, created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_events_correlation
    ON audit_events (correlation_id)
    WHERE correlation_id IS NOT NULL;
'@

Write-TextNoBom -Path $MigrationPath -Content $migration
Write-OK "Added migration: migrations\000002_market_domain_foundation.sql"

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Implement the public catalogue read API.
#    This is intentionally read-only until the account/permission integration
#    contract is implemented. No untrusted identity headers are accepted.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

Write-Step "Writing the catalogue handler"

$catalogueHandler = @'
package catalogue

import (
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"net/http"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"
)

const (
	defaultPageSize = 20
	maxPageSize     = 100
	maxSearchLength = 120
)

type Handler struct {
	DB *sql.DB
}

type OfferSummary struct {
	ID                 string    `json:"id"`
	Code               string    `json:"code"`
	MarketID           string    `json:"market_id"`
	MarketName         string    `json:"market_name"`
	PublicCategoryID   string    `json:"public_category_id,omitempty"`
	PublicCategoryName string    `json:"public_category_name,omitempty"`
	ClassificationID   string    `json:"classification_id"`
	Title              string    `json:"title"`
	Summary            string    `json:"summary,omitempty"`
	OfferType          string    `json:"offer_type"`
	PriceAmount        string    `json:"price_amount,omitempty"`
	CurrencyCode       string    `json:"currency_code,omitempty"`
	QuantityTotal      string    `json:"quantity_total,omitempty"`
	QuantityUnit       string    `json:"quantity_unit,omitempty"`
	Attributes         json.RawMessage `json:"attributes"`
	PublishedAt        time.Time `json:"published_at"`
	ValidUntil         *time.Time `json:"valid_until,omitempty"`
}

type ListResponse struct {
	Data       []OfferSummary `json:"data"`
	Page       int            `json:"page"`
	PageSize   int            `json:"page_size"`
	Total      int64          `json:"total"`
	TotalPages int64          `json:"total_pages"`
}

type APIError struct {
	Code    string `json:"code"`
	Message string `json:"message"`
}

func (h Handler) Register(mux *http.ServeMux) {
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /api/v1/catalogue/offers", h.ListOffers)
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /api/v1/catalogue/offers/{id}", h.GetOffer)
}

func (h Handler) ListOffers(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if h.DB == nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, "database_unavailable", "Catalogue storage is unavailable.")
		return
	}

	page, err := parsePositiveInt(r.URL.Query().Get("page"), 1, 1000000)
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid_page", "page must be a positive integer.")
		return
	}

	pageSize, err := parsePositiveInt(r.URL.Query().Get("page_size"), defaultPageSize, maxPageSize)
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid_page_size", "page_size must be between 1 and 100.")
		return
	}

	search := strings.TrimSpace(r.URL.Query().Get("q"))
	if len(search) > maxSearchLength {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "search_too_long", "q must not exceed 120 characters.")
		return
	}

	categoryID := strings.TrimSpace(r.URL.Query().Get("category_id"))
	marketID := strings.TrimSpace(r.URL.Query().Get("market_id"))

	const filter = `
		FROM offers o
		INNER JOIN markets m ON m.id = o.market_id
		LEFT JOIN public_categories pc ON pc.id = o.public_category_id
		WHERE o.status = 'published'
		  AND m.status = 'active'
		  AND (o.valid_from IS NULL OR o.valid_from <= now())
		  AND (o.valid_until IS NULL OR o.valid_until > now())
		  AND ($1 = '' OR o.title ILIKE '%' || $1 || '%' OR COALESCE(o.summary, '') ILIKE '%' || $1 || '%')
		  AND ($2 = '' OR o.public_category_id::text = $2)
		  AND ($3 = '' OR o.market_id::text = $3)
	`

	var total int64
	countQuery := "SELECT count(*) " + filter
	if err := h.DB.QueryRowContext(r.Context(), countQuery, search, categoryID, marketID).Scan(&total); err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "catalogue_query_failed", "The catalogue could not be loaded.")
		return
	}

	offset := (page - 1) * pageSize
	const listSelect = `
		SELECT
			o.id::text,
			o.public_code,
			o.market_id::text,
			m.name,
			COALESCE(o.public_category_id::text, ''),
			COALESCE(pc.name, ''),
			o.classification_id::text,
			o.title,
			COALESCE(o.summary, ''),
			o.offer_type,
			COALESCE(o.price_amount::text, ''),
			COALESCE(o.currency_code, ''),
			COALESCE(o.quantity_total::text, ''),
			COALESCE(o.quantity_unit, ''),
			o.attributes,
			o.published_at,
			o.valid_until
	`
	rows, err := h.DB.QueryContext(
		r.Context(),
		listSelect+filter+`
			ORDER BY o.published_at DESC, o.id DESC
			LIMIT $4 OFFSET $5
		`,
		search, categoryID, marketID, pageSize, offset,
	)
	if err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "catalogue_query_failed", "The catalogue could not be loaded.")
		return
	}
	defer rows.Close()

	data := make([]OfferSummary, 0, pageSize)
	for rows.Next() {
		var item OfferSummary
		var categoryIDValue string
		var categoryName string
		var validUntil sql.NullTime
		var attributes []byte

		if err := rows.Scan(
			&item.ID,
			&item.Code,
			&item.MarketID,
			&item.MarketName,
			&categoryIDValue,
			&categoryName,
			&item.ClassificationID,
			&item.Title,
			&item.Summary,
			&item.OfferType,
			&item.PriceAmount,
			&item.CurrencyCode,
			&item.QuantityTotal,
			&item.QuantityUnit,
			&attributes,
			&item.PublishedAt,
			&validUntil,
		); err != nil {
			writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "catalogue_scan_failed", "A catalogue record could not be read.")
			return
		}

		item.PublicCategoryID = categoryIDValue
		item.PublicCategoryName = categoryName
		if validUntil.Valid {
			value := validUntil.Time
			item.ValidUntil = &value
		}
		if len(attributes) == 0 {
			item.Attributes = json.RawMessage(`{}`)
		} else if json.Valid(attributes) {
			item.Attributes = json.RawMessage(append([]byte(nil), attributes...))
		} else {
			writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "invalid_catalogue_data", "A catalogue record contains invalid attributes.")
			return
		}
		data = append(data, item)
	}
	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "catalogue_read_failed", "The catalogue could not be read completely.")
		return
	}

	totalPages := int64(0)
	if total > 0 {
		totalPages = (total + int64(pageSize) - 1) / int64(pageSize)
	}

	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, ListResponse{
		Data:       data,
		Page:       page,
		PageSize:   pageSize,
		Total:      total,
		TotalPages: totalPages,
	})
}

func (h Handler) GetOffer(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if h.DB == nil {
		writeError(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, "database_unavailable", "Catalogue storage is unavailable.")
		return
	}

	id := strings.TrimSpace(r.PathValue("id"))
	if id == "" || len(id) > 64 {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "invalid_offer_id", "A valid offer ID is required.")
		return
	}

	const query = `
		SELECT
			o.id::text,
			o.public_code,
			o.market_id::text,
			m.name,
			COALESCE(o.public_category_id::text, ''),
			COALESCE(pc.name, ''),
			o.classification_id::text,
			o.title,
			COALESCE(o.summary, ''),
			o.offer_type,
			COALESCE(o.price_amount::text, ''),
			COALESCE(o.currency_code, ''),
			COALESCE(o.quantity_total::text, ''),
			COALESCE(o.quantity_unit, ''),
			o.attributes,
			o.published_at,
			o.valid_until
		FROM offers o
		INNER JOIN markets m ON m.id = o.market_id
		LEFT JOIN public_categories pc ON pc.id = o.public_category_id
		WHERE o.id::text = $1
		  AND o.status = 'published'
		  AND m.status = 'active'
		  AND (o.valid_from IS NULL OR o.valid_from <= now())
		  AND (o.valid_until IS NULL OR o.valid_until > now())
	`

	var item OfferSummary
	var categoryIDValue string
	var categoryName string
	var validUntil sql.NullTime
	var attributes []byte

	err := h.DB.QueryRowContext(r.Context(), query, id).Scan(
		&item.ID,
		&item.Code,
		&item.MarketID,
		&item.MarketName,
		&categoryIDValue,
		&categoryName,
		&item.ClassificationID,
		&item.Title,
		&item.Summary,
		&item.OfferType,
		&item.PriceAmount,
		&item.CurrencyCode,
		&item.QuantityTotal,
		&item.QuantityUnit,
		&attributes,
		&item.PublishedAt,
		&validUntil,
	)

	if err != nil {
		if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
			writeError(w, http.StatusNotFound, "offer_not_found", "The published offer was not found.")
			return
		}
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "catalogue_query_failed", "The offer could not be loaded.")
		return
	}

	item.PublicCategoryID = categoryIDValue
	item.PublicCategoryName = categoryName
	if validUntil.Valid {
		value := validUntil.Time
		item.ValidUntil = &value
	}
	if len(attributes) == 0 {
		item.Attributes = json.RawMessage(`{}`)
	} else if json.Valid(attributes) {
		item.Attributes = json.RawMessage(append([]byte(nil), attributes...))
	} else {
		writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, "invalid_catalogue_data", "The offer contains invalid attributes.")
		return
	}

	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, item)
}

func parsePositiveInt(raw string, fallback, maximum int) (int, error) {
	if raw == "" {
		return fallback, nil
	}
	value, err := strconv.Atoi(raw)
	if err != nil || value < 1 || value > maximum {
		return 0, errors.New("out of range")
	}
	return value, nil
}

func writeError(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, code, message string) {
	writeJSON(w, status, APIError{Code: code, Message: message})
}

func writeJSON(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, value any) {
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
	w.Header().Set("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
	w.WriteHeader(status)
	_ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(value)
}
'@

Write-TextNoBom -Path $CataloguePath -Content $catalogueHandler
Write-OK "Added internal\catalogue\handler.go"

Write-Step "Writing catalogue tests"

$catalogueTests = @'
package catalogue

import (
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"testing"
)

func TestListOffersRejectsInvalidPage(t *testing.T) {
	handler := Handler{}
	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	handler.Register(mux)

	request := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/catalogue/offers?page=0", nil)
	response := httptest.NewRecorder()
	mux.ServeHTTP(response, request)

	if response.Code != http.StatusBadRequest {
		t.Fatalf("expected status %d, got %d", http.StatusBadRequest, response.Code)
	}
}

func TestListOffersRejectsOversizedPageSize(t *testing.T) {
	handler := Handler{}
	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	handler.Register(mux)

	request := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/catalogue/offers?page_size=101", nil)
	response := httptest.NewRecorder()
	mux.ServeHTTP(response, request)

	if response.Code != http.StatusBadRequest {
		t.Fatalf("expected status %d, got %d", http.StatusBadRequest, response.Code)
	}
}

func TestListOffersRejectsLongSearch(t *testing.T) {
	handler := Handler{}
	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	handler.Register(mux)

	longSearch := make([]byte, 121)
	for i := range longSearch {
		longSearch[i] = 'a'
	}

	request := httptest.NewRequest(
		http.MethodGet,
		"/api/v1/catalogue/offers?q="+string(longSearch),
		nil,
	)
	response := httptest.NewRecorder()
	mux.ServeHTTP(response, request)

	if response.Code != http.StatusBadRequest {
		t.Fatalf("expected status %d, got %d", http.StatusBadRequest, response.Code)
	}
}

func TestListOffersReportsUnavailableDatabase(t *testing.T) {
	handler := Handler{}
	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	handler.Register(mux)

	request := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "/api/v1/catalogue/offers", nil)
	response := httptest.NewRecorder()
	mux.ServeHTTP(response, request)

	if response.Code != http.StatusServiceUnavailable {
		t.Fatalf("expected status %d, got %d", http.StatusServiceUnavailable, response.Code)
	}
}
'@

Write-TextNoBom -Path $CatalogueTestPath -Content $catalogueTests
Write-OK "Added internal\catalogue\handler_test.go"

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Integrate catalogue routes while preserving liveness/readiness endpoints.
#    The original router is already backed up above.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

Write-Step "Integrating the API router"

$router = @'
package httpapi

import (
	"database/sql"
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"github.com/igd-tesa/real-asset-management-platform/general-market-backend/internal/catalogue"
)

func New(db *sql.DB) http.Handler {
	mux := http.NewServeMux()

	mux.HandleFunc("GET /health/live", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		writeHealthJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{
			"status": "alive",
		})
	})

	mux.HandleFunc("GET /health/ready", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		if db == nil {
			writeHealthJSON(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, map[string]string{
				"status": "not_ready",
				"reason": "database_unavailable",
			})
			return
		}

		ctx, cancel := timeouts(r)
		defer cancel()

		if err := db.PingContext(ctx); err != nil {
			writeHealthJSON(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, map[string]string{
				"status": "not_ready",
				"reason": "database_unavailable",
			})
			return
		}

		writeHealthJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]string{
			"status": "ready",
		})
	})

	catalogue.Handler{DB: db}.Register(mux)

	return securityHeaders(mux)
}

func timeouts(r *http.Request) (context.Context, context.CancelFunc) {
	return context.WithTimeout(r.Context(), 2*time.Second)
}

func writeHealthJSON(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, body any) {
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8")
	w.Header().Set("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
	w.WriteHeader(status)
	_ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(body)
}

func securityHeaders(next http.Handler) http.Handler {
	return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		w.Header().Set("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
		w.Header().Set("X-Frame-Options", "DENY")
		w.Header().Set("Referrer-Policy", "no-referrer")
		w.Header().Set("Cache-Control", "no-store")
		next.ServeHTTP(w, r)
	})
}
'@

# Ensure context is imported; keep the generated source explicit.
$router = $router.Replace(
    '"database/sql"',
    '"context"' + "`r`n" + "`t" + '"database/sql"'
)

Write-TextNoBom -Path $RouterPath -Content $router
Write-OK "Updated internal\httpapi\router.go"

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Format source files and run actual checks.
#    No database reset and no automatic server startup.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

Write-Step "Formatting the changed Go files"

Push-Location $ProjectRoot
try {
    & gofmt -w `
        "internal\catalogue\handler.go" `
        "internal\catalogue\handler_test.go" `
        "internal\httpapi\router.go"

    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
        throw "gofmt failed with exit code $LASTEXITCODE"
    }

    Write-OK "gofmt completed."

    Write-Step "Running go test ./..."

    & go test ./...
    $testExitCode = $LASTEXITCODE

    if ($testExitCode -eq 0) {
        Write-OK "go test ./... passed."
    }
    else {
        Write-Warn "go test ./... failed with exit code $testExitCode."
    }

    Write-Step "Running go vet ./..."

    & go vet ./...
    $vetExitCode = $LASTEXITCODE

    if ($vetExitCode -eq 0) {
        Write-OK "go vet ./... passed."
    }
    else {
        Write-Warn "go vet ./... failed with exit code $vetExitCode."
    }

    Write-Step "Building the server"

    & go build ./cmd/server
    $buildExitCode = $LASTEXITCODE

    if ($buildExitCode -eq 0) {
        Write-OK "go build ./cmd/server passed."
        $generatedBinary = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "server.exe"
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $generatedBinary) {
            Remove-Item -LiteralPath $generatedBinary -Force
        }
    }
    else {
        Write-Warn "go build ./cmd/server failed with exit code $buildExitCode."
    }

    $result = @"
General Market Backend - Phase 1
Timestamp: $timestamp
Project root: $ProjectRoot
Backup: $backupRoot

Files added:
- migrations\000002_market_domain_foundation.sql
- internal\catalogue\handler.go
- internal\catalogue\handler_test.go

File updated:
- internal\httpapi\router.go

go test exit code: $testExitCode
go vet exit code: $vetExitCode
go build exit code: $buildExitCode

Important:
- No database was dropped or recreated.
- No migration was executed directly by this script.
- Database migration behavior depends on the existing startup migration runner.
- Public catalogue GET endpoints are implemented.
- Order creation, reservation, matching, trade finalization, and external service calls are not exposed by this phase.
- External identity and permission integration must be implemented before protected write APIs are enabled.
"@

    $reportPath = Join-Path $ProjectRoot "phase1-implementation-report-$timestamp.txt"
    Write-TextNoBom -Path $reportPath -Content $result

    Write-Host "`n================ RESULT ================" -ForegroundColor White
    Write-Host "Root: $ProjectRoot"
    Write-Host "Backup: $backupRoot"
    Write-Host "Report: $reportPath"
    Write-Host "go test exit code: $testExitCode"
    Write-Host "go vet exit code: $vetExitCode"
    Write-Host "go build exit code: $buildExitCode"
    Write-Host "========================================`n"

    if (
        $testExitCode -ne 0 -or
        $vetExitCode -ne 0 -or
        $buildExitCode -ne 0
    ) {
        Write-Warn "At least one verification failed. Do not treat this phase as accepted until the failure is inspected."
    }
}
finally {
    Pop-Location
}
